# WM Keyboard settings audit

Every user-facing setting, screen by screen. For each row: does it need to exist,
who benefits, is the default right for the majority, and should onboarding ask.

## Summary

- **977 settings** audited across 15 domains.
  - keep: 759
  - keep-but-demote: 153
  - merge: 31
  - remove: 16
  - automate: 18
- **22 defaults judged wrong** for the majority.
- **32 genuinely split** preferences.
- **15 worth asking during onboarding.**
- **85 settings that should exist and do not.**
- **142 documentation problems.**

## Act on these first

### Defaults that are wrong for the majority

| Setting | Screen | Now | Should be | Why |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Phone number formats | tools/clipboard | empty set | seed one mask from the SIM/locale dial code on first run | KDoc admits empty is 'right for someone who copies numbers from three countries and wrong for everyone else'. |
| Your languages (per-language row) | languages | Seeded from phone languages (max 4); fallback QWERTY + Avro + Probhat + Jatiya | QWERTY only as the shipped fallback | BuiltInLayouts.defaultEnabledIds:324 ships 3 Bengali layouts. Seeding only runs if the wizard opens on an untouched set, so IME-enabled-from-Android users get them. |
| Lazy pronunciation (懶音) | language/yue | Off | On | The code's own comment: these are "the sound mergers most Hong Kong speakers have, and therefore spell" — off means typing lei gets zero candidates for 你. |
| Bottom padding | layout | 32 dp (Android 15+) / 8 dp | 8 dp on every version | KeyboardScreen.kt:1285 already applies navigationBarsPadding(); the 32 dp stacks on top of the bar inset, so A15+ users lose ~56-80 dp under the keys. |
| Haptic style | keypress | Tap (wizard writes Key) | SYSTEM_TAP — stop the wizard overwriting it | Onboarding seeds HapticPlayer.bestSupportedStyle, which returns only SYSTEM_KEY or HEAVY_CLICK, so the KDoc's measured SYSTEM_TAP default never ships. |
| Space repeat interval | keypress | 50 ms | 100 ms | The KDoc splits the two keys because a runaway spacebar has to be undone, then ships delete's own cadence anyway. |
| Currency keys | keypress | ৳ € £ ¥ ₹ ₿ (stored empty) | device-locale currency first, then the built-in set | A fixed ৳-first order on a 352-language keyboard; the first glyph is also the key's corner hint, so order is visible. |
| Typing rhythm | typing | 0% (off) | 50% if the signal is validated, else delete the feature | A slider shipped at off is a feature nobody discovers. Either enable it at a tuned value or drop it. |
| Adapt to app tone | typing | Off | On | KDoc: ranking only, nothing hidden or blocked. Off by default means the work never reaches a real user. |
| Ctrl + number opens a toolbar tool | typing | On | Off | Its own KDoc admits browsers use exactly these and the keyboard wins in a focused field. The leader path already works. |
| Number keys select suggestions | typing | Alt + number | After the shortcut key (LEADER_DIGIT) | The enum's own KDoc calls ALT_DIGIT opt-in because apps claim modifier+digit, yet it ships as the default. |
| Currency keys | keypress | ৳ € £ ¥ ₹ ₿ | locale currency first, then € £ ¥ ₹ ₿ | Taka first is a Bangladesh default shipped worldwide; the device locale already knows the right glyph. Also the one row here with no reset control. |
| Smart detection of key hits | typing | Off | On | Biases only boundary taps, never deliberate presses. Competing keyboards ship this always on; nobody hunts Typing settings for accuracy. |
| Number row in symbols | layout | On | Off | The symbols layer's own top row is already 1-0 (BuiltInLayouts.kt:797), so On draws digits twice and burns a row of height. |
| Turn off when the keyboard closes | tools/fancy | Off | On | KDoc names the failure itself: fancy text is one nickname or one message, and forgetting the tool restyles the next mail. Default should protect against that. |
| Match capitals | tool/* (36 tools) | off for every tool | on for AI (short, ambiguous keyword) | SmartSuggest's own KDoc argues literal matching so "AI" works and "Ai" doesn't, yet toolKeywordCase ships empty — nothing is case-sensitive. |
| Southern hemisphere | tool/MOON_PHASE | off | derived from saved latitude / device region | weatherLatitude is already stored for Weather and Qibla; its sign answers this. Derive it and keep the toggle as an override. |
| Fahrenheit | tool/WEATHER | off (Celsius) | region-derived (°F in US, LR, MM) | Calendar's alt calendars and weekend are already region-seeded in the same repository; temperature unit is the same class of guess. |
| Play animated image | theme-editor | Off | On when the picked file is animated | Picking a GIF silently shows one frame until a switch further down is found. The decoder already knows the file is animated. |
| Source: Photo collection | photo-rotation | Off | On | A user with photos in the collection but no service key turns rotation on and gets nothing. Their own picks should count first. |
| Expand dictionary shortcuts | privacy | Off | On | Zero cost with no shortcuts defined; AOSP and Gboard expand them by default, so off silently breaks a migrated setup. |
| TalkBack support | accessibility | Labels | Explore | Its own KDoc says Explore "is what TalkBack users expect"; Explore is inert unless touch exploration runs (KeyboardScreen.kt:10058). |

### Settings to remove, merge, automate or demote

| Setting | Screen | Verdict | Who benefits | Why |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Key hint font size | appearance | demote | users who read long-press hints and find 10sp too small | KDoc: 1.0 keeps the 10sp base. Not gated on longPressHints, so it shows when hints are off. |
| Reverse order for RTL languages | appearance | demote | few; an RTL typist with muscle memory for fixed tool spots | Does nothing unless an RTL layout is enabled, yet the row is drawn for everyone. |
| Spread tools across the bar | appearance | merge | users who pin two or three tools and want them even | Interacts silently with Scroll the toolbar; one 3-way Spread/Pack/Scroll choice is honest. |
| Scroll the toolbar | appearance | merge | users who pin more tools than fit at a tappable size | KDoc: forces the packed layout, overriding Spread. Merge both into one layout choice. |
| Label text size | appearance | REMOVE | nobody; 7-14sp micro-tuning under an off-by-default toggle | KDoc only justifies 10 over 9. Derive it from toolbar height instead of exposing 8 steps. |
| Tool background shape | appearance | demote | theme tinkerers building a matched look | 12 shapes for a 38dp chip. Already hidden while the radius is 0; a theme can override it. |
| Tool width | appearance | demote | users building a pill-style Gboard strip | KDoc: 38 is the classic circle; wider only reads as a pill with a low radius plus scrolling. |
| Pills per row | appearance | demote | pill users on wide or folded screens | 1-3, only under the Pills layout. Two levels deep for a layout most never pick. |
| Fill pills with the tool color | appearance | demote | people who like a colourful panel | KDoc: dead unless coloredToolIcons is on, but the UI doesn't gate on it, only on Pills. |
| Tools per page | appearance | AUTOMATE | nobody; a page should hold what fits the panel | 4-40, three levels deep behind an off-by-default toggle. Compute from panel height and columns. |
| Theme gallery style (Auto / Grouped / Flat) | themes | demote | few; Auto already picks from the persona quiz | Settings-app cosmetics only; AUTO resolves from PersonaDepth, so most users never need the row. |
| Suggest contact emails | typing | merge | people who type addresses into web forms | Same permission, same source, same disclosure as contact names. One 'use my contacts' toggle plus the email sub-row would do. |
| Contact emails in email fields too | typing | AUTOMATE | nobody obvious — the parent toggle already says yes | KDoc: email fields keep no composing buffer, so this is the one place the feature matters. Turning it off guts the parent. |
| Suggest app names | typing | demote | users who search or reference apps from a text field | No permission, ranked below dictionary words, so off costs nothing. Narrow enough to belong under an advanced group. |
| From URL | customdictionaries | demote | users pasting a link instead of downloading then picking a file | Saves two steps, http/https only. Duplicate of Import for most people; fine as the secondary button it already is. |
| Add words to the system dictionary | privacy | demote | users who also run another keyboard or the system spell checker | KDoc: 'the on-device lexicon already covers this keyboard'. Exports words outside the app, so off is right. |
| Expand dictionary shortcuts | privacy | demote | users migrating with omw-style entries already in Android's list | Largely superseded by snippets; a migration convenience rather than a feature, and it reads a system provider. |
| Show app name first | languages | REMOVE | nobody | Cosmetic label word-order, nested behind an off-by-default toggle, and its own KDoc admits it cannot change which part Android bolds. |
| Select languages in the system settings | languages | demote | Android 13-and-older user whose switcher shows only one language | On API 34+ the app enables subtypes itself; its own subtitle says "use it only if the switcher looks wrong". Hide below-34 only. |
| Traditional characters | language/zh\|ja\|yue | demote | Taiwan/Hong Kong typist | Simplified is the larger population. Renders on the Japanese screen where JapaneseComposer never applies it — gate to zh/yue. |
| Region (Standard / Taiwan / Hong Kong) | language/zh\|ja\|yue | demote | Taiwan typist who wants 計程車, not 出租車 | Character mapping alone does not reach regional vocabulary. Same inert-on-Japanese problem as the toggle above it. |
| Characters that go back | layout | demote | user who also wants ) or " to spring back | Only reachable with the toggle on. Default is the sentence-ending set; digits deliberately excluded so "12" stays put. |
| Side padding | layout | merge | nobody; identical maths to Keyboard width at Centre | dockedWidthArrangement folds it into the same widthFraction as keyboardWidthPercent; with position Left/Right all slack goes to one side, so it is not the symmetric padding its KDoc promises. |
| Keyboard scale (per screen) | layout | merge | foldable owner scaling both heights in one drag | Redundant with the Key height + Number row height sliders right below it. Scales neither width nor padding despite the "whole keyboard" label, and the variant summary row ignores it. |
| One-handed mode | layout | merge | thumb-typist on a big phone | This is live render state, not a preference; its Left/Right duplicates the per-orientation Side rows below and the two can disagree. Should be on/off, with Side deciding the edge. |
| Portrait height | layout | demote | short-thumbed user pulling the top rows down | 100% is a no-op default, yet the row is drawn even while One-handed is Off, unlike Split gap and Floating width which are gated. |
| Landscape height | layout | demote | short-thumbed user in landscape | No-op default and always visible; six one-handed rows show for a mode that ships Off. |
| Comma key opens emoji | layout | demote | user who wants the globe key back and emoji on the comma | With globeAsEmoji on by default, turning this on yields two emoji keys and no comma key. Only coherent paired with globe off. |
| Swap the comma and 🌐 keys | layout | demote | migrant from another keyboard who wants the original order | Pure arrangement: on puts whichever key became emoji in the outer slot and keeps the comma beside the spacebar. |
| Press and hold release haptics | keypress | demote | very few; people wanting a full press-hold-release loop | A third vibration in one gesture. Off is right and almost nobody turns it on; belongs behind an advanced disclosure. |
| Vibrate on space | keypress | demote | thumb typists bothered by the big key buzzing | Narrowest of the four per-event gates. Real but rare taste; fold the four into one 'Vibrate on…' sub-row. |
| Minimum popup duration | keypress | demote | fast typists who want the bubble to linger | A comfort floor almost nobody hunts for. Keep it, but below a 'more' disclosure with the other popup timing. |
| Maximum popup duration | keypress | REMOVE | nobody | KDoc calls it a stuck-bubble backstop, not a comfort knob, and the info text says never lower it. Make it a constant. |
| Popup font size | keypress | demote | low-vision users wanting a bigger preview glyph | Cosmetic tuning on top of a bubble that already scales with height; belongs with the other popup appearance knobs. |
| Popup height | keypress | demote | users whose fingertip covers a short bubble | One slider writes whichever style is active, so each keeps its own tuned value; a theme can override the height outright. |
| Popup shape | keypress | demote | theme tinkerers matching popups to key shape | Governs every popup surface, not just the bubble, and a theme override wins over it. Pure appearance. |
| Popup corner radius | keypress | merge | theme tinkerers, and only on rounded or cut shapes | Inert for four of the six shapes. Belongs inside the shape picker, which is already handed radiusDp for its preview. |
| Ctrl shortcuts as raw key events | keypress | demote | Termux users with a Ctrl key on a custom layout | Only bites on a custom layout carrying Ctrl. EditorInfo cannot spot a terminal, so it stays a switch — but a rare one. |
| Press and hold A to select all | keypress | merge | power users wanting clipboard actions without a Ctrl key | Six near-identical rows; make one multi-select. Bound to the literal letter 'a', so it does nothing on non-Latin layouts. |
| Press and hold C to copy | keypress | merge | power users copying a whole field in one hold | Same row family as Hold A. Replaces C's accent popup outright, which is the trade the info text names. |
| Press and hold X to cut | keypress | merge | power users cutting a field in one hold | Same row family; falls back to select-all when nothing is selected, like Hold C. |
| Press and hold V to paste | keypress | merge | power users pasting without opening the clipboard tool | Same row family. Most useful of the six, since the clipboard tool is otherwise a toolbar trip. |
| Press and hold Z to undo | keypress | merge | power users undoing without the toolbar Undo tool | Same row family; sends the same shortcut the Undo tool does, so it duplicates an existing surface. |
| Press and hold Y to redo | keypress | merge | power users redoing without the toolbar Redo tool | Same row family, and the rarest of the six. Y carries no accents on most layouts, so the trade is cheap. |
| Autocorrect confidence | typing | demote | users burned by wrong corrections who won't disable autocorrect wholesale | KDoc: mirrors SuggestionEngine.DEFAULT_AUTOCORRECT_CONFIDENCE=4.0. Adaptive confidence already moves it automatically, so few need the raw dial. |
| Adaptive confidence | typing | AUTOMATE | nobody — nobody wants corrections to keep firing after they undo them | KDoc describes a pure self-correcting loop anchored to the slider. No user story for off; drop the toggle. |
| Typing rhythm | typing | REMOVE | nobody — ships at 0, so the signal never runs for any user | A slider shipped at off is a feature nobody discovers. Either enable it at a tuned value or drop it. |
| Skip words in capital letters | typing | demote | habitual ALL-CAPS typists who still want their typos fixed | KDoc: acronyms and shouting are deliberate. The off case is real but rare; belongs below the fold. |
| Context-aware suggestions | typing | AUTOMATE | nobody — nobody asks for worse-ranked suggestions | Info text: reorders only on real evidence and never changes autocorrect. Pure on-device win with no off story. |
| Add missing spaces | typing | demote | someone typing compound IDs who hates surprise splits | Heavily guarded (both halves must be words, revertible with backspace). Narrow off case for a top-level row. |
| Detection strength | typing | demote | heavy code-switchers who find Balanced too timid or too pushy | Three calibrated points; already nested under the detection toggle. Correctly advanced. |
| Fix number row slips | typing | AUTOMATE | nobody — the single-digit-only rule already protects room3 | KDoc: only same-length single-digit swaps are trusted, and it is already gated on the number row. |
| Adapt to app tone | typing | AUTOMATE | nobody finds it; ranking-only, buried among 21 corrections rows | KDoc: ranking only, nothing hidden or blocked. Off by default means the work never reaches a real user. |
| Double space for a full stop | typing | merge | everyone ending sentences without a symbols-layer trip | Tab wins over period when both are on — that is a three-way radio wearing two switches. Merge into one choice. |
| Double space for a tab | typing | merge | form-fillers and people indenting notes | Occupies the same double-space slot as the period rule; should be one 'Double space types...' choice. |
| Contact emails in email fields too | typing | REMOVE | nobody — a third-level knob under an off-by-default toggle | Email fields are the main reason to enable email completion at all; hard-code it on. |
| Suggest app names | typing | demote | people who name apps in messages ('use Signal') | x400 source weight pollutes the strip, so off is right — but the payoff is too thin for a top-level row. |
| Only in number fields | typing | demote | users of apps that put the code box behind a plain text input | Real but narrow; the failure it fixes (no chip appears) is hard to connect to this row. |
| Offer a code for | typing | demote | people on carriers where the SMS lands minutes late | KDoc: a chip showing last hour's code is worse than no chip. Few users will ever tune this. |
| Type codes one character at a time | typing | AUTOMATE | nobody — the KDoc says it is indistinguishable in an ordinary single field | Strictly better everywhere per its own KDoc: whole-commit drops all but the first character in split code boxes. |
| Glide across the spacebar | typing | demote | people whose glides overshoot the spacebar and split words | Only shown in glide-word mode. Real but advanced; belongs under the glide sub-group. |
| Swipe start distance | typing | demote | shaky hands (raise it) and light swipers (lower it) | Multiple of system touch slop. Genuine accessibility value but pure advanced tuning. |
| Delay after typing | typing | demote | fast tap-typists whose finger slides off a key mid-burst | The main defence against accidental glides; 0 disables it. Advanced, but it is the fix people search for. |
| Extra time for a dot or a cross | typing | demote | handwriters dotting an i or crossing a t as a second stroke | Only visible in handwrite mode on full builds — already about as demoted as a row can be. |
| Trail width | typing | merge | nobody specifically; three sliders for one cosmetic trail | Width, length and opacity should be one trail-appearance control; the colour already comes from the theme. |
| Trail length | typing | merge | nobody specifically; garnish on a gesture already finished | Same cosmetic knob family as trail width and opacity. Three sliders is two too many. |
| Arrows on the spacebar | typing | demote | people who find the arrows visual clutter | Only shown when a swipe slot is set to Language and more than one mode is enabled. Pure cosmetics. |
| Spacebar text | typing | demote | people who want a custom or blank spacebar label | Overlaps the row above: a custom label overrides the display choice entirely. Supports %s for the live name. |
| Release the keys while audio plays | typing | demote | people who type with music on and still want cursor keys | A real trade for constant listeners, but it is a sub-toggle of an already off-by-default feature. |
| Write the modifier names | typing | demote | Mac users who read the caret and shift glyphs fluently | Badge typography only. On is right for PC keycaps; the off case is people wanting narrower badges. |
| Time to press a key | typing | demote | beginners who need longer to read a bar full of badges | KDoc: this is really how long the badges stay up; three seconds was too short to read new ones. |
| Arrow keys move a highlight | typing | AUTOMATE | nobody — its own KDoc says without it a shortcut can open a tool but not use one | Turning it off breaks the feature it belongs to. Fold it into Tool shortcuts. |
| Esc closes the tool | typing | AUTOMATE | nobody — Esc is only consumed when a panel is actually open | KDoc: a bare Escape still belongs to the app, so the vim/browser conflict this guards against cannot occur. |
| Show the suggestion numbers | typing | demote | people who find permanent digit badges noisy | Only shown in Alt+digit mode. KDoc: the strip is what a hardware user looks at, so the ink is worth it. |
| Show the keyboard for shortcuts | typing | demote | laptop-style users who never want the on-screen board back | State is restored when the tool closes, so the cost of on is temporary. Niche off case. |
| Add tablet keys automatically | keymap_edit | demote | author of a grid already laid out wide by hand | Must default On: a file written before the field existed decodes without it, so any other default silently opts out all shipped layouts. |
| Icon hint | keymap_edit (key sheet) | demote | a handful of authors icon-marking the corner hint | Rarest field in the sheet; sits between Icon and Alternates and costs everyone a row. Fold under an "advanced" disclosure. |
| Characters that go back | layout | demote | the few who want a slash or dash to bounce back too | Digits deliberately excluded — typing "12" is exactly when you want to stay. Only shown when the toggle is on; no reset control. |
| Style to start with (Fancy tool) | tools/fancy | demote | someone who always types the same fancy style | "Follow the strip" is the null pick and is one press away, which is why the row deliberately has no reset control. |
| Keep the language after you turn it off | tools/fancy | demote | heavy fancy-text users wanting it one 🌐 swipe away | Off keeps the globe cycling the languages you actually read. Very small audience; a third fancy toggle is a lot of surface. |
| Reverse the order for right-to-left languages | appearance/toolbar | AUTOMATE | nobody — the layout's script direction is already known at draw time | The app knows the script. An override exists for muscle memory nobody has reported; drop the row, keep the behaviour. |
| Label text size | appearance/toolbar | demote | low-vision users who turned labels on | Only appears with labels on; a 7sp floor is unreadable and 14sp will not fit a 44dp bar. Narrow real range. |
| Tool background shape | appearance/toolbar | demote | theme tinkerers matching the tool background to their key shape | Cosmetic-only and already gated behind radius > 0. Shares the key-shape picker, so it costs nothing to keep. |
| Tool background width | appearance/toolbar | demote | people building a pill-shaped Gboard-style strip | KDoc: 38 is the classic circle, wider stretches to a pill. Range floor equals the default, so it only widens. |
| Pills per row | appearance/toolbar | demote | pills users in landscape (3) or with long tool names (1) | Only shown in Pills layout, which is itself opt-in. Doubly nested, so cheap to keep. |
| Fill the pills with the tool color | appearance/toolbar | demote | pills users who want colour blocks instead of tinted glyphs | KDoc: only has effect while coloredToolIcons is on. Twice-nested cosmetic switch; off keeps the panel calm. |
| Tools per page | appearance/toolbar | demote | paging users matching page size to their row size | KDoc: ceiling is past what fits any phone because a tall page just scrolls. Range is honest but the top half is useless. |
| Gradient tool colors | tools | demote | theme tinkerers who want two-tone glyphs | Purely decorative and doubles the per-tool colour rows from one to two. Correctly nested under coloredToolIcons. |
| Icon colour / Icon colour (start) | tool/* | demote | colour-coders who want their pinned tools to match | Per-tool cosmetic override, gated on coloredToolIcons and resettable from inside its own picker dialog. |
| Icon colour (end) | tool/* | demote | gradient users pinning the far end of one tool's gradient | Only shown with gradients on. Default tracks the near end so the pair moves together until pinned — good design. |
| Open screens inside apps | tool/APP_LAUNCHER | demote | power users jumping straight to a deep screen in an app | Long-press only, so it costs nothing when unused. Off exists mainly to stop accidental long-press surprises. |
| Show private screens | tool/APP_LAUNCHER | demote | Android developers poking at another app's activity list | KDoc: they fail with SecurityException when tapped, so they are debugging fare. Correctly off and correctly hidden by default. |
| Pop-up when you copy | tool/CLIPBOARD | merge | users of fields that give no visual copy confirmation | It is a feedback setting about the keyboard's own copy shortcuts, not clipboard history — the docs already apologise for it sitting here. |
| Pinned entries last | tool/CLIPBOARD | demote | users with many pins who mostly want the fresh copy | Pure ordering preference with no downside either way; matters only once you have several pins. |
| Show the source app | tool/CLIPBOARD | demote | few — people who copy the same text from several apps | KDoc: needs Usage Access and is a best-effort guess of the foreground app. Heavy grant for one line in a long-press popup. |
| Degree readout | tool/COMPASS | REMOVE | nobody — a compass without a bearing is strictly worse | No KDoc justification. The numeric bearing is the reason to open a compass rather than look at the sky. |
| Angle readout | tool/LEVEL | REMOVE | nobody — a bubble level without degrees is a decoration | Same shape as the compass degree toggle: no KDoc reason, and hiding the number removes the tool's only output. |
| Southern hemisphere | tool/MOON_PHASE | AUTOMATE | southern-hemisphere users — but they should never have to say so | weatherLatitude is already stored for Weather and Qibla; its sign answers this. Derive it and keep the toggle as an override. |
| Fahrenheit | tool/WEATHER | AUTOMATE | US/Liberia/Myanmar users — who currently get Celsius silently | Calendar's alt calendars and weekend are already region-seeded in the same repository; temperature unit is the same class of guess. |
| Send the whole photo | tool/CAMERA | demote | users who keep losing the top of a document to the viewfinder crop | KDoc: off keeps what-you-see-is-what-you-send, which is the safer promise. On recovers the 4:3 slivers. |
| Haptics | tool/CAMERA | merge | few — people who want camera buzz separate from keyboard buzz | Duplicates the global Sound & haptics preference for one panel. A user who turned keyboard haptics off almost certainly wants these off too. |
| Key repeat interval | tool/TEXT_EDIT | demote | users who overshoot the cursor on a held arrow key | 60ms is a standard repeat rate. Real but rarely-touched accessibility knob; nothing else in the app exposes it. |
| Pause learning | tool/INCOGNITO | demote | few — people who want incognito for clipboard only | Not learning is most of what incognito means; turning it off leaves a mode that barely does anything. Keep, but it is near-dead weight. |
| Pause clipboard capture | tool/INCOGNITO | demote | few — people who want incognito for learning only | Same shape as Pause learning. Together the two split one coherent mode into a matrix most users will never reason about. |
| Row order (Emoji / Toolbar / Symbol) | rows | demote | the few running emoji row and symbol row at once | emojiBarMode and symbolRowEnabled both default off, so this group reorders one row. Hide it until an optional row is on. |
| Drags edit the active mode | modes | REMOVE | nobody | Off makes drags write globally while the mode's list still wins on screen — the drag looks like it did nothing. Its own info text says so. |
| Override with last used | emoji | demote | user whose global tone is overridden by a stray per-emoji pick | KDoc: a per-emoji tone is a more specific choice. Off exists only to un-stick per-emoji memory; very few need it. |
| Clear recents button | emoji | merge | nobody wants the toggle; some want the button | A preference over whether a button exists. Clear-all is otherwise unreachable; fold it into the history-cell long-press menu that already offers Remove from recents. |
| Emoji descriptions | emoji | demote | users telling near-identical faces apart; low-vision users | Adds the Unicode name to the long-press popup. Nobody plausibly turns it off; the row is close to dead weight. |
| Scroll the emoji row | emoji | demote | users who want more than barCount emoji reachable | KDoc: off so a sideways swipe can't slide the row out from under a tap. Third-level gated row. |
| Import / Replace emoji font file | emoji | demote | users with a font file not in the addon repo | Legacy single-slot import beside the multi-font library. Duplicates what Installed does, but is the only route for an off-repo file. |
| Emoji button in toolbar (tool page copy) | tool/emoji | REMOVE | nobody; identical row two screens away | Exact duplicate of the Emoji-screen row (MainActivity.kt:9063 vs 5814). The All emoji settings nav below it already covers this. |
| Send GIFs as | tool/gif | demote | users sending WebP-backed results into WhatsApp | Sticker mode only ever applies to WebP results; an animated GIF can never take it, so the choice is inert for most picks. |
| Multiple sources | tool/gif, tool/sticker | demote | users with both Klipy and GIPHY keys configured | Does nothing at all with one key, which is the common case. No reset control: it is a bare ChoiceControl, not ChoiceSetting. |
| Results for each search | tool/gif, tool/sticker | demote | metered-data users; power users wanting a deeper grid | The panel does not paginate, so this is the only way past 24. Real but niche; docs currently deny it exists. |
| Icon colour (top left) | tool/* (shared header) | demote | people who colour-code their toolbox | Only drawn when the global colourful-icons switch is on; a per-tool override of a per-tool accent is deep customisation. |
| Icon colour, bottom right | tool/* (shared header) | demote | gradient tinkerers only | Second row appears only with tool icon gradients on. Default tracks the near end, so the pair moves together until pinned. |
| Match capitals | tool/* (shared header) | demote | someone whose custom trigger is an acronym | Shipped keywords are ordinary lowercase words, so this row is inert for almost everyone; it earns its place only after a custom trigger. |
| Key vibration | tool/power-saving | merge | nobody picks this individually; the group as a whole helps | Twelve individual drop switches is the definition of settings bloat. Collapse to Light/Balanced/Aggressive with an advanced expander. |
| Key sounds | tool/power-saving | merge | nobody individually | Same as key vibration: belongs in a preset, not its own row. |
| Animations | tool/power-saving | merge | nobody individually | Reuses the reduce-motion flag, so it can surprise someone who never touched accessibility; still preset material. |
| Glide trail | tool/power-saving | merge | nobody individually | Cheap half of the glide trade-off; drops the overlay while decoding keeps working. Preset material. |
| Key popup | tool/power-saving | REMOVE | nobody | KDoc admits the popup is cheap to draw, so the row ships off and saves nothing measurable when turned on. |
| Glide typing | tool/power-saving | demote | someone squeezing the last hour out of a phone | The one genuine trade-off here: swipe decoding is the most expensive thing the keyboard does, but it is why many installed it. |
| Emoji suggestions | tool/power-saving | merge | nobody individually | Preset material; nobody arrives at this screen wanting emoji prediction back but haptics gone. |
| Smart chips | tool/power-saving | merge | nobody individually | Per-word scanning for sums and keywords is real cost, but the choice belongs in a preset. |
| Background network | tool/power-saving | demote | metered-data users, not only battery savers | The only drop switch with a second reason to exist (data, not power); it silently overrides three other settings elsewhere. |
| Screenshot watching | tool/power-saving | merge | nobody individually | Only matters to the minority who turned screenshot capture on at all; preset material. |
| On-device models | tool/power-saving | merge | Whisper and handwriting users on low battery | Heavy CPU inference is worth dropping, but a Whisper user who wants it kept would rather flip one preset. |
| Typing statistics | tool/power-saving | REMOVE | nobody | Its own KDoc calls the saving "unmeasured and speculative" — a few integer bumps per keystroke. A row that exists to admit it does nothing. |
| Keep the language after you turn it off | tool/fancy | demote | heavy fancy-text users who dislike the layout snapping back | Niche behaviour of a novelty tool; off matches the least surprising "put my keyboard back" expectation. |
| Turn off when the keyboard closes | tool/fancy | demote | people who forget fancy text is still on | Arguably should default on — silently typing ⓕⓐⓝⓒⓨ into a work email is a worse failure than an extra tap. |
| Automatic space | tool/handwriting | demote | handwriters who write CJK or want no gaps | Writing words one after another almost always wants spaces; the toggle exists for scripts that do not. |
| Full-screen picker | tool/gif, tool/sticker | demote | people who want the toolbar kept in reach | Panel pays for the toolbar row with its own back header, so on gives strictly more grid. Off is the minority taste. |
| Multiple sources | tool/gif, tool/sticker | demote | people with two provider keys configured | Inert unless two keys exist. Drawn as a bare ChoiceControl, so unlike the rows around it it has no reset affordance. |
| Results for each search (GIF) | tool/gif, tool/sticker | demote | metered-data users who want fewer thumbnails | Page size, not quality. Almost nobody tunes it; the docs even claim it is not adjustable, which is how invisible it is. |
| Results for each search | tool/web-search, tool/image-search | demote | almost nobody; one API request either way | The screen itself admits each search costs one request regardless, so the slider changes scroll length and nothing else. |
| Vibrate on detection | tool/qr-scan | demote | anyone scanning without watching the screen | Cheap confirmation that a code was caught; only silent-phone users turn it off. |
| Translate to English | tool/voice | demote | bilingual users dictating a foreign language into English | Only shown with Whisper selected; a surprising mode to have on by accident, so off is right. |
| English dialect | tool/grammar | AUTOMATE | British/Indian/Australian spellers, who are currently corrected wrongly | Weekend and alternate calendars already derive from region here; dialect should too instead of assuming American. |
| Re-check delay | tool/grammar | demote | almost nobody; a latency knob with no visible symptom | Users cannot judge 350 versus 500 ms without a stopwatch. Fine to keep, wrong to show above the dialect row's fold. |
| Underline only, no fix popup | tool/grammar | demote | people who want squiggles without the system popup stealing taps | Android 12+ only and only while Harper is the system checker — two gates deep for a cosmetic difference. |
| Markdown links | tool/wikipedia | demote | people writing notes in Markdown apps | A bare URL pastes correctly everywhere; Markdown only helps in Markdown-aware fields, so off is right. |
| Result precision | tool/calculator | demote | people who want money-style two-decimal answers | 8 places is more than a phone calculator ever needs, but it is harmless; the row is tuning, not a decision. |
| Decimal places | tool/currency | demote | users of zero-decimal currencies (JPY, KRW, IDR) | 2 is right for most currencies; could be derived from the target currency's own minor-unit count instead of asked. |
| Refresh the rates every | tool/currency | AUTOMATE | nobody; upstream rates change about once a day | The docs concede anything under 24 h mostly changes how soon a failed fetch retries — that is a retry policy, not a preference. |
| Currency rates from | tool/currency | demote | people whose currency one provider does not quote | ER-API covers ~160 currencies against Frankfurter's ~30, so the default is the broad one; the choice is a fallback for outages. |
| Try the other sources (fiat) | tool/currency | REMOVE | nobody — nobody wants a failed conversion on purpose | Turning fallbacks off can only make the tool fail more often. Clever data model (head plus tail), but no user need. |
| Coin decimal places | tool/currency | demote | nobody much; Auto already keeps five meaningful digits | Auto exists precisely because a fixed decimal count is wrong across BTC and SHIB, so the manual values are traps. |
| Refresh coin prices every | tool/currency | demote | active traders wanting fresher quotes | Unlike the fiat cache this one is defensible — coin prices genuinely move by the minute — but 5 suits nearly everyone. |
| Coin prices from | tool/currency | demote | people whose altcoin only one source quotes | Matters only alongside the Add-a-ticker feature, where coverage really does differ between providers. |
| Try the other sources (crypto) | tool/currency | REMOVE | nobody | Same as the fiat fallback switch: no user benefits from fewer fallbacks. |
| Image size | tool/qr-gen | demote | someone printing a generated code | Affects the sent PNG only; the on-panel preview is a fixed size, so the slider has no visible effect while dragging. |
| Send as (QR) | tool/qr-gen | REMOVE | nobody | Sticker mode needs a WebP source and a generated QR is always PNG, so both options send an image. Documented as having no effect. |
| Error correction | tool/qr-gen | demote | people printing codes onto imperfect surfaces | L/M/Q/H are the standard's own letters. Drawn as a bare ChoiceControl, so it has no reset control unlike neighbouring rows. |
| Capitalize the words | tool/password | demote | people meeting a mixed-case requirement | Adds almost no entropy; exists to satisfy site rules, so off is right and the row is bottom-of-screen material. |
| Include a digit | tool/password | demote | same rule-satisfying user as capitalize | Appends one digit to one word. Pure compliance knob, not a security choice. |
| Default test | tool/typing-test | demote | people who always run the same test shape | This is remembered state as much as a setting — the panel's own chip row writes it, so the screen mostly mirrors it. |
| Length (seconds) | tool/typing-test | demote | people who want a 60-second run | Bests are keyed per config, so a free slider lets you mint a 103-second record nobody can compare; the code's own preset list is 15/30/60/120. |
| Length (words) | tool/typing-test | demote | same as the seconds slider | Same preset-versus-slider mismatch: TypingTestWordCounts lists 10/25/50/100, the settings row offers every value between. |
| App names | tool/app-launcher | demote | people who recognise icons and want more per row | Labels are what make search results legible; off is a density preference for a small minority. |
| Open screens inside apps | tool/app-launcher | demote | people who prefer long-press to mean pin | Really a gesture remap: with it off, holding an app pins it instead. Worth keeping, worth naming better. |
| Show private screens | tool/app-launcher | demote | developers poking at other apps | Its own KDoc calls them debugging fare — tapping one throws SecurityException. Correctly off, but it is a developer row in a user screen. |
| Send the whole photo | tool/camera | demote | people who want the strips the panel cropped away | What-you-see-is-what-you-get is the less surprising default; this is the one case where the photo holds more than you framed. |
| Shutter sound | tool/camera | demote | people photographing quietly | On matches every camera app. Worth noting the toggle cannot win where the OS enforces a shutter sound by law. |
| Haptics (camera) | tool/camera | demote | people who keep vibration off everywhere | Panel-local feedback only; the Send vibration is the ordinary key haptic set under Typing, which the row does not say. |
| Suggest copied codes in code boxes | tool/clipboard | demote | anyone pasting an OTP copied by hand | Well-argued exception to the hide-secrets rule — in a code box the code is the only thing being typed. Very few will change it. |
| Pop-up when you copy | tool/clipboard | demote | people in apps that give no copy feedback of their own | Android already toasts on copy on many versions, so on would double up. Lives on the clipboard page but writes a feedback field. |
| Maximum entries | tool/clipboard | demote | heavy copiers who scroll far back | The other half of the expiry bound: a busy day can pile up hundreds inside the window, and that is a haystack, not history. |
| Bottom control row | tool/clipboard | demote | people who paste then keep typing without leaving the panel | The emoji panel ships this row on; the clipboard panel ships it off. Same ergonomics, opposite default — one of the two is wrong. |
| Full-screen clipboard panel | tool/clipboard | demote | people who want the toolbar in reach | The panel pays for the toolbar row with its own back header, so on strictly buys more cards on screen. |
| Pinned entries last | tool/clipboard | demote | people with many pins who mostly want recents | Pins-first matches why you pinned; the inversion only helps once the pin list is long. |
| Search bar | tool/clipboard | demote | people with a long history to sift | Off by default even though max entries defaults to 100 — the exact size where search starts paying for its row. |
| Show the source app | tool/clipboard | demote | people who copy from many apps and lose track | Costs the Usage Access special permission for a best-effort guess shown only in a long-press popup. Correctly off. |
| Forget sensitive clips after | tool/clipboard | demote | people who want a tighter or looser leash on secrets | Only shown for Short-lived. Deliberately never capped by the ordinary expiry, which is the subtle part users will not notice. |
| Offer a code for | typing | demote | people slow to switch apps for a code | KDoc has it right: a chip showing last hour's code is worse than no chip. Three minutes needs almost no tuning. |
| Clear the notification | typing | demote | people who hate a shade full of spent codes | A keyboard dismissing another app's notification is a surprising side effect, so opt-in is right. |
| Type codes one character at a time | typing | AUTOMATE | nobody chooses this; per-box code fields need it | Its own KDoc says per-character is indistinguishable from a whole commit in a normal field, so there is nothing to decide. |
| Fahrenheit | tool/weather | AUTOMATE | US users; everyone else already has the right unit | Region tells you this for free, exactly as the weekend and calendar defaults already do. Wind stays km/h either way, which is inconsistent. |
| Second calendar | tool/calendar | demote | people who need two alternate calendars at once | Genuine for Bangladesh (Bengali plus Hijri); elsewhere the second slot is usually empty and could be revealed on demand. |
| Degree readout | tool/compass | demote | nobody much; it only hides a number | Pure cosmetics on a novelty panel. Harmless, but it is the kind of row a 500-entry settings app accumulates. |
| Angle readout | tool/level | demote | nobody much; hides the pitch/roll numbers | Same class as the compass degree readout: a cosmetic toggle on a tool most people open twice. |
| Southern hemisphere | tool/moon | AUTOMATE | southern-hemisphere viewers, who see a mirrored moon | A real difference, but the app can often infer it: the weather location's latitude sign answers it without asking. |
| Icon colour (start) | tool/AI | demote | theme tinkerer matching the toolbar palette | Cosmetic, already gated behind the global colourful-tool-icons switch. Pure decoration. |
| Icon colour (end) | tool/AI | demote | same tinkerer, gradient variant | Second row only when gradients are on. Derived default keeps the pair moving together until pinned. |
| Keyword is case sensitive | tool/AI | demote | nobody in practice for the word "ai" | Generic per-tool control; cannot be removed for one tool. Off matches every stored keyword being lower case. |
| Show model reasoning | tool/AI | demote | someone debugging a slow local reasoning model | KDoc: off hides <think> behind a progress bar and strips it from the result. Raw think text is noise for normal use. |
| Model picker on the panel | tool/AI | demote | user with 2+ backends who wants panel height back | Row already auto-hides below two choices, so the switch only matters to a multi-provider user who never switches. |
| Start from the saved prompt | ai_action_edit | merge | user editing a template each run | Only ever shown under the switch above; the pair is really one three-way choice: saved / ask blank / ask prefilled. |
| Write the whole prompt | ai_action_edit | demote | prompt engineer writing their own safety wording | Off keeps the injection guard that stops field text being read as commands. Also how unrecognised legacy prompts are preserved. |
| Filter by action | ai_history | demote | heavy user isolating one action's failures | Only drawn when more than one action appears in the data, so the row stays short. |
| Icon colour (start / end) | tool/VOICE | demote | theme tinkerer | Cosmetic, gated behind colourful tool icons; the end row only appears with gradients on. |
| Keyword is case sensitive | tool/VOICE | demote | nobody — "dictate" is never a proper noun | Generic per-tool control that cannot be removed for one tool alone. |
| Browse the catalog + size filter | tool/VOICE | demote | user hunting a specific Whisper size | Collapsed by default because "Yours" and "Suggested" answer the question for most people; single-language graphs only appear for enabled languages. |
| Theme gallery style | themes | demote | someone who finds family cards with dots confusing | Auto already resolves from the persona quiz. A three-way segmented row above the grid to restyle a list is over-served. |
| Import FlorisBoard theme | themes | demote | FlorisBoard migrants, once, ever | A once-in-a-lifetime migration action holding permanent space beside Create and Import. Belongs in an overflow menu. |
| Play animated image | theme-editor | AUTOMATE | anyone who picked a GIF and expected it to move | Picking a GIF silently shows one frame until a switch further down is found. The decoder already knows the file is animated. |
| Menu shape | theme-editor | demote | a theme author overruling the safety rule; very few | safeContainerKind already picks right: decorative shapes fall back to rounded because a slanted menu clips its rows. |
| Card shape | theme-editor | demote | a theme author overruling the safety rule; very few | Same rule as the menu shape: a scalloped card eats its own first line, so the derived answer is nearly always right. |
| Pause learning | tools/incognito | demote | nobody obvious — off makes incognito nearly a no-op | Also gates typing-stat counting. Off leaves incognito doing only chip-blocking and a badge. |
| Pause clipboard capture | tools/incognito | demote | user who wants clip history kept even in incognito | Same shape as Pause learning: a switch that subtracts from the feature it sits under. |
| Follow private browsing (duplicate) | tools/incognito | merge | nobody — same field drawn twice | Identical field to the Privacy row; two rows for one boolean invites the did-I-set-both question. |
| Internet | permissions | demote | privacy auditor checking the manifest without reading it | No control at all — pure inventory. Three such rows is a third of the screen for zero action. |
| Network state | permissions | demote | privacy auditor | Only used for the metered-connection check before large downloads; not actionable. |
| Vibrate | permissions | demote | nobody, realistically | Haptics have their own settings; listing the permission adds nothing a user can act on. |
| Keyboard font | accessibility | demote | typography tinkerers | Same font browser as Appearance → Fonts. Second door is fine but it sits directly under the Readable-font shortcut. |
| Write the modifier names | typing (Physical keyboard) | demote | non-Mac users reading badges | Cosmetic badge wording. KDoc justifies on (⌃/⇧ glyphs are a Mac convention) but this belongs on the shortcuts sub-screen. |
| Time to press a key | typing (Physical keyboard) | demote | slow readers of the badge row | KDoc: three seconds was enough to act, nowhere near enough to read a bar of new badges. Belongs beside the leader. |
| Arrow keys move a highlight | typing (Physical keyboard) | demote | hardware users driving open panels | KDoc: without it a shortcut can open a tool but not use one. Off is nearly self-defeating; demote out of the main list. |
| Esc closes the tool | typing (Physical keyboard) | demote | nobody concrete — only fires while a panel is open | Only consumed when the keyboard actually has something open, so the app conflict it guards against barely exists. |
| Show the suggestion numbers | typing (Physical keyboard) | demote | hardware users who find the digits noisy | KDoc: the strip is the one thing a hardware user looks at constantly, so its keys are worth the ink. |
| Show the keyboard for shortcuts | typing (Physical keyboard) | demote | hardware users opening tools that need somewhere to draw | A tool with no surface is a no-op, and the IME is restored on close, so off is close to a broken state. |
| Key popup | tools / power saving | demote | barely anyone — the bubble is cheap to draw | KDoc itself calls it cheap. It is a switch whose whole justification is that it saves little. |
| Typing statistics | tools / power saving | REMOVE | nobody measurable | Its own KDoc: "the saving is unmeasured and speculative". A few integer bumps per keystroke does not deserve a switch. |
| Share GitHub link | about | merge | almost nobody — a third near-identical share row | On a non-Play build this is the third row that opens the same share sheet. One row with a source picker would do. |
| Dictionaries (Word lists group) | about | REMOVE | nobody | AboutScreens.kt:642 — the onClick is an empty lambda, yet NavRow draws a chevron promising navigation. Make it a caption or delete it. |
| Clear log | plugin detail | demote | plugin authors debugging | Whole Log group only appears once something was printed, which already keeps it out of everyone else's way. |
| Refresh (all repositories) | addons | demote | users waiting on a just-published addon | Every visit already refreshes every repository in the background (AddonScreens.kt:443), so the button is mostly redundant. |

### Worth asking during onboarding

| Setting | Screen | Default | Why it is a real split |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mode (System / Light / Dark / Amoled) | themes | System | Genuinely split preference, one tap to answer, and the onboarding wizard already asks it. |
| Number row | layout | On | KDoc: digits otherwise cost a ?123 trip. But a 6th row is ~18% more screen. Wizard already asks it with a live preview. |
| Emoji key instead of 🌐 | layout | On | Costs multilingual users their globe key, pushing switching onto a spacebar swipe. The wizard already asks it with a keyboard preview. |
| Key press haptics | keypress | On | Genuinely split taste, highest-impact feedback switch, one tap with an immediate buzz. The wizard already asks it on the feedback page. |
| Quick swipe on the spacebar | typing | Language | Genuinely tied and high impact; already the wizard's one gestures question, asked as a pair with hold+swipe. |
| Press and hold, then swipe | typing | Cursor | Asked as one paired question with quick swipe in onboarding — one question, not two. |
| Go back to the letters | layout | Off | KDoc names the split itself: half the audience wants one character, half types a whole line of punctuation. Affects every symbol trip; askable in one sentence. |
| Number row | layout | On | KDoc: every phone this decade has room for a sixth row. Genuinely ~50/50 and a whole row of height — already asked on the wizard's gestures page. |
| Default skin tone | emoji | None (✋ yellow) | No tone is a majority; affects every 👍 sent, and asking is one tap on six swatches. Not currently in the wizard. |
| Emoji font | emoji | System | Already asked in the wizard with a live tofu-vs-Noto comparison (OnboardingPages.kt:800). System is the only safe zero-download default. |
| Hide emoji that your device cannot show | emoji | Off | Off is right globally; the wizard already asks exactly the users who need it, and only when tofu is detected (OnboardingPages.kt:821). |
| Location | tool/weather | unset | The tool is inert without it and the keyboard never reads GPS, so it must be asked. The onboarding tool page already shares this composable. |
| How many languages do you type in? | onboarding (persona) | Unset (treated as one) | Genuinely ~50/50, one tap, and it decides whether the globe key is dead. Already asked; do not ask again elsewhere. |
| How much keyboard do you want? | onboarding (persona) | Unset (treated as balanced) | Highest-leverage question in the app: replaces the enable-all-70-tools default and gates four wizard pages. |
| How private should it be? | onboarding (persona) | Unset (treated as standard) | Strict turns off learn-from-typing, clipboard history and stats, and adds incognito — three settings people rarely find. |

### Settings that should exist and do not

| Proposed setting | Area | Hard-coded at | Proposed default | Why |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Suggestion strip text size | appearance | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/ui/KeyboardScreen.kt:3031` | x1.0, range 0.8-1.4 | Key labels scale 0.7-1.5x but suggestion words are pinned at 16sp; low-vision users cannot enlarge them. |
| Toolbox tool label size | appearance | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/ui/KeyboardScreen.kt:5898` | follow toolbarLabelSize | Toolbar labels get a 7-14sp slider; the toolbox captions are hard-coded at 12.5sp. |
| Installed-fonts list for the other 20 script sections | appearance | `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:8094` | show the library, filtered by script, in every section | A user who installs a Devanagari or Thai font has no picker anywhere to select it. |
| Reset Appearance to defaults | appearance | `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:4673` | per-group Reset action | Nine sliders on this screen with no way back; only pinned tools and icons have a reset. |
| CJK candidate text size | appearance | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/ui/KeyboardScreen.kt:3214` | follow the suggestion text size | Candidate row is fixed at 20sp; CJK users needing bigger or denser candidates cannot change it. |
| Learn a word only after N uses | dictionaries | `core/prediction/.../UserLexicon.kt:98 (learnWord count=1) + SuggestionEngine.kt:1439` | 2 | One typo committed once is permanently exempt from autocorrect; users have no way to raise the bar |
| Seed a phone format from the device region | dictionaries | `core/settings/.../SettingsRepository.kt:2465 (phoneFormats = emptySet())` | one mask derived from the SIM/locale dial code, editable | Empty means every 7-15 digit run becomes a chip — the exact false positive the screen exists to fix |
| Clear the whole blacklist | dictionaries | `app/.../MainActivity.kt:6296 (BlacklistSettings)` | n/a (action) | Only per-row deletion, and it is DataStore-backed so the Storage screen offers no bulk clear either |
| Reorder or pin snippets | dictionaries | `app/.../MainActivity.kt:12081 (SnippetSettings) / SnippetStore.items()` | manual drag order, new snippets appended | Panel and list both use creation order, and the panel has no search, so common snippets sink |
| Search / filter the personal dictionary | dictionaries | `app/.../MainActivity.kt:6192 + UserLexicon.kt:379 (MAX_WORDS = 10_000)` | search field, plus a per-language filter using the wordLang already stored | Up to 10,000 words render as one flat count-sorted list; finding a word to delete is impractical |
| Disable a custom word list without deleting it | dictionaries | `app/.../MainActivity.kt:7771 (delete is the only per-list action)` | enabled | Testing whether a bad import is polluting suggestions costs a delete and a re-import |
| Show languages that have lists on disk but are switched off | dictionaries | `app/.../MainActivity.kt:7742 (for (language in settings.enabledLanguages))` | include any langId with files present | Lists for a disabled language become unmanageable here; the twin emoji-keyword screen already handles this case |
| Ask before downloading on mobile data | languages | `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:435` | On | Whisper and LLM downloads confirm on metered networks; language packs and a 42 MB CJK dict do not check at all |
| Default word-list size tier | languages | `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:1056` | Large | Tier is per-composition state, so it snaps back to Large on every visit; wanting Everything means re-picking per language |
| Auto-link romanized languages | languages | `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:5319` | On | autoPairRomanizedSecondaries runs on every add, so a secondary link removed by hand comes back next time any language is added |
| Forget remembered apps (per-app language) | languages | `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:2289` | an action row, no stored value | layoutByPackage is written invisibly and has no viewer or clear action; one accidental switch pins that app forever |
| Per-pair Fuzzy Pinyin | languages | `core/input/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/input/composer/PinyinFuzzy.kt:18` | all pairs on when Fuzzy is on | 11 confusion groups are all-or-nothing; Sogou/Google Pinyin let users pick pairs, e.g. an/ang on but n/l off |
| Morse commit pause | languages | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/WMKeyboardService.kt:15645` | 750 ms | Fixed 750 ms; slow or motor-impaired users lose sequences mid-letter, and the docs already flag it as unadjustable |
| Split only on large screens | layout | `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:936` | Auto: split when smallest width >= 600 dp or landscape | Split is one global flag; a portrait phone can turn on a layout that is unusable there and stays split after rotating back. |
| Per-screen key spacing, side padding, bottom row height | layout | `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/ScreenVariant.kt:53` | null = follow portrait | SizingOverride carries height/width/padding/font/alignment but not these three, and landscape wants tighter spacing most. |
| Per-screen number row | layout | `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/ScreenVariant.kt:53` | null = follow portrait | A landscape phone has the least room for a sixth row, but the digit row is all-or-nothing across every screen shape. |
| Floating keyboard height | layout | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/ui/KeyboardScreen.kt:1601` | 100% | Height is drag-only between 0.6x and 1.6x while width gets a slider; no way to type an exact height or undo a bad drag. |
| Reset floating position and size | layout | `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:942` | centre-bottom, 320 dp, 100% | floatingXFraction/YFraction and floatingHeightScale are only ever written by drags; a panel dragged awkwardly has no settings-side way back. |
| Reset size and position | layout | `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:5052` | 48 dp / 42 dp / follow keys / 0% / 100% / SDK-based padding / 100% | The seven-slider Size and position group has no reset; only per-variant overrides do, and the Resize tool's Reset only undoes that session. |
| Key repeat start delay | keypress | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/ui/KeyboardScreen.kt:11284 (also :11441)` | 300 ms, independent of the long-press delay | Repeat starts after longPressDelayMs, so slowing accents for tremor also delays backspace repeat. |
| Sound on key repeat | keypress | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/ui/KeyboardScreen.kt:11290 (also :11455)` | On | vibrateOnRepeat gates the buzz, but the click still machine-guns on every held backspace with no gate. |
| Which letters carry hold shortcuts | keypress | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/ui/KeyboardScreen.kt:9367` | a c v x z y, user-remappable | Hardcoded to literal a/c/v/x/z/y, so all six toggles silently do nothing on non-Latin layouts. |
| Backspace swipe word distance | keypress | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/ui/KeyboardScreen.kt:11267` | 72 dp first word, 56 dp after | 72 dp for the first word is a long pull on a small phone and a twitch on a tablet. |
| Follow the system touch-vibration setting | keypress | `core/feedback/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/feedback/HapticPlayer.kt:114` | Off (keeps today's behaviour) | FLAG_IGNORE_GLOBAL_SETTING means turning off system touch vibration does not quiet the keyboard. |
| Double-space window | typing | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/WMKeyboardService.kt:5325` | 400 ms, range 200-800 ms | 400 ms hard-coded: slow or tremor-affected typists never get the full stop, fast typists get it by accident |
| Punctuation chip set | typing | `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/WMKeyboardService.kt:15656` | . , ? ! ' (editable list) | Fixed to . , ? ! ' — a Bengali user cannot add danda, a Spanish user cannot add inverted marks. currencyKeys is the precedent |
| Turn the swipe trail off | typing | `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:3183` | range 0-100% with Off at 0 | Opacity slider floors at 0.1, so no-trail gliding is only reachable via power saving mode, which changes other things too |

### Documentation to fix

| Page | Severity | Problem |
|---|---|---|
| `docs/src/content/docs/themes/fonts.mdx` | wrong | Lines 19 and 82 say a missing Play Services font provider makes a Google Font row fall back silently to the system face. The code removes every Google Font row (MainActivity.kt:8153) and shows an explanatory paragraph instead (MainActivity.kt:8039-8046). |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/fonts.mdx` | wrong | Line 43 repeats the same wrong claim about Play Services being missing; the rows are hidden, not silently substituted. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/appearance.mdx` | wrong | Tool background shape row says "the same eleven shapes as Key shape"; KeyShapeKind has 12 entries and the picker iterates all of them. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/icons.mdx` | stale | "Two ungrouped rows sit below the pack list" — there are three: the addon-store row "Get more icon packs" (IconScreens.kt:253) is undocumented. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/fonts.mdx` | gap | The addon-store group at the top of the Fonts screen (MainActivity.kt:8047) is never described; only the reverse deep-link from Addons is. |
| `MISSING: Theme gallery style (Auto / Grouped / Flat)` | gap | appUi.themeGalleryStyle is a visible ChoiceControl on the themes screen (ThemeScreens.kt:875-879) and appears in no docs page; reference/settings/themes.mdx documents Mode and Material You but not this. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/smart/personal-dictionary.mdx` | wrong | Says Add word 'sets a new word's weight to exactly 200' (also reference/settings/dictionaries.mdx line 17). UserLexicon.addWord ADDS 200 to the existing count, so adding a word already typed 50 times yields 250. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/tools/snippets.mdx` | wrong | {timezone} documented as 'e.g. GMT+06:00'. SnippetVariable.TIMEZONE formats with "zzzz", the long zone name (e.g. 'Bangladesh Standard Time'); the GMT-offset form only appears as a fallback. |
| `MISSING: phone number formats reference` | gap | No reference/settings page. One paragraph in tools/clipboard.mdx:68 is all there is; mask syntax (X wildcards, pinning a digit, the 4-15 digit bounds, trunk-0 and dial-code leeway, why +XX degrades to a national mask) and the live tester are undocumented. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/smart/personal-dictionary.mdx` | gap | No mention of the 32-character word-length limit. Both learnWord and addWord silently drop longer words, so Add word appears to succeed and nothing is added. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/dictionaries.mdx` | gap | No mention of the 10,000-word cap, the 9,000 eviction target or the half-life decay in UserLexicon.compactIfNeeded. A user whose old words vanished has nothing to read. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/languages/dictionaries.mdx` | stale | Lines 85-86 give the paths as 'Typing / Custom dictionaries' and 'Typing / Personal dictionary'; reference/settings/dictionaries.mdx gives the same rows as 'Typing / Suggestions / …'. Both rows are in the Suggestions group. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/tools/snippets.mdx` | stale | Line 119 notes delete has no confirmation but the page never warns that the trash sits immediately beside the edit pencil on every row, which is where the misfire comes from. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` | wrong | Says "List languages in Android's switcher ... default on" (lines 56 and 129). SettingsRepository.kt:1203 is osLanguageSwitcher = false, and the KDoc explains why off is right. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/languages/overview.mdx` | wrong | Line 81 repeats "List languages in Android's switcher, under System language switcher, is on by default". Code default is off. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/languages/switching.mdx` | wrong | Lines 98 and 129 repeat the same "on by default" claim for the system switcher. Code default is off. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/languages/notation.mdx` | wrong | Line 104 says music/braille/morse/ipa each get "a Numerals choice (defaults to Auto on all four)". All four are numeralSystem = LATIN, and the group only renders when it differs, so no Numerals group appears on any of them. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` | wrong | Line 93 says "Also suggest from ... all start off". RomanizedPairing auto-links romanized pairs both ways on add (SettingsRepository.kt:5319), so Banglish+Bengali start on and even toast about it. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` | wrong | Line 26 gives the region order as SIM, network, then locale. DeviceLocales.kt reads SIM, network, time zone, then locale — the time-zone signal is missing (overview.mdx has it right). |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` | gap | The per-language walkthrough ("up to eight groups", line 66) omits two groups the code renders: Spellings / Use known spellings (on by default, LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:743) and Style / Fancy style (line 702). |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` | stale | Row titles drifted from the shipped strings: docs say "List languages in Android's switcher" and "Choose languages in system settings"; the app draws "List languages in the Android switcher" and "Select languages in the system settings". |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` | stale | Line 156 documents an internal source comment as a user-facing caveat. The comment (LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:1350) and its unreachable "coming soon" branch should be deleted from the code rather than described in the reference. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/languages/notation.mdx` | stale | Line 104 says "only English (QWERTY) and the three Bangla layouts start on by default", which is the raw fallback (BuiltInLayouts.defaultEnabledIds) and contradicts overview.mdx's seeded-from-your-phone story. bengali.mdx:71 makes the same claim. |
| `MISSING: metered-connection behaviour for language data` | gap | No page states that language packs, emoji keywords and the 42 MB CJK dictionaries download on mobile data with no confirmation, unlike Whisper/LLM models which do confirm. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/layout.mdx` | wrong | Number row default listed as "Off (On on a tablet)". Code default is On for every device (SettingsRepository.kt:1045), which also makes the tablet overlay branch in DeviceFormDefaults.kt:38 unreachable — numberRowUntouched && !numberRow can never be true. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/layout.mdx` | wrong | Bottom padding range given as 0–40 dp in both the Size & position table and the Per-screen table. MAX_BOTTOM_PADDING_DP is 160 (raised in commit 238966fd for the Resize tool) and both sliders use it. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/typing/size-position.mdx` | wrong | Same stale bottom-padding claim: "from 0dp to 40dp". Actual slider is 0–160 dp. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/typing/size-position.mdx` | wrong | Side padding described as shaving an equal margin off both edges. dockedWidthArrangement (KeyboardScreen.kt:1370) folds it into one widthFraction, so with Keyboard position Left or Right the whole reduction lands on one side. Same claim in the layout_side_padding_info string. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/layout.mdx` | stale | Details bullet claims "Symbols on shift isn't in Settings search". It is indexed at SettingsSearch.kt:375. |
| `MISSING: Resize toolbar tool` | gap | ToolbarTool.RESIZE (inline drag-resize over the docked keyboard, Reset/Done pills, 160 dp padding ceiling, shipped 2026-08-09) appears in no docs page — not layout.mdx, not typing/size-position.mdx, not reference/settings/tools.mdx. It is now the primary way sizing gets changed. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` | wrong | Timing table still lists one "Key repeat interval"; the screen has two sliders, Delete repeat interval and Space repeat interval (both 20-200 ms, 50 ms). |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` | wrong | "Hold ?123 for numpad" documented as default Off; LayoutBehaviorSettings.symbolsLongPressNumpad defaults to true. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` | wrong | Minimum popup duration default given as 150 ms; KeyPopupSettings.minDurationMs is 140. |

| `docs/src/content/docs/typing/feedback.mdx` | wrong | Options table gives Haptic style default "Key"; the stored default is Tap (SYSTEM_TAP). The wizard does write Key, which the page never explains. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` | gap | Key popup table omits the Popup shape nav row (default Rounded) and the Popup corner radius slider (0-40 dp, 12 dp). |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` | stale | Search-index bullet is out of date: Key sound, Sound volume, Maximum popup duration and Hold ?123 are indexed now. Only Haptic style, Sound style and Key release sound are missing. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` | stale | Row and group names still read "Long-press …"; the UI strings now say "Press and hold …" (group title "Press and hold shortcuts"). |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` | gap | Popup height documented as one 110 dp value; the setting stores a separate height per bubble style (110 on-key, 65 floating) and a theme can override it. |

| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/typing.mdx` | wrong | Line 39: 'the confidence slider and the next three toggles only appear while it's on' — 11 rows are gated on autocorrect, not four |

| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/typing.mdx` | gap | Gestures table omits 'Ask when a glide is unclear' (gesture.ambiguityPicker, default On), shown whenever glide typing is in Type words mode |

| `MISSING: mixed-language detection (suggestionStrip.languageDetection + languageDetectionStrength)` | gap | No page anywhere describes per-field language detection or its Gentle/Balanced/Aggressive strengths, though it is on by default and shifts both ranking and autocorrect |
| `MISSING: one-time-code chips from notifications (OtpSettings)` | gap | A notification-access-gated, privacy-sensitive feature with five settings has no docs page; smart/chips.mdx documents only the clipboard fast path |
| `MISSING: fix in-app info text, app/src/main/res/values/strings_typing.xml:91` | wrong | typing_auto_capitalize_info says capitalization happens 'only in English mode'; the code gates on script.hasLetterCase (WMKeyboardService.kt:15234), so every cased script gets it |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` | wrong | "Only enabled layouts appear on either list" and "Your layouts: the enabled layouts you've made" are wrong. LayoutEditorScreens.kt:388-404 deliberately does NOT filter customs by enabled — the comment says filtering made Duplicate and Import produce layouts that then vanished. Same claim in languages/custom-layouts.mdx:103. |
| `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` | wrong | "Enabling a layout under Languages doesn't consult this list directly; you can switch on something broken" is wrong. rememberLayoutEnableGate (LayoutEditorScreens.kt:772) now blocks enabling and names every BLOCKING finding. | `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` | wrong | Edge-case bullet claims the JSON serializer "writes every field explicitly, defaults included" and that the on-screen caption is misleading. | `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` | wrong | Action picker is "25 options across six groups" and the Typing list omits Caps lock. KeyActionCatalog has 26 entries, including CapsLock. Same count in languages/custom-layouts.mdx:45. |
