## Screen by screen

### Typing, autocorrect, suggestions, glide

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Autocorrect | `autocorrect` | toggle | On | keep | everyone typing prose on glass; off for code/transliteration purists | ok |  | Master switch gating 11 rows below it. Also a one-tap toolbar tool, so it is genuinely flipped often. |
| Autocorrect confidence | `autocorrectConfidence` | slider | x4.0 (1.5-10) | demote | users burned by wrong corrections who won't disable autocorrect wholesale | ok |  | KDoc: mirrors SuggestionEngine.DEFAULT_AUTOCORRECT_CONFIDENCE=4.0. Adaptive confidence already moves it automatically, so few need the raw dial. |
| Adaptive confidence | `autocorrectAdaptive` | toggle | On | AUTOMATE | nobody — nobody wants corrections to keep firing after they undo them | ok |  | KDoc describes a pure self-correcting loop anchored to the slider. No user story for off; drop the toggle. |
| Typing rhythm | `suggestionStrip.timingSignalStrength` | slider | 0% (off) | REMOVE | nobody — ships at 0, so the signal never runs for any user | WRONG → 50% if the signal is validated, else delete the feature |  | A slider shipped at off is a feature nobody discovers. Either enable it at a tuned value or drop it. |
| Undo autocorrect with backspace | `revertAutocorrectOnBackspace` | toggle | On | keep | anyone whose name or slang keeps getting corrected | ok |  | Also teaches the original word back. Off exists for people who want backspace strictly literal. |
| Skip words in capital letters | `autocorrectSkipAllCaps` | toggle | On | demote | habitual ALL-CAPS typists who still want their typos fixed | ok |  | KDoc: acronyms and shouting are deliberate. The off case is real but rare; belongs below the fold. |
| Block offensive words | `suggestionStrip.blockOffensiveWords` | toggle | On | keep | adults who swear constantly and want the words suggested | ok |  | KDoc: on as AOSP ships it; the words can still be typed verbatim, so off only affects the strip. |
| Context-aware suggestions | `suggestionStrip.contextRerank` | toggle | On | AUTOMATE | nobody — nobody asks for worse-ranked suggestions | ok |  | Info text: reorders only on real evidence and never changes autocorrect. Pure on-device win with no off story. |
| Add missing spaces | `suggestionStrip.autocorrectSplits` | toggle | On | demote | someone typing compound IDs who hates surprise splits | ok |  | Heavily guarded (both halves must be words, revertible with backspace). Narrow off case for a top-level row. |
| Detect the language you type | `suggestionStrip.languageDetection` | toggle | On | keep | Banglish/Hinglish typists mixing two languages in one field | ok |  | KDoc: inert unless the language has secondary suggestion languages, so monolinguals never see an effect. |
| Detection strength | `suggestionStrip.languageDetectionStrength` | choice | Balanced | demote | heavy code-switchers who find Balanced too timid or too pushy | ok |  | Three calibrated points; already nested under the detection toggle. Correctly advanced. |
| Fix number row slips | `suggestionStrip.numberRowCorrections` | toggle | On | AUTOMATE | nobody — the single-digit-only rule already protects room3 | ok |  | KDoc: only same-length single-digit swaps are trusted, and it is already gated on the number row. |
| Adapt to app tone | `suggestionStrip.registerPriors` | toggle | Off | AUTOMATE | nobody finds it; ranking-only, buried among 21 corrections rows | WRONG → On |  | KDoc: ranking only, nothing hidden or blocked. Off by default means the work never reaches a real user. |
| Fix missing apostrophes | `autoApostrophe` | toggle | On | keep | everyone typing English contractions | ok |  | Fixed contraction lookup, not a guess; runs even with autocorrect off. Off for people who write dont deliberately. |
| Automatic capital letters | `autoCapitalize` | toggle | On | keep | everyone writing sentences; off for lowercase-aesthetic typists | ok |  | Gated on script.hasLetterCase, so Bengali is unaffected. The in-app info text wrongly says English-only. |
| Double space for a full stop | `doubleSpacePeriod` | toggle | On | merge | everyone ending sentences without a symbols-layer trip | ok |  | Tab wins over period when both are on — that is a three-way radio wearing two switches. Merge into one choice. |
| Double space for a tab | `doubleSpaceTab` | toggle | Off | merge | form-fillers and people indenting notes | ok |  | Occupies the same double-space slot as the period rule; should be one 'Double space types...' choice. |
| Automatic space after punctuation | `autoSpaceAfterPunctuation` | toggle | Off | keep | fast chat typists who never space after a comma | ok |  | KDoc: it changes what a keypress produces, which is the one kind of help that has to be asked for. |
| Space after a suggestion | `suggestionStrip.autoSpaceAfterSuggestion` | toggle | On | keep | typists in languages or fields where a trailing space is wrong | ok |  | Filed under corrections with the other space rules; a resumed mid-word never gets a second space. |
| Wrap the selection with brackets | `textEditing.wrapSelectionWithPair` | toggle | On | keep | anyone quoting or parenthesising selected text | ok |  | Off restores plain replace-selection for people who habitually type over a selection. |
| Shift changes the case of the selection | `textEditing.recapitalizeSelectionWithShift` | toggle | On | keep | anyone fixing SHOUTED or mis-cased text | ok |  | Off keeps shift meaning next-character-only, a real habit conflict for people who select then type. |
| Suggestions | `suggestions` | toggle | On | keep | privacy-minded and distraction-free typists | ok |  | Master switch for the strip; autocorrect stays separate, which is the right split. |
| Punctuation suggestions | `suggestionStrip.punctuation` | toggle | Off | keep | people who hate the symbols-layer trip for a comma | ok |  | Competes with the emoji tail for the same slot; off keeps the strip for words, which most people want. |
| Suggestions in every field | `showSuggestionsInAllFields` | toggle | On | keep | Instagram/Keep users whose apps wrongly suppress the strip | ok |  | KDoc: many apps set NO_SUGGESTIONS on ordinary text fields; on matches what most keyboards quietly do. |
| Suggestion strip always visible | `suggestionStrip.suggestionsFirst` | toggle | Off | keep | people who prefer words over the toolbar at rest | ok |  | Toolbar-first at rest is the app's identity; this is the escape hatch for strip-first users. |
| Best suggestion in the middle | `suggestionStrip.suggestionPrimaryCenter` | toggle | On | keep | Gboard switchers (centre) versus AOSP/SwiftKey switchers (left) | split |  | A genuine taste split, but low impact and instantly reversible — not worth spending an onboarding question. |
| Suggest contact names | `contactSuggestions` | toggle | Off | keep | anyone typing friends' names; also feeds the system spell checker | ok |  | Permission-gated with a prominent disclosure, so off by default is mandatory, not a preference. |
| Suggest contact emails | `contactEmailSuggestions` | toggle | Off | keep | people who type addresses into chat and web forms | ok |  | Same permission as names but a different index; separate toggle is justified by the flat 4000 weight it carries. |
| Contact emails in email fields too | `contactEmailSuggestionsInEmailFields` | toggle | On | REMOVE | nobody — a third-level knob under an off-by-default toggle | ok |  | Email fields are the main reason to enable email completion at all; hard-code it on. |
| Suggest app names | `appNameSuggestions` | toggle | Off | demote | people who name apps in messages ('use Signal') | ok |  | x400 source weight pollutes the strip, so off is right — but the payoff is too thin for a top-level row. |
| Inline emoji search | `inlineEmojiSearch` | toggle | On | keep | anyone who types :smi for an emoji; off for people typing timestamps | ok |  | Colon-triggered, so it hijacks a real character; the off switch earns its place. |
| Password manager suggestions | `inlineAutofill` | toggle | On | keep | Bitwarden/1Password users; off for people who dislike login chips | ok |  | Android 11+ only. Chips are drawn by the manager itself; the keyboard only lends the row. |
| Smart replies | `suggestionStrip.systemSmartReplies` | toggle | On | keep | privacy-minded users who don't want the system reading the conversation | split |  | KDoc argues the separate lane well; but on-by-default lets Android System Intelligence read chats in a privacy-forward app. |
| Smart detection of key hits | `layoutBehavior.smartHitDetection` | toggle | Off | keep | large-thumbed fast typists on small screens | ok |  | It changes what a keypress produces, which by this app's own rule must be opted into. Needs Suggestions on. |
| Personal dictionary | `-` | nav | — | keep | everyone who taught the keyboard a word | - |  | Nav row into the dictionaries domain; contents audited elsewhere. |
| Custom dictionaries | `-` | nav | — | keep | people importing wordlists for a niche vocabulary | - |  | Nav row into the dictionaries domain; contents audited elsewhere. |
| Suggestion blacklist | `suggestionBlacklist` | nav | empty | keep | anyone with a word the keyboard keeps forcing on them | ok |  | Subtitle switches to a live count once non-empty, which is the right amount of state on a nav row. |
| Smart chips | `smartSuggestions` | toggle | On | keep | people who want the strip reserved for words only | ok |  | Master switch for four chip families that can each take the whole strip. |
| Calculate as you type | `smartCalc` | toggle | On | keep | anyone typing sums; off for people writing dates and scores | ok |  | Mirrored inside the Calculator tool's own options — same setting, two surfaces, correctly done. |
| Convert currencies | `smartCurrency` | toggle | On | keep | people who quote prices across currencies | ok |  | Subtitle names the live target currency, so the row explains itself without opening the tool. |
| Convert units | `smartUnits` | toggle | On | keep | metric/imperial straddlers | ok |  | Short abbreviations only trigger glued to the number, so false positives are already rare. |
| Tool keywords | `smartToolKeywords` | toggle | On | keep | people whose ordinary vocabulary collides with keywords like wiki or calc | ok |  | Narrow chip, so it costs little; the off case is a real annoyance for some vocabularies. |
| Codes from notifications | `otp.enabled` | toggle | Off | keep | anyone pasting 2FA codes several times a week | ok |  | Needs notification access, so off twice over by design. Codes are never persisted. |
| Allow notification access | `-` | nav | — | keep | users who enabled codes but haven't granted access yet | - |  | Only shown while the grant is missing — a status row, not a preference. |
| Only in number fields | `otp.numberFieldsOnly` | toggle | On | demote | users of apps that put the code box behind a plain text input | ok |  | Real but narrow; the failure it fixes (no chip appears) is hard to connect to this row. |
| Offer a code for | `otp.expiryMinutes` | slider | 3 min (1-10) | demote | people on carriers where the SMS lands minutes late | ok |  | KDoc: a chip showing last hour's code is worse than no chip. Few users will ever tune this. |
| Clear the notification | `otp.dismissNotification` | toggle | Off | keep | people who hate a shade full of spent code notifications | ok |  | Off is right: cancelling someone's notification is a side effect that should be asked for. |
| Type codes one character at a time | `otp.perDigitEntry` | toggle | On | AUTOMATE | nobody — the KDoc says it is indistinguishable in an ordinary single field | ok |  | Strictly better everywhere per its own KDoc: whole-commit drops all but the first character in split code boxes. |
| Glide typing | `gestureTyping` | toggle | On | keep | swipe typists; off for one-thumb tappers who trigger it by accident | ok |  | Misfires are already handled by the post-type cooldown, so the default rarely needs changing. |
| Handwrite with swipes | `letterSwipeAction` | choice | Type words | keep | full-edition users who write faster than they tap | ok |  | Full builds only (bundled ML Kit model). Reassigns the same gesture, so a choice not a toggle is right. |
| Glide across the spacebar | `gesture.spaceGlideMultiWord` | toggle | On | demote | people whose glides overshoot the spacebar and split words | ok |  | Only shown in glide-word mode. Real but advanced; belongs under the glide sub-group. |
| Ask when a glide is unclear | `gesture.ambiguityPicker` | toggle | On | keep | Bengali fixed-layout glide users (ka/kha share one key) | ok |  | KDoc: committing the likelier word silently is the worst option. Lifting without choosing still commits the leader. |
| Space after a glided word | `gesture.autoSpaceAfterGlide` | toggle | On | keep | people gliding in languages where a trailing space is wrong | ok |  | Third of three independent auto-space rules; the docs already flag that none covers the others. |
| Swipe start distance | `gesture.startThresholdSlop` | slider | x2.0 (0.5-4) | demote | shaky hands (raise it) and light swipers (lower it) | ok |  | Multiple of system touch slop. Genuine accessibility value but pure advanced tuning. |
| Delay after typing | `gesture.postTypeCooldownMs` | slider | 160 ms (0-500) | demote | fast tap-typists whose finger slides off a key mid-burst | ok |  | The main defence against accidental glides; 0 disables it. Advanced, but it is the fix people search for. |
| Extra time for a dot or a cross | `gesture.handwriteDotCooldownMs` | slider | 700 ms (0-1500) | demote | handwriters dotting an i or crossing a t as a second stroke | ok |  | Only visible in handwrite mode on full builds — already about as demoted as a row can be. |
| Trail width | `gesture.trailWidthDp` | slider | 10 dp (2-24) | merge | nobody specifically; three sliders for one cosmetic trail | ok |  | Width, length and opacity should be one trail-appearance control; the colour already comes from the theme. |
| Trail length | `gesture.trailDurationMs` | slider | 350 ms (100-1200) | merge | nobody specifically; garnish on a gesture already finished | ok |  | Same cosmetic knob family as trail width and opacity. Three sliders is two too many. |
| Trail opacity | `gesture.trailOpacity` | slider | 55% (10-100%) | keep | people who find the trail distracting but still want to glide | ok |  | Range floor is 0.1, so 'no trail at all' is unreachable here even though power saving forces 0. |
| Quick swipe on the spacebar | `spaceShortSwipe` | choice | Language | keep | bilinguals (Language) versus cursor-precision editors (Cursor) | split | yes | Genuinely tied and high impact; already the wizard's one gestures question, asked as a pair with hold+swipe. |
| Press and hold, then swipe | `spaceLongSwipe` | choice | Cursor | keep | same users as the quick-swipe slot, on the other gesture | split | yes | Asked as one paired question with quick swipe in onboarding — one question, not two. |
| 2-D cursor touchpad | `layoutBehavior.spaceCursor2d` | toggle | Off | keep | people editing multi-line text from the spacebar | ok |  | Claims the downward axis and so overrides swipe-down-to-hide; off by default is the safe ordering. |
| Swipe down to hide the keyboard | `layoutBehavior.spaceSwipeDownHide` | toggle | Off | keep | people who dismiss the keyboard constantly | ok |  | Shares its name with a separate toolbar toggle under Appearance — confusing, but both are legitimately independent. |
| Arrows on the spacebar | `spacebarLanguageArrows` | toggle | On | demote | people who find the arrows visual clutter | ok |  | Only shown when a swipe slot is set to Language and more than one mode is enabled. Pure cosmetics. |
| The spacebar shows | `layoutBehavior.spacebarDisplay` | choice | Language | keep | users with several layouts per language (Layout or Both) | ok |  | Language alone is ambiguous once one language has two layouts; the choice is earned. |
| Spacebar text | `spacebarLabel` | text | blank (live language name) | demote | people who want a custom or blank spacebar label | ok |  | Overlaps the row above: a custom label overrides the display choice entirely. Supports %s for the live name. |
| Swipe to delete words | `backspaceSwipeDelete` | toggle | On | keep | anyone deleting a phrase; off for people who delete precisely | ok |  | Whole-word deletion is destructive and un-undoable, so an off switch is warranted. |
| Shift and Enter type a new line | `layoutBehavior.shiftEnterNewline` | toggle | Off | keep | chat users who cannot otherwise put a line break in a message | split |  | KDoc fears a keyboard that stops sending messages; but only a user-armed shift counts, so On is arguably safe. |
| Volume keys move the cursor | `volumeCursor` | toggle | Off | keep | precision editors and users with motor impairments | ok |  | Hijacks hardware keys, so off by default is required. Only active while the keyboard shows. |
| Release the keys while audio plays | `volumeCursorMediaAware` | toggle | On | demote | people who type with music on and still want cursor keys | ok |  | A real trade for constant listeners, but it is a sub-toggle of an already off-by-default feature. |
| Process the physical keys | `hardwareKeyboardInput` | toggle | On | keep | Bengali/Avro typists on a Bluetooth keyboard | ok |  | KDoc: off types raw characters and hands input to the system layout. Load-bearing for transliteration users. |
| Tool shortcuts | `hardwareKeyboard.shortcutsEnabled` | toggle | On | keep | physical-keyboard users; off for anyone whose leader chord collides | ok |  | KDoc: the default leader is a double-tapped modifier that produces no character, so on is cheap. |
| Tool shortcuts list | `hardwareKeyboard.toolByLetter` | nav | Double-tap Ctrl + default letters | keep | hardware-keyboard users rebinding a tool letter | ok |  | Sub-screen shows the live leader as its value. Not deep-linkable, per the docs. |
| Ctrl + number opens a toolbar tool | `hardwareKeyboard.toolbarDigitChord` | toggle | On | keep | nobody net-positive by default: it steals browser tab switching | WRONG → Off |  | Its own KDoc admits browsers use exactly these and the keyboard wins in a focused field. The leader path already works. |
| Write the modifier names | `hardwareKeyboard.hintModifierWords` | toggle | On | demote | Mac users who read the caret and shift glyphs fluently | ok |  | Badge typography only. On is right for PC keycaps; the off case is people wanting narrower badges. |
| Time to press a key | `hardwareKeyboard.pickerTimeoutMs` | slider | 8 s (1-30 s) | demote | beginners who need longer to read a bar full of badges | ok |  | KDoc: this is really how long the badges stay up; three seconds was too short to read new ones. |
| Arrow keys move a highlight | `hardwareKeyboard.panelNavigation` | toggle | On | AUTOMATE | nobody — its own KDoc says without it a shortcut can open a tool but not use one | ok |  | Turning it off breaks the feature it belongs to. Fold it into Tool shortcuts. |
| Esc closes the tool | `hardwareKeyboard.escClosesPanel` | toggle | On | AUTOMATE | nobody — Esc is only consumed when a panel is actually open | ok |  | KDoc: a bare Escape still belongs to the app, so the vim/browser conflict this guards against cannot occur. |
| Number keys select suggestions | `hardwareKeyboard.suggestionHotkeys` | choice | Alt + number | keep | hardware typists picking a candidate without reaching for the screen | WRONG → After the shortcut key (LEADER_DIGIT) |  | The enum's own KDoc calls ALT_DIGIT opt-in because apps claim modifier+digit, yet it ships as the default. |
| Show the suggestion numbers | `hardwareKeyboard.suggestionHintsAlways` | toggle | On | demote | people who find permanent digit badges noisy | ok |  | Only shown in Alt+digit mode. KDoc: the strip is what a hardware user looks at, so the ink is worth it. |
| macOS shortcuts | `hardwareKeyboard.macShortcuts` | toggle | Off | keep | people pairing an Apple keyboard or Magic Keyboard with Android | ok |  | KDoc: on a PC keyboard Meta is the Search/Windows key and belongs to the system. Correctly opt-in. |
| Ctrl+Space changes the language | `hardwareKeyboard.languageSwitchChord` | toggle | On | keep | code-editor users who need Ctrl+Space for completion | ok |  | KDoc: the chord is the ChromeOS/Windows convention and core to a multilingual keyboard; the toggle exists for editor users. |
| Show the keyboard for shortcuts | `hardwareKeyboard.autoShowUi` | toggle | On | demote | laptop-style users who never want the on-screen board back | ok |  | State is restored when the tool closes, so the cost of on is temporary. Niche off case. |

**Missing here:** Double-space window (400 ms hard-coded: slow or tremor-affected typists never get the full stop, fast typists get it by accident); Punctuation chip set (Fixed to . , ? ! ' — a Bengali user cannot add danda, a Spanish user cannot add inverted marks. currencyKeys is the precedent); Turn the swipe trail off (Opacity slider floors at 0.1, so no-trail gliding is only reachable via power saving mode, which changes other things too); Per-app / per-mode autocorrect and suggestions (KeyboardMode overrides theme, toolbar and emoji row but not typing intelligence; code editors and terminals need corrections off per app); Spacebar cursor sensitivity (16 dp per character is hard-coded; users with tremor overshoot and users on large screens run out of spacebar); Backspace word-swipe sensitivity (The 72/56/28 dp acceleration curve is hard-coded, so an over-eager word delete has no user-side remedy except turning the gesture off); Number of suggestion slots (Fixed at three candidates; tablets and landscape have room for five, and the strip already shrinks long words to fit three)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/typing.mdx` — gap: Automatic corrections table is missing 8 shipped rows: Adaptive confidence, Typing rhythm, Context-aware suggestions, Add missing spaces, Detect the language you type, Detection strength, Fix number row slips, Adapt to app tone
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/typing.mdx` — gap: The whole 'One-time codes' group (Codes from notifications, Allow notification access, Only in number fields, Offer a code for, Clear the notification, Type codes one character at a time) is absent; the page says 'eight groups' but the screen has nine
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/typing.mdx` — wrong: Line 178: 'Number keys pick suggestions' default is given as 'After the shortcut key'; the shipped default is ALT_DIGIT, drawn as 'Alt + number' (SettingsRepository.kt:757)
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/typing.mdx` — wrong: Line 39: 'the confidence slider and the next three toggles only appear while it's on' — 11 rows are gated on autocorrect, not four
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/typing.mdx` — gap: Physical keyboard table is missing 6 shipped rows: Ctrl + number opens a toolbar tool, Write the modifier names, Time to press a key, Show the suggestion numbers, macOS shortcuts, Ctrl+Space changes the language
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/typing.mdx` — gap: Gestures table omits 'Ask when a glide is unclear' (gesture.ambiguityPicker, default On), shown whenever glide typing is in Type words mode
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/typing.mdx` — stale: Row names do not match the UI as drawn, so Settings-search terms fail: 'Gesture typing' vs 'Glide typing', 'Cooldown after typing' vs 'Delay after typing', 'Skip all-caps words' vs 'Skip words in capital letters', 'Suggestions bar always visible' vs 'Suggestion strip always visible'
- `docs/src/content/docs/smart/autocorrect.mdx` — gap: Options table (line 84) omits Adaptive confidence, Typing rhythm, Context-aware suggestions, Add missing spaces, language detection and number-row corrections; line 96 also claims the whole group is gated on the autocorrect switch
- `docs/src/content/docs/smart/suggestions.mdx` — gap: Options table (line 103) omits 'Adapt to app tone' and 'Context-aware suggestions' — both ship today and appear on no page of the site
- `docs/src/content/docs/typing/glide-typing.mdx` — gap: Options table (line 120) omits gesture.ambiguityPicker ('Ask when a glide is unclear'), whose KDoc treats it as a headline behaviour for fixed-layout Bengali glides
- `MISSING: mixed-language detection (suggestionStrip.languageDetection + languageDetectionStrength)` — gap: No page anywhere describes per-field language detection or its Gentle/Balanced/Aggressive strengths, though it is on by default and shifts both ranking and autocorrect
- `MISSING: one-time-code chips from notifications (OtpSettings)` — gap: A notification-access-gated, privacy-sensitive feature with five settings has no docs page; smart/chips.mdx documents only the clipboard fast path
- `MISSING: fix in-app info text, app/src/main/res/values/strings_typing.xml:91` — wrong: typing_auto_capitalize_info says capitalization happens 'only in English mode'; the code gates on script.hasLetterCase (WMKeyboardService.kt:15234), so every cased script gets it

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/SettingsUi.kt:673 — ResetSetting: a per-row restore icon, drawn only while the value differs from the shipped default. Landing right now (uncommitted working-tree change)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2523-3455 — every toggle, slider, choice and text row on the Typing screen now passes default = SettingsDefaults.<field>, so all ~78 non-nav rows carry that reset control`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11482 — TextFieldSetting draws the reset as a trailing icon (used by Spacebar text)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:3560 — 'Reset to defaults' button on the Tool shortcuts list sub-screen, behind the confirmation dialog at 3588`, `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:1616 — SettingsDefaults, the single source the reset controls compare against`, `No group-level or screen-level 'reset Typing' affordance exists`

_Coverage: Full coverage of the assigned range, read twice. Pass 1 covered MainActivity.kt lines 2455-3351 (the pre-edit numbering). Mid-audit the user edited the file concurrently: a per-row reset feature (SettingsDefaults + ResetSetting) landed, shifting TypingSettings to lines 2523-3455, so I re-read the entire function at the new numbering — no settings were added or removed, every row gained a default= argument. All 9 groups and all 83 entries are reported: Automatic corrections (21), Suggestions (16 incl. 3 nav rows), Smart chips (5), One-time codes (6), Gestures (18), Backspace (1), Enter key (1), Volume keys (2), Physical keyboard (13). Also read the KDoc for KeyboardSettings, SuggestionStripSettings, GestureSettings, TextEditingSettings, LayoutBehaviorSettings, OtpSettings, HardwareKeyboardSettings and SuggestionHotkeyMode, plus app/src/main/res/values/strings_typing.xml for the drawn titles and info text. Docs read in full: reference/settings/typing.mdx, smart/autocorrect.mdx, smart/suggestions.mdx, smart/chips.mdx, smart/grammar.mdx, smart/spell-checker.mdx, reference/gestures.mdx, plus the Options section of typing/glide-typing.mdx. NOT covered: the contents of the four sub-screens reached by nav rows (Personal dictionary, Custom dictionaries, Suggestion blacklist, Tool shortcuts list) — I judged only the nav rows themselves; the first three belong to the dictionaries domain. Grammar check and the system spell checker are documented in my docs list but their settings live on the Tools / Grammar check screen, outside my source range, so I audited those two pages only for claims about typing and found none stale. The One-time codes group sits in my source range but overlaps the clipboard/OTP feature area, so another agent may also report it._

### Dictionaries, personal words, blacklist, phone formats, snippets

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Personal dictionary | `-` | nav | empty list | keep | anyone who taught the keyboard a typo and wants it gone | ok |  | Only place to undo learning. Backed by learning/user_lexicon.json; every edit bumps lexiconVersion so the live IME reloads. |
| Custom dictionaries | `-` | nav | no lists | keep | speakers of the ~330 languages with no bundled word list | ok |  | CustomDictionaries KDoc: only en/bn ship a list; imports are the only completions other languages get. |
| Suggestion blacklist | `suggestionBlacklist` | nav | empty set | keep | user tired of one specific word being offered/corrected to | ok |  | Empty default is right — nothing should be withheld until asked. Subtitle counts entries. |
| Suggest contact names | `contactSuggestions` | toggle | off | keep | people who type friends' names constantly in chat apps | ok |  | Needs READ_CONTACTS; off is the only defensible default for address-book access. Gated by a prominent disclosure. |
| Suggest contact emails | `contactEmailSuggestions` | toggle | off | merge | people who type addresses into web forms | ok |  | Same permission, same source, same disclosure as contact names. One 'use my contacts' toggle plus the email sub-row would do. |
| Contact emails in email fields too | `contactEmailSuggestionsInEmailFields` | toggle | on | AUTOMATE | nobody obvious — the parent toggle already says yes | ok |  | KDoc: email fields keep no composing buffer, so this is the one place the feature matters. Turning it off guts the parent. |
| Suggest app names | `appNameSuggestions` | toggle | off | demote | users who search or reference apps from a text field | ok |  | No permission, ranked below dictionary words, so off costs nothing. Narrow enough to belong under an advanced group. |
| Add word | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user teaching a name before typing it the first time | - |  | UserLexicon.addWord boost 200 makes it compete immediately and exempt from autocorrect. Silently no-ops past 32 chars. |
| Delete word (per row) | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user removing a learned typo that now blocks autocorrect | - |  | forget() also strips the word from bigrams/trigrams. No confirmation, but a single word is cheap to re-add. |
| Get more dictionaries (addon store) | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | users who would never find a word list on their own | - |  | AddonStoreGroup(AddonType.Dictionary); the discovery path for a screen that otherwise demands you supply a file. |
| Import word list | `-` | action | n/a | keep | anyone with a Hunspell .dic or frequency list | - |  | Per enabled language. 32 MiB cap; a file that parses to zero words is deleted again and reported. |
| From URL | `-` | action | n/a | demote | users pasting a link instead of downloading then picking a file | - |  | Saves two steps, http/https only. Duplicate of Import for most people; fine as the secondary button it already is. |
| Delete word list (per row) | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user who imported a junk list and wants it out of suggestions | - |  | Only way to undo an import; bumps customDictVersion so the IME re-reads. |
| Add word (blacklist) | `suggestionBlacklist` | action | n/a | keep | user blocking one recurring wrong correction | - |  | Stored trimmed + lowercased so spellings cannot duplicate. Only entry point — no long-press-from-strip path exists. |
| Delete word (blacklist row) | `suggestionBlacklist` | action | n/a | keep | user un-blocking a word they now want suggested | - |  | Immediate effect; no bulk clear exists alongside it. |
| Add format | `clipboard.phoneFormats` | action | n/a | keep | anyone whose invoice totals keep wearing a phone chip | - |  | Dialog takes a real number and derives the mask (phoneMaskFrom → PhoneFormats.fromExample), which is the only usable way to author one. |
| Delete format (per row) | `clipboard.phoneFormats` | action | n/a | keep | user who moved country | - |  | Deleting the last mask restores detect-every-number, so it doubles as this screen's reset. |
| Try a number | `-` | text | empty | keep | user checking a mask before trusting it with their clips | - |  | A mask is a promise about numbers not visible from this screen; the live matcher is what makes it verifiable. |
| Phone number formats | `clipboard.phoneFormats` | nav | empty set | keep | clipboard-chip users drowning in false phone chips | WRONG → seed one mask from the SIM/locale dial code on first run |  | KDoc admits empty is 'right for someone who copies numbers from three countries and wrong for everyone else'. |
| Get more snippets (addon store) | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | users who want canned replies without writing them | - |  | AddonStoreGroup(AddonType.Snippets); packs ship confirm=true so they offer rather than rewrite. |
| Add snippet | `-` | action | n/a | keep | anyone retyping an address or sign-off | - |  | The feature's only authoring path. |
| Import snippets | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user moving to a new phone or sharing a pack | - |  | Always adds alongside with fresh ids; repairs bad rows and reports what it changed. |
| Export snippets | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user backing up handwritten snippets | - |  | Disabled while the list is empty. .wmsettings.json deliberately excludes snippets, so this is the only per-feature export. |
| Edit snippet (per row) | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user fixing a typo in a saved template | - |  | Reopens the same dialog pre-filled; hands back a whole Snippet so new fields cannot be dropped. |
| Delete snippet (per row) | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user pruning a snippet pack | - |  | Fires immediately with no confirm and no undo, next to the edit pencil — the one destructive tap in this domain worth guarding. |
| Label | `-` | text | empty | keep | everyone — it is the panel card's headline | - |  | Required (Save disabled while blank); the panel has no search, so the label is how a snippet is found. |
| Snippet text | `-` | text | empty | keep | everyone | - |  | The snippet itself. Required; supports the 18 template variables plus {date:pattern}. |
| Word / Pattern | `-` | choice | Word | keep | users who want one snippet to cover every name or time | ok |  | Word is the cheap, exact case and what most people want; Pattern is opt-in and costs the regex machinery. |
| Trigger word | `-` | text | empty | keep | users who want omw to expand without opening a panel | ok |  | Optional — blank means panel-tap only, which is right: silent rewriting has to be asked for. |
| Pattern | `-` | text | empty | keep | power users writing capture-based expansions | - |  | Validated live (backrefs and nested quantifiers refused), with chips for (.+)/$1/^/$ and the regex engine's own complaint shown. |
| Words to match | `-` | choice | 3 | keep | user whose pattern must reach a longer phrase | ok |  | SnippetMatcher.DEFAULT_WORDS=3, MAX_WORDS=8. Docs justify 3: names are short, so a sentence is left alone. |
| Try it (pattern tester) | `-` | text | empty | keep | anyone who wrote a pattern that silently never fires | - |  | Settings is a different process, so a runaway pattern costs a moment here instead of a dropped keystroke. |
| Ask before it expands | `-` | toggle | off | keep | users of downloaded reply packs who don't want silent rewrites | ok |  | Off suits snippets you wrote yourself; packs ship it on. On also moves patterns to the per-keystroke path, so off is the cheap default. |
| Learn from typing | `learnFromTyping` | toggle | on | keep | everyone — it is what makes the personal dictionary exist | ok |  | All data stays on device; incognito and structured-field gates already cover the sensitive cases. |
| Add words to the system dictionary | `addWordsToSystemDictionary` | toggle | off | demote | users who also run another keyboard or the system spell checker | ok |  | KDoc: 'the on-device lexicon already covers this keyboard'. Exports words outside the app, so off is right. |
| Expand dictionary shortcuts | `suggestionStrip.expandUserDictShortcuts` | toggle | off | demote | users migrating with omw-style entries already in Android's list | ok |  | Largely superseded by snippets; a migration convenience rather than a feature, and it reads a system provider. |
| Clear learned words | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user handing the phone over or resetting bad learning | - |  | Deletes lexicon, emoji usage, correction stats and CJK history. No confirm, and unlike the per-word edits it does not bump lexiconVersion. |

**Missing here:** Learn a word only after N uses (One typo committed once is permanently exempt from autocorrect; users have no way to raise the bar); Seed a phone format from the device region (Empty means every 7-15 digit run becomes a chip — the exact false positive the screen exists to fix); Clear the whole blacklist (Only per-row deletion, and it is DataStore-backed so the Storage screen offers no bulk clear either); Reorder or pin snippets (Panel and list both use creation order, and the panel has no search, so common snippets sink); Search / filter the personal dictionary (Up to 10,000 words render as one flat count-sorted list; finding a word to delete is impractical); Disable a custom word list without deleting it (Testing whether a bad import is polluting suggestions costs a delete and a re-import); Show languages that have lists on disk but are switched off (Lists for a disabled language become unmanageable here; the twin emoji-keyword screen already handles this case)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/smart/personal-dictionary.mdx` — wrong: Says Add word 'sets a new word's weight to exactly 200' (also reference/settings/dictionaries.mdx line 17). UserLexicon.addWord ADDS 200 to the existing count, so adding a word already typed 50 times yields 250.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/snippets.mdx` — wrong: {timezone} documented as 'e.g. GMT+06:00'. SnippetVariable.TIMEZONE formats with "zzzz", the long zone name (e.g. 'Bangladesh Standard Time'); the GMT-offset form only appears as a fallback.
- `MISSING: phone number formats reference` — gap: No reference/settings page. One paragraph in tools/clipboard.mdx:68 is all there is; mask syntax (X wildcards, pinning a digit, the 4-15 digit bounds, trunk-0 and dial-code leeway, why +XX degrades to a national mask) and the live tester are undocumented.
- `docs/src/content/docs/smart/personal-dictionary.mdx` — gap: No mention of the 32-character word-length limit. Both learnWord and addWord silently drop longer words, so Add word appears to succeed and nothing is added.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/dictionaries.mdx` — gap: No mention of the 10,000-word cap, the 9,000 eviction target or the half-life decay in UserLexicon.compactIfNeeded. A user whose old words vanished has nothing to read.
- `docs/src/content/docs/languages/dictionaries.mdx` — stale: Lines 85-86 give the paths as 'Typing / Custom dictionaries' and 'Typing / Personal dictionary'; reference/settings/dictionaries.mdx gives the same rows as 'Typing / Suggestions / …'. Both rows are in the Suggestions group.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/snippets.mdx` — stale: Line 119 notes delete has no confirmation but the page never warns that the trash sits immediately beside the edit pencil on every row, which is where the misfire comes from.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:12071 — Privacy › Clear learned words (deletes user_lexicon, emoji_usage, correction_stats, cjk_history)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/storage/StorageCategories.kt:604 — Storage › Learned words: clears learning/ and bumps lexiconVersion so the live IME re-reads`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/storage/StorageCategories.kt:654 — Storage › Snippets: clears snippets/`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/storage/StorageCategories.kt:680 — Storage › Custom word lists: per-file and clear-all, manageRoute customdictionaries`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:6249 — per-word delete, personal dictionary`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:6328 — per-word delete, blacklist`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:6417 — per-format delete; removing the last mask restores detect-every-number (SettingsRepository.kt:7307)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:7771 — per-list delete, custom dictionaries (bumps customDictVersion)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:12343 — per-snippet delete`

_Coverage: Read in full: MainActivity.kt DictionarySettings, BlacklistSettings, PhoneFormatSettings + phoneMaskFrom, CustomDictionarySettings, SnippetSettings, SnippetDialog, SnippetPatternFields; the Typing screen's Suggestions group (contact/app-name toggles and the three nav rows); the Privacy screen's on-device-learning group and Clear learned words; the clipboard tool-detail nav row to phoneformats. Also read SettingsRepository KDoc for lexiconVersion, customDictVersion, contactSuggestions, contactEmailSuggestions(+InEmailFields), appNameSuggestions, suggestionBlacklist, learnFromTyping, addWordsToSystemDictionary, clipboard.phoneFormats; plus UserLexicon.kt, CustomDictionaries.kt, SnippetStore.kt, SnippetMatcher.kt, PhoneFormats.kt, SnippetFile limits, SettingsBackup, StorageCategories. Read all six assigned docs pages plus tools/snippets.mdx and tools/clipboard.mdx (which is where phone formats are actually documented). Did a second pass over all three source ranges. NOT covered: the emoji-keyword screen (EmojiKeywordSettings, immediately after CustomDictionarySettings — treated as the emoji domain); the Snippets tool's own Enabled / Icon colour / Keyword shortcut rows in tool detail (tools domain); the downloadable-wordlist rows on each language screen (languages domain); Backup & restore's Dictionary/Snippets sections (backup domain). Three entries — Learn from typing, Add words to the system dictionary, Expand dictionary shortcuts — are drawn on the Privacy screen and may overlap with the privacy agent; included because they are the personal-dictionary story. MainActivity.kt was being edited concurrently during this audit, so cited line numbers were taken in one final pass and may drift by tens of lines._

### Key press: haptics, sound, popups, repeat, long press

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Key sound | `keySound` | toggle | Off | keep | typists who want audible confirmation; kids, learners | ok |  | Off matches stock and never surprises a quiet room. The wizard already offers it with a live preview, so discovery is handled. |
| Sound style | `keySoundStyle` | choice | Click | keep | anyone who dislikes the stock tick; mech-keyboard fans | ok |  | Seven chips, each previews on tap. Click is the device's own key tick, so it matches what the phone already sounds like. |
| Installed sounds (Custom only) | `keySoundCustom.customId` | list | none installed | keep | users who installed a sound addon or own clip | - |  | Library, not a preference. Only drawn under Custom, since the list has no effect until that style is picked. |
| Import a sound file (.mp3) | `-` | action | - | keep | users with their own click recording | - |  | Only route for a local file. Label says .mp3 but the picker also accepts .ogg and .wav (SoundFile.IMPORT_MIME_TYPES). |
| Sound add-ons store row | `-` | nav | - | keep | users with no file of their own to import | - |  | The store is where most sounds actually come from; without it the empty library has no remedy in reach. |
| Installed sound packs (Pack only) | `keySoundCustom.packId` | list | none installed | keep | mechanical-switch pack users | - |  | Row shows variant count and roles, and tapping plays a whole keystroke, so the pack is judged on both halves. |
| Import a sound pack (.wmsoundpack) | `-` | action | - | keep | users sideloading a monkeytype-style pack | - |  | No usable MIME narrowing for a ZIP with a custom extension, so the manifest inside is the real check. |
| Sound pack add-ons store row | `-` | nav | - | keep | pack users; the shipped repo carries 26 packs | - |  | Same pairing as single sounds: a store row beside the importer so an empty list is not a dead end. |
| Key release sound | `keySoundCustom.playRelease` | toggle | On | keep | pack users who want the switch returning | ok |  | Row hides itself unless the selected pack recorded key-ups, so it can never read as a broken switch. KDoc: honour what the pack recorded. |
| Sound volume | `keySoundVolume` | slider | 50% | keep | anyone whose media volume is set loud | ok |  | Relative to media volume, so half is a safe landing point. Floor is 5%, not 0 — the master toggle owns silence. |
| Key press haptics | `hapticFeedback` | toggle | On | keep | most typists; the main feedback channel | split | yes | Genuinely split taste, highest-impact feedback switch, one tap with an immediate buzz. The wizard already asks it on the feedback page. |
| Haptic style | `hapticStyle` | choice | Tap (wizard writes Key) | keep | users on OEMs whose keyboard waveform differs from the generic one | WRONG → SYSTEM_TAP — stop the wizard overwriting it |  | Onboarding seeds HapticPlayer.bestSupportedStyle, which returns only SYSTEM_KEY or HEAVY_CLICK, so the KDoc's measured SYSTEM_TAP default never ships. |
| Haptic strength | `hapticStrengthMs` | slider | 15 ms | keep | Custom-style users on motors without amplitude control | ok |  | Only drawn under Custom, the one path where pulse length is the lever that bites; predefined effects are HAL-fixed. |
| Haptic intensity | `hapticAmplitude` | slider | 255 (100%) | keep | Sharp/Custom users who want a softer buzz | ok |  | Full drive by default because the OS haptic-intensity setting already scales it down; shown only for Custom and Sharp. |
| Press and hold haptics | `hapticOnLongPress` | toggle | On | keep | everyone using accent popups; signals the finger can lift | ok |  | The second buzz is what tells you the popup opened without looking. Delete and space are excluded, so no double-buzz. |
| Press and hold release haptics | `hapticOnLongPressRelease` | toggle | Off | demote | very few; people wanting a full press-hold-release loop | ok |  | A third vibration in one gesture. Off is right and almost nobody turns it on; belongs behind an advanced disclosure. |
| Vibrate on space | `feedback.vibrateOnSpace` | toggle | On | demote | thumb typists bothered by the big key buzzing | ok |  | Narrowest of the four per-event gates. Real but rare taste; fold the four into one 'Vibrate on…' sub-row. |
| Vibrate on delete swipe | `feedback.vibrateOnDeleteSwipe` | toggle | On | keep | anyone clearing a sentence with one backspace pull | ok |  | Per-word buzzing during a long pull reads as a buzz-saw; on by default is right because the words-gone count is otherwise invisible. |
| Vibrate on key repeat | `feedback.vibrateOnRepeat` | toggle | On | keep | anyone holding backspace; stops ~20 buzzes a second | ok |  | Highest-value of the per-event gates: at the 50 ms default a held backspace fires twenty vibrations per second. |
| Mute haptics in Do Not Disturb | `feedback.hapticsRespectDnd` | toggle | Off | keep | night and meeting users wanting a fully silent phone | ok |  | KDoc: DND targets notifications, not touch feedback, so following the platform is correct; opting in is one switch. |
| Key popup | `popup.enabled` | toggle | On | keep | most typists, and anyone whose finger hides the key | ok |  | Stock behaviour and clearly majority. The wizard asks it too, but the split is not close enough to be worth the attention. |
| Popup on number pads | `popup.inNumericFields` | toggle | Off | keep | anyone typing a PIN with someone beside them | ok |  | KDoc: on a PIN pad the floating digit is noise at best and shoulder-surfable at worst. |
| Minimum popup duration | `popup.minDurationMs` | slider | 140 ms | demote | fast typists who want the bubble to linger | ok |  | A comfort floor almost nobody hunts for. Keep it, but below a 'more' disclosure with the other popup timing. |
| Maximum popup duration | `popup.maxDurationMs` | slider | 750 ms | REMOVE | nobody | ok |  | KDoc calls it a stuck-bubble backstop, not a comfort knob, and the info text says never lower it. Make it a constant. |
| Popup on key | `popup.onKey` | toggle | On | keep | people who prefer a small bubble above the fingertip | ok |  | Real two-way visual taste and cheap to flip; On is the stock look. A theme may override it (KbTheme.popupOnKey). |
| Popup font size | `popup.fontScale` | slider | ×1.00 | demote | low-vision users wanting a bigger preview glyph | ok |  | Cosmetic tuning on top of a bubble that already scales with height; belongs with the other popup appearance knobs. |
| Popup height | `popup.heightDp / popup.floatingHeightDp` | slider | 110 dp on-key, 65 dp floating | demote | users whose fingertip covers a short bubble | ok |  | One slider writes whichever style is active, so each keeps its own tuned value; a theme can override the height outright. |
| Popup shape | `popup.shape` | nav | Rounded | demote | theme tinkerers matching popups to key shape | ok |  | Governs every popup surface, not just the bubble, and a theme override wins over it. Pure appearance. |
| Popup corner radius | `popup.cornerRadiusDp` | slider | 12 dp | merge | theme tinkerers, and only on rounded or cut shapes | ok |  | Inert for four of the six shapes. Belongs inside the shape picker, which is already handed radiusDp for its preview. |
| Press and hold delay | `longPressDelayMs` | slider | 300 ms | keep | tremor users, and fast typists opening popups by accident | ok |  | Below the platform's 400 ms, right for a keyboard. Note the Accessibility copy runs to 800 ms; values above 700 are unreachable here. |
| Delete repeat interval | `keyRepeat.deleteMs` | slider | 50 ms | keep | anyone clearing a line in one hold | ok |  | 20 deletes a second matches stock keyboards, and the loop stops itself once the field is empty. |
| Space repeat interval | `keyRepeat.spaceMs` | slider | 50 ms | keep | few; people who hold space and hate undoing runaway spaces | WRONG → 100 ms |  | The KDoc splits the two keys because a runaway spacebar has to be undone, then ships delete's own cadence anyway. |
| Caps lock double press | `layoutBehavior.shiftCapsLockMs` | slider | 350 ms | keep | slow double-tappers, and people locking caps by accident | ok |  | Just above the platform's 300 ms double-tap window; KDoc names the trade both ways. |
| Press and hold hints | `longPressHints` | toggle | On | keep | anyone learning where the digits and symbols hide | ok |  | The hint is the only discovery path for the alternates; keys running a hold shortcut correctly show none. |
| All accents on press and hold | `layoutBehavior.showAllPopupKeys` | toggle | Off | keep | multilingual Latin typists staying on one layout | ok |  | KDoc: the built-in popups are short on purpose and the full set is a wall of glyphs most people never want. |
| Press and hold ?123 for the number pad | `layoutBehavior.symbolsLongPressNumpad` | toggle | On | keep | anyone entering numbers inside a normal text field | ok |  | KDoc: the keypad is otherwise buried in the toolbox, and a held ?123 costs nothing when it is not wanted. |
| Currency keys | `layoutBehavior.currencyKeys` | text | ৳ € £ ¥ ₹ ₿ (stored empty) | keep | users whose currency is not among the shipped six | WRONG → device-locale currency first, then the built-in set |  | A fixed ৳-first order on a 352-language keyboard; the first glyph is also the key's corner hint, so order is visible. |
| Ctrl shortcuts as raw key events | `rawClipboardShortcuts` | toggle | Off | demote | Termux users with a Ctrl key on a custom layout | ok |  | Only bites on a custom layout carrying Ctrl. EditorInfo cannot spot a terminal, so it stays a switch — but a rare one. |
| Press and hold A to select all | `longPressLetterActions.selectAll` | toggle | Off | merge | power users wanting clipboard actions without a Ctrl key | ok |  | Six near-identical rows; make one multi-select. Bound to the literal letter 'a', so it does nothing on non-Latin layouts. |
| Press and hold C to copy | `longPressLetterActions.copy` | toggle | Off | merge | power users copying a whole field in one hold | ok |  | Same row family as Hold A. Replaces C's accent popup outright, which is the trade the info text names. |
| Press and hold X to cut | `longPressLetterActions.cut` | toggle | Off | merge | power users cutting a field in one hold | ok |  | Same row family; falls back to select-all when nothing is selected, like Hold C. |
| Press and hold V to paste | `longPressLetterActions.paste` | toggle | Off | merge | power users pasting without opening the clipboard tool | ok |  | Same row family. Most useful of the six, since the clipboard tool is otherwise a toolbar trip. |
| Press and hold Z to undo | `longPressLetterActions.undo` | toggle | Off | merge | power users undoing without the toolbar Undo tool | ok |  | Same row family; sends the same shortcut the Undo tool does, so it duplicates an existing surface. |
| Press and hold Y to redo | `longPressLetterActions.redo` | toggle | Off | merge | power users redoing without the toolbar Redo tool | ok |  | Same row family, and the rarest of the six. Y carries no accents on most layouts, so the trade is cheap. |

**Missing here:** Key repeat start delay (Repeat starts after longPressDelayMs, so slowing accents for tremor also delays backspace repeat.); Sound on key repeat (vibrateOnRepeat gates the buzz, but the click still machine-guns on every held backspace with no gate.); Which letters carry hold shortcuts (Hardcoded to literal a/c/v/x/z/y, so all six toggles silently do nothing on non-Latin layouts.); Backspace swipe word distance (72 dp for the first word is a long pull on a small phone and a twitch on a tablet.); Follow the system touch-vibration setting (FLAG_IGNORE_GLOBAL_SETTING means turning off system touch vibration does not quiet the keyboard.)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` — wrong: Timing table still lists one "Key repeat interval"; the screen has two sliders, Delete repeat interval and Space repeat interval (both 20-200 ms, 50 ms).
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` — wrong: "Hold ?123 for numpad" documented as default Off; LayoutBehaviorSettings.symbolsLongPressNumpad defaults to true.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` — wrong: Minimum popup duration default given as 150 ms; KeyPopupSettings.minDurationMs is 140.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` — wrong: Sound style listed as six options; there are seven — the Sound pack chip, the installed-packs list with its own importer, and the Key release sound toggle are all absent.
- `docs/src/content/docs/typing/feedback.mdx` — wrong: Options table gives Haptic style default "Key"; the stored default is Tap (SYSTEM_TAP). The wizard does write Key, which the page never explains.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` — gap: Key popup table omits the Popup shape nav row (default Rounded) and the Popup corner radius slider (0-40 dp, 12 dp).
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` — stale: Search-index bullet is out of date: Key sound, Sound volume, Maximum popup duration and Hold ?123 are indexed now. Only Haptic style, Sound style and Key release sound are missing.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` — stale: Row and group names still read "Long-press …"; the UI strings now say "Press and hold …" (group title "Press and hold shortcuts").
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keypress.mdx` — gap: Popup height documented as one 110 dp value; the setting stores a separate height per bubble style (110 on-key, 65 floating) and a theme can override it.
- `MISSING: per-row reset affordance` — gap: No page documents the Restore icon that appears on a changed row (ResetSetting), now wired to 34 of this screen's rows. Users have no way to learn it exists.
