_Coverage: Covered all 44 user-visible entries in the domain: KeySoundGroup, InstalledSoundSection, InstalledSoundPackSection, KeyReleaseSoundToggle and KeyPressSettings (all four groups). Read the KDoc for KeyPopupSettings, KeyRepeatSettings, KeySoundSettings, FeedbackSettings, LongPressLetterActions, HapticStyle, KeySoundStyle and the haptic/sound/timing fields of KeyboardSettings, plus HapticPlayer.kt, KeySoundPlayer.kt, the IME call sites in KeyboardScreen.kt and WMKeyboardService.kt, Onboarding.kt/OnboardingPages.kt (feedback page and haptic seeding), SettingsSearch.kt (index), strings_keypress.xml, strings_hardware.xml, keypress.mdx and typing/feedback.mdx. IMPORTANT: MainActivity.kt was edited by someone else mid-audit — line numbers shifted by about 118 and `default = SettingsDefaults.*` reset wiring was added to 34 rows. Every line number above comes from the re-read at the end, and the entry list was verified twice against the current file. Not covered: the Sound and haptics toolbar panel's own compact controls (ToolPanels.kt:1990+) beyond confirming they write the same fields; the power-saving overrides that force haptics/sound/popup off (another agent's domain); the Accessibility copy of long-press delay (read only to confirm the duplicate and its wider 150-800 range). Nothing was verified on a device._

### Appearance, fonts, icon packs

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Keyboard themes | `keyboardThemeId` | nav | Default | keep | everyone; the look is the first thing people change | - |  | Hub row into the gallery; trailing value names the active theme. |
| Keyboard font | `keyFontId` | nav | System default | keep | users who dislike the stock face | - |  | Hub row; value comes from KeyboardFonts.genericDisplayName. |
| Icons | `icons.activePackId` | nav | Default | keep | icon-pack installers and single-glyph tweakers | - |  | Value shows pack name, or a count of changed icons. |
| Key corner radius | `keyCornerRadiusDp` | slider | 8 dp | keep | people who want square or pill-shaped keys | ok |  | 0-28 dp; theme editor seeds its per-theme radius from this value. |
| Key label font size | `fontScale` | slider | x1.0 | keep | low-vision typists and small-screen users | ok |  | 0.7-1.5x; also surfaced as a percentage on Accessibility, same field. |
| Key hint font size | `layoutBehavior.hintFontScale` | slider | x1.0 | demote | users who read long-press hints and find 10sp too small | ok |  | KDoc: 1.0 keeps the 10sp base. Not gated on longPressHints, so it shows when hints are off. |
| Show the toolbar | `toolbarBehavior.enabled` | toggle | On | keep | users who want every pixel for keys | ok |  | KDoc: off hides suggestions and every pinned tool; guarded by a confirm dialog. |
| Swipe down to hide | `toolbarBehavior.swipeDownHide` | toggle | Off | keep | one-handed users who dismiss the keyboard constantly | ok |  | KDoc: off so the gesture never surprises anyone reordering or scrolling the bar. |
| Only toolbar with hardware keyboard | `toolbarBehavior.onlyWithHardwareKeyboard` | toggle | Off | keep | tablet users with a Bluetooth keyboard attached | ok |  | KDoc: off keeps the platform's usual behaviour. Real need, narrow audience. |
| Reverse order for RTL languages | `toolbarBehavior.reverseForRtl` | toggle | On | demote | few; an RTL typist with muscle memory for fixed tool spots | ok |  | Does nothing unless an RTL layout is enabled, yet the row is drawn for everyone. |
| Spread tools across the bar | `toolbarBehavior.greedy` | toggle | On | merge | users who pin two or three tools and want them even | ok |  | Interacts silently with Scroll the toolbar; one 3-way Spread/Pack/Scroll choice is honest. |
| Toolbar height | `toolbarHeightDp` | slider | 44 dp | keep | anyone trading strip height for key height | ok |  | 32-80 dp; also sets the suggestion row height, and labels need the extra room. |
| Scroll the toolbar | `toolbarBehavior.scrollable` | toggle | Off | merge | users who pin more tools than fit at a tappable size | ok |  | KDoc: forces the packed layout, overriding Spread. Merge both into one layout choice. |
| Hide toolbar & clipboard on lock screen | `toolbarBehavior.hideWhenLocked` | toggle | Off | keep | people who copy OTPs and hand their phone around | ok |  | KDoc: off so the keyboard looks the same locked or not. Privacy opt-in, not a majority default. |
| Tool labels | `toolbarLabels` | toggle | Off | keep | new users who can't tell one tool icon from another | ok |  | Costs vertical space in a 44dp strip, so off is right until asked for. |
| Label text size | `toolbarLabelSize` | slider | 10 sp | REMOVE | nobody; 7-14sp micro-tuning under an off-by-default toggle | ok |  | KDoc only justifies 10 over 9. Derive it from toolbar height instead of exposing 8 steps. |
| Reset pinned tools | `toolbarTools` | action | Emoji, Clipboard, Settings | keep | anyone who over-pinned and wants out | - |  | Confirms first; resets the global row only, never a mode's own pins. |
| Tool circle radius | `toolCircleRadiusDp` | slider | 20 dp | keep | users choosing between Gboard circles and bare icons | ok |  | 20 with the 38dp width is a full circle; 0 means no background at all. |
| Tool background shape | `toolShape` | nav | Rounded | demote | theme tinkerers building a matched look | ok |  | 12 shapes for a 38dp chip. Already hidden while the radius is 0; a theme can override it. |
| Tool width | `toolbarBehavior.toolWidthDp` | slider | 38 dp | demote | users building a pill-style Gboard strip | ok |  | KDoc: 38 is the classic circle; wider only reads as a pill with a low radius plus scrolling. |
| Toolbox layout | `toolbox.layout` | choice | Icons | keep | users who need readable tool names, not captions | ok |  | Icons fits more per screen; Pills is the legibility option. Real fork, cheap either way. |
| Toolbox grid size | `toolboxColumns` | slider | 4 per row | keep | users with many tools, and large-phone owners | ok |  | 3-6; only shown while the layout is Icons. |
| Pills per row | `toolbox.pillColumns` | slider | 2 per row | demote | pill users on wide or folded screens | ok |  | 1-3, only under the Pills layout. Two levels deep for a layout most never pick. |
| Fill pills with the tool color | `toolbox.pillFilled` | toggle | Off | demote | people who like a colourful panel | ok |  | KDoc: dead unless coloredToolIcons is on, but the UI doesn't gate on it, only on Pills. |
| Swipe the toolbox in pages | `toolbox.paginate` | toggle | Off | keep | users with 20+ tools who lose their place scrolling | ok |  | Scrolling grid is the safer default; paging is a deliberate preference. |
| Tools per page | `toolbox.pageSize` | slider | 12 per page | AUTOMATE | nobody; a page should hold what fits the panel | ok |  | 4-40, three levels deep behind an off-by-default toggle. Compute from panel height and columns. |
| Get more fonts | `-` | nav | - | keep | users wanting faces beyond the 20 curated ones | - |  | Addon-store row at the top of the screen; undocumented. |
| English font | `keyFontId` | list | System default | keep | anyone who wants different key labels; also drives Cyrillic and Greek | ok |  | 20 Google faces plus library; each row previews in its own face. Rows vanish without the GMS font provider. |
| Per-script font (one section per enabled script) | `scriptFontIds` | list | Automatic (Noto) | keep | Bengali, Devanagari, Thai, CJK typists whose Noto face reads poorly | ok |  | 22 script sections, each shown only while a language using it is enabled. |
| Installed fonts (library list + delete) | `keyFontId / scriptFontIds` | list | empty | keep | users who imported or installed their own face | - |  | Own section above the curated list; delete clears the selection first so nothing points at a dead file. |
| Import font file (.ttf / .otf) | `customFontName` | action | - | keep | users with a licensed or branded font file | - |  | Only the English and Bengali sections offer it; imports land in the shared library. |
| Icon pack | `icons.activePackId` | list | Built-in icons | keep | users reskinning the whole glyph set at once | ok |  | Built-in row always first; each installed row carries export and delete. |
| Export icon pack | `-` | action | - | keep | pack authors turning My icons into a shareable file | - |  | Per-row button; writes a .wmicons ZIP through the system picker. |
| Delete icon pack | `-` | action | - | keep | anyone reclaiming space or dropping a pack | - |  | Confirms first, then forgets any override pinned to that pack. |
| Get more icon packs | `-` | nav | - | keep | users who want packs without hunting for files | - |  | Addon-store row beside the file importer; missing from the docs. |
| Import an icon pack | `-` | action | - | keep | users handed a .wmicons file directly | - |  | Switches to the pack on success, since installing and not using it isn't the intent. |
| Reset all icons | `icons.overrides` | action | - | keep | anyone who lost track of what they changed | - |  | Runs with no confirmation, unlike deleting a pack. Keeps installed packs. |
| Per-slot icon (93 slots, 4 groups) | `icons.overrides` | list | Default | keep | users who want a recognizable shift, enter or tool glyph | - |  | Row subtitle names the source; picker offers 181 built-ins, own SVG, or Use default. |
| Mode (System / Light / Dark / Amoled) | `themeMode` | choice | System | keep | everyone, AMOLED owners most | ok | yes | Genuinely split preference, one tap to answer, and the onboarding wizard already asks it. |
| Material You colors | `dynamicColor` | toggle | On | keep | Android 12+ users who theme their phone wallpaper | ok |  | Gated on API 31 in code but the row is still drawn below it, where it does nothing. |
| Theme gallery style (Auto / Grouped / Flat) | `appUi.themeGalleryStyle` | choice | Auto | demote | few; Auto already picks from the persona quiz | ok |  | Settings-app cosmetics only; AUTO resolves from PersonaDepth, so most users never need the row. |

**Missing here:** Suggestion strip text size (Key labels scale 0.7-1.5x but suggestion words are pinned at 16sp; low-vision users cannot enlarge them.); Toolbox tool label size (Toolbar labels get a 7-14sp slider; the toolbox captions are hard-coded at 12.5sp.); Installed-fonts list for the other 20 script sections (A user who installs a Devanagari or Thai font has no picker anywhere to select it.); Reset Appearance to defaults (Nine sliders on this screen with no way back; only pinned tools and icons have a reset.); CJK candidate text size (Candidate row is fixed at 20sp; CJK users needing bigger or denser candidates cannot change it.)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/themes/fonts.mdx` — wrong: Lines 19 and 82 say a missing Play Services font provider makes a Google Font row fall back silently to the system face. The code removes every Google Font row (MainActivity.kt:8153) and shows an explanatory paragraph instead (MainActivity.kt:8039-8046).
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/fonts.mdx` — wrong: Line 43 repeats the same wrong claim about Play Services being missing; the rows are hidden, not silently substituted.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/appearance.mdx` — wrong: Tool background shape row says "the same eleven shapes as Key shape"; KeyShapeKind has 12 entries and the picker iterates all of them.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/icons.mdx` — stale: "Two ungrouped rows sit below the pack list" — there are three: the addon-store row "Get more icon packs" (IconScreens.kt:253) is undocumented.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/fonts.mdx` — gap: The addon-store group at the top of the Fonts screen (MainActivity.kt:8047) is never described; only the reverse deep-link from Addons is.
- `MISSING: Theme gallery style (Auto / Grouped / Flat)` — gap: appUi.themeGalleryStyle is a visible ChoiceControl on the themes screen (ThemeScreens.kt:875-879) and appears in no docs page; reference/settings/themes.mdx documents Mode and Material You but not this.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:4797 — Reset pinned tools row (impl at MainActivity.kt:2277, confirms then writes DefaultToolbarTools)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/IconScreens.kt:263 — Reset all icons: clearIconOverrides plus turns the active pack off, no confirmation`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/IconScreens.kt:574 — per-slot "Use default" in the icon picker; also deletes the slot's SVG from My icons`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/IconScreens.kt:201 — "Built-in icons" row, the reset-to-default for the active pack`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:8176 — the "System default" / "Automatic (Noto)" row in every font picker section`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:478 — "Automatic" row in KeyShapePickerDialog (only passed by the theme editor, not by the tool-shape picker)`

_Coverage: Read in full: MainActivity.kt 4570-4925 (AppearanceSettings, all three groups plus the disable-toolbar dialog) and 7940-8330 (FontSettings, FontPickerSection, FontChoiceRow, import helpers); IconScreens.kt entire file; ThemeScreens.kt 690-890 for themeMode, dynamicColor and appUi.themeGalleryStyle, which were on my field list but live on the themes screen. Read the KDoc for every field named: KeyboardSettings 882-1000 (themeMode, dynamicColor, keyCornerRadiusDp, fontScale, keyFontId, scriptFontIds, customScriptFontNames), 1255-1330 (toolbar fields, icons), 1464 (toolboxColumns), ToolbarBehavior 578-640, ToolboxSettings 655-703, IconSettings 2639-2666, LayoutBehaviorSettings hintFontScale 2699-2704, AppUiSettings 2302-2330. Cross-checked KeyboardFonts.kt (20 English faces, 22 script sections, 8 Bengali alternatives, GMS provider gating), IconSlots.kt group counts, BuiltinIcons.kt (verified 181 catalog entries), ThemeSpec.kt KeyShapeKind (12, not 11), ToolbarTool.kt DefaultToolbarTools. Read all five assigned docs pages end to end. NOT covered: the Themes gallery itself and everything in the theme editor (another agent's domain — I only judged the three fields explicitly assigned to me), the Accessibility screen's duplicate font-size and Readable-font shortcuts, and the emoji font picker. I did not run the app, so slider ranges and defaults are read from code, not observed on screen._

### Layout: sizing, split, floating, one-handed, screen variants

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Number row | `numberRow` | toggle | On | keep | anyone typing digits or OTPs without a layer trip | split | yes | KDoc: digits otherwise cost a ?123 trip. But a 6th row is ~18% more screen. Wizard already asks it with a live preview. |
| Number row height | `numberRowHeightDp` | slider | 42 dp (40 small / 38 large tablet) | keep | users wanting digits without a full extra row of height | ok |  | Shorter than the 48 dp keys so the row adds less height; tablet values gated on keyHeightUntouched. |
| Symbols on shift | `layoutBehavior.numberRowShiftSymbols` | toggle | Off | keep | coders reaching = \ < > [ ] { } \| ~ without ?123 | ok |  | Niche, already hidden behind Number row. Off keeps the digit row predictable under shift. |
| Number row in symbols | `layoutBehavior.numberRowInSymbols` | toggle | On | keep | someone who wants a one-row-shorter ?123 layer | ok |  | On swaps the ?123 layer's own digit row for the bracket fill row, so it adds symbols rather than duplicating digits. |
| Go back to the letters | `layoutBehavior.symbolsReturnToLetters` | toggle | Off | keep | anyone who opens ?123 for one full stop | split |  | KDoc names the split: the other half open ?123 to type a whole line of punctuation. Low stakes, found in use, not worth a wizard slot. |
| Characters that go back | `layoutBehavior.symbolsReturnChars` | text | !?.,;: (empty = default set) | demote | user who also wants ) or " to spring back | ok |  | Only reachable with the toggle on. Default is the sentence-ending set; digits deliberately excluded so "12" stays put. |
| Type native digits in | `layoutBehavior.numeralCommitScope` | choice | Text fields | keep | Bengali/Arabic/Hindi typist who still needs parseable phone fields | ok |  | TEXT_ONLY keeps ASCII where the OS parses digits. Global on purpose: it is about fields, not script. |
| Key height | `keyHeightDp` | slider | 48 dp (46 small / 44 large tablet) | keep | everyone; the highest-value sizing control in the app | ok |  | 48 dp matches the Material touch target; tablets start lower because the 6th row is on and width, not height, is what they have. |
| Bottom row height | `layoutBehavior.bottomRowHeightDp` | slider | 0 = Follow keys | keep | user who wants a fatter spacebar without a taller board | ok |  | 0 leaves the row unchanged until asked. Custom layouts with their own row heights ignore it. |
| Side padding | `layoutBehavior.sidePadScale` | slider | 0% | merge | nobody; identical maths to Keyboard width at Centre | ok |  | dockedWidthArrangement folds it into the same widthFraction as keyboardWidthPercent; with position Left/Right all slack goes to one side, so it is not the symmetric padding its KDoc promises. |
| Key spacing | `keyGapScale` | slider | 100% | keep | fat-finger users spreading keys; density fans packing them | ok |  | 1x is the built-in gap. Gap is part of row height, so it also nudges total keyboard height. |
| Bottom padding | `bottomPaddingDp` | slider | 32 dp (Android 15+) / 8 dp | keep | gesture-nav user whose bottom row sits on the nav bar | WRONG → 8 dp on every version |  | KeyboardScreen.kt:1285 already applies navigationBarsPadding(); the 32 dp stacks on top of the bar inset, so A15+ users lose ~56-80 dp under the keys. |
| Keyboard width | `keyboardWidthPercent` | slider | 100% | keep | tablet/foldable owner docking a narrower board | ok |  | Full width is right on a phone; below 100% it reveals the position choice. |
| Keyboard position | `keyboardAlignment` | choice | Centre | keep | one-handed tablet user docking left or right | ok |  | Only drawn below 100% width. Centre is the neutral pick and what most narrowing is for. |
| Landscape (per-screen row) | `sizingOverrides[LANDSCAPE]` | nav | Follows portrait | keep | anyone whose portrait key height is too tall sideways | ok |  | The most-used override. Empty override costs nothing and reads "Follows portrait" until touched. |
| Portrait, unfolded | `sizingOverrides[PORTRAIT_UNFOLDED]` | nav | Follows portrait | keep | foldable or tablet owner | ok |  | "Unfolded" is smallest dimension >= 600 dp, so plain tablets get it too, not just foldables. |
| Landscape, unfolded | `sizingOverrides[LANDSCAPE_UNFOLDED]` | nav | Follows portrait | keep | tablet owner typing sideways | ok |  | Fourth shape; cheap because an unset override stores nothing. |
| Keyboard scale (per screen) | `sizingOverrides[v].keyboardScale` | slider | 100% | merge | foldable owner scaling both heights in one drag | ok |  | Redundant with the Key height + Number row height sliders right below it. Scales neither width nor padding despite the "whole keyboard" label, and the variant summary row ignores it. |
| Key height (per screen) | `sizingOverrides[v].keyHeightDp` | slider | base 48 dp | keep | landscape user who wants shorter keys | ok |  | The main reason the per-screen editor exists; vertical space is the scarce thing in landscape. |
| Number row height (per screen) | `sizingOverrides[v].numberRowHeightDp` | slider | base 42 dp | keep | landscape user squeezing the digit row | ok |  | Hidden unless the number row is on; pairs with the key-height override. |
| Bottom padding (per screen) | `sizingOverrides[v].bottomPaddingDp` | slider | base value | keep | user whose nav bar moves to the side in landscape | ok |  | The gesture-bar inset genuinely differs by orientation, so this one is not cosmetic. |
| Keyboard width (per screen) | `sizingOverrides[v].keyboardWidthPercent` | slider | base 100% | keep | tablet owner narrowing only the landscape board | ok |  | Width is the value most worth differing by screen shape; a full-width tablet landscape board is unreachable. |
| Font size (per screen) | `sizingOverrides[v].fontScale` | slider | base 1.0x | keep | user wanting smaller labels on a scaled-down landscape board | ok |  | Same field as Appearance > Key label font size; owned there, only overridable here. No duplication of the base control. |
| Keyboard position (per screen) | `sizingOverrides[v].keyboardAlignment` | choice | base Centre | keep | tablet owner docking the landscape board to one edge | ok |  | Only drawn when that shape resolves below 100% width, mirroring the base gate. |
| Follow portrait again | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone who over-tuned one screen shape | - |  | The only true reset in this domain. Calls clearVariantSizing and only appears once the override is non-empty. |
| One-handed mode | `oneHandedMode` | choice | Off | merge | thumb-typist on a big phone | ok |  | This is live render state, not a preference; its Left/Right duplicates the per-orientation Side rows below and the two can disagree. Should be on/off, with Side deciding the edge. |
| Portrait width | `oneHanded.portrait.widthPercent` | slider | 78% | keep | thumb-typist tuning reach | ok |  | Capped 40-85 by KDoc so the exit/flip rail always has room on the inner edge. |
| Portrait height | `oneHanded.portrait.heightScale` | slider | 100% | demote | short-thumbed user pulling the top rows down | ok |  | 100% is a no-op default, yet the row is drawn even while One-handed is Off, unlike Split gap and Floating width which are gated. |
| Portrait side | `oneHanded.portrait.side` | choice | Right | keep | left-handed user | ok |  | Right suits the majority; the in-keyboard rail's flip button writes back here for this orientation. |
| Landscape width | `oneHanded.landscape.widthPercent` | slider | 55% | keep | thumb-typist in landscape | ok |  | KDoc: 78% would barely help reach on a landscape-wide board, hence the separate narrower default. |
| Landscape height | `oneHanded.landscape.heightScale` | slider | 100% | demote | short-thumbed user in landscape | ok |  | No-op default and always visible; six one-handed rows show for a mode that ships Off. |
| Landscape side | `oneHanded.landscape.side` | choice | Right | keep | left-handed user in landscape | ok |  | Stored separately because grip changes when the phone turns; rail flip writes per orientation. |
| Split keyboard | `splitKeyboard` | toggle | Off | keep | tablet or foldable two-thumb typist | ok |  | Global and ungated by device or orientation: nothing stops it on a portrait phone, where halved rows are close to unusable. |
| Split gap | `splitGapPercent` | slider | 12% | keep | tablet typist widening the halves | ok |  | Only shown when split is on. 12% reads as a clear gap without narrowing keys much. |
| Floating keyboard | `floatingKeyboard` | toggle | Off | keep | tablet user typing over a video or large app | ok |  | Off is right; while active it replaces the width, position and bottom-padding logic entirely. |
| Floating keyboard width | `floatingWidthDp` | slider | 320 dp | keep | floating user who wants an exact width, not a drag | ok |  | Only shown while floating; shares the corner grip's 240 dp floor so a drag can never store what the slider could not. |
| Comma key opens emoji | `commaAsEmoji` | toggle | Off | demote | user who wants the globe key back and emoji on the comma | ok |  | With globeAsEmoji on by default, turning this on yields two emoji keys and no comma key. Only coherent paired with globe off. |
| Emoji key instead of 🌐 | `globeAsEmoji` | toggle | On | keep | monolingual user who wants emoji one tap away | split | yes | Costs multilingual users their globe key, pushing switching onto a spacebar swipe. The wizard already asks it with a keyboard preview. |
| Swap the comma and 🌐 keys | `swapCommaAndGlobe` | toggle | On | demote | migrant from another keyboard who wants the original order | ok |  | Pure arrangement: on puts whichever key became emoji in the outer slot and keeps the comma beside the spacebar. |

**Missing here:** Split only on large screens (Split is one global flag; a portrait phone can turn on a layout that is unusable there and stays split after rotating back.); Per-screen key spacing, side padding, bottom row height (SizingOverride carries height/width/padding/font/alignment but not these three, and landscape wants tighter spacing most.); Per-screen number row (A landscape phone has the least room for a sixth row, but the digit row is all-or-nothing across every screen shape.); Floating keyboard height (Height is drag-only between 0.6x and 1.6x while width gets a slider; no way to type an exact height or undo a bad drag.); Reset floating position and size (floatingXFraction/YFraction and floatingHeightScale are only ever written by drags; a panel dragged awkwardly has no settings-side way back.); Reset size and position (The seven-slider Size and position group has no reset; only per-variant overrides do, and the Resize tool's Reset only undoes that session.)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/layout.mdx` — wrong: Number row default listed as "Off (On on a tablet)". Code default is On for every device (SettingsRepository.kt:1045), which also makes the tablet overlay branch in DeviceFormDefaults.kt:38 unreachable — numberRowUntouched && !numberRow can never be true.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/layout.mdx` — wrong: Bottom padding range given as 0–40 dp in both the Size & position table and the Per-screen table. MAX_BOTTOM_PADDING_DP is 160 (raised in commit 238966fd for the Resize tool) and both sliders use it.
- `docs/src/content/docs/typing/size-position.mdx` — wrong: Same stale bottom-padding claim: "from 0dp to 40dp". Actual slider is 0–160 dp.
- `docs/src/content/docs/typing/size-position.mdx` — wrong: Side padding described as shaving an equal margin off both edges. dockedWidthArrangement (KeyboardScreen.kt:1370) folds it into one widthFraction, so with Keyboard position Left or Right the whole reduction lands on one side. Same claim in the layout_side_padding_info string.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/layout.mdx` — stale: Details bullet claims "Symbols on shift isn't in Settings search". It is indexed at SettingsSearch.kt:375.
- `MISSING: Resize toolbar tool` — gap: ToolbarTool.RESIZE (inline drag-resize over the docked keyboard, Reset/Done pills, 160 dp padding ceiling, shipped 2026-08-09) appears in no docs page — not layout.mdx, not typing/size-position.mdx, not reference/settings/tools.mdx. It is now the primary way sizing gets changed.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:5222 — "Follow portrait again" NavRow clears one screen variant's whole override (repository.clearVariantSizing)`, `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:5800 — clearVariantSizing(variant), the repo side of the above`, `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:2862 — symbolsReturnCharSet(): an empty "Characters that go back" field falls back to DefaultSymbolsReturnChars`, `feature/ime/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/ime/ui/ResizeOverlay.kt:278 — the inline Resize tool's Reset pill, but it returns to the values the session opened with, NOT to defaults`

_Coverage: Covered all 39 user-visible entries in LayoutSettings plus its three label helpers. Note the file shifted during the session (user edited concurrently): LayoutSettings is now MainActivity.kt:4946-5371, not the 4925-5380 given; I re-read the full function at the new offsets and the body is unchanged. Also read: SettingsRepository.kt KDoc for keyHeightDp/numberRowHeightDp/bottomPaddingDp/split*/floating*/keyboardWidthPercent/keyboardAlignment/keyGapScale/numberRow/globeAsEmoji/commaAsEmoji/swapCommaAndGlobe, the whole LayoutBehaviorSettings block (2675-2886), OneHandedProfile/Settings + range constants (255-315, 3438-3449), ScreenVariant.kt in full, DeviceFormDefaults.kt in full, Numerals.kt NumeralCommitScope, strings_layout.xml, SettingsSearch.kt layoutRows(), and reference/settings/layout.mdx in full plus the sizing sections of typing/size-position.mdx. Verified render behaviour in KeyboardScreen.kt (dockedWidthArrangement, numberRowShown, one-handed arrangement, floating clamps) and the tool dispatch in WMKeyboardService.kt. NOT covered, as they belong to other domains: keyCornerRadiusDp and the base fontScale (drawn on Appearance, MainActivity.kt:4678); the duplicate Split gap and Floating width sliders on the Split/Floating tool-detail screens (~MainActivity.kt:9075-9110, tools domain); the ONE_HANDED/SPLIT/FLOATING/RESIZE toolbar tools themselves and their pinning; the Accessibility \"Key size and spacing\" nav row that links here._

### Languages, scripts, CJK, per-app language

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Your languages (per-language row) | `enabledLanguages / enabledLayoutIds` | nav | Seeded from phone languages (max 4); fallback QWERTY + Avro + Probhat + Jatiya | keep | everyone; the entry point to every per-language option | WRONG → QWERTY only as the shipped fallback |  | BuiltInLayouts.defaultEnabledIds:324 ships 3 Bengali layouts. Seeding only runs if the wizard opens on an untouched set, so IME-enabled-from-Android users get them. |
| Add language | `-` | nav | 352 languages | keep | anyone adding a second language | - |  | Only route to the full registry. Search matches endonym/English/id/locale/layout name (LanguageSearchKey). |
| Suggested for you | `-` | list | Up to 4, from phone languages then region | keep | new user whose language is one tap away instead of a search | ok |  | Permission-free device signals only (DeviceLocales); empties as languages are added, so it costs nothing once done. |
| Download data automatically | `autoDownloadLanguageData` | toggle | On | keep | anyone typing a non-English language | ok |  | KDoc: a language whose data never arrives predicts badly and searches emoji only in English; packs are small. Word lists never auto-fetch. |
| Switch order | `enabledLayoutIds` | list | Order languages were added | keep | 3+ layout user who wants their common pair adjacent | - |  | Only appears with >1 layout. Orders layouts not languages, so two English layouts keep separate ring slots. |
| Your layouts (custom layout toggle) | `enabledLayoutIds` | toggle | Off (an installed layout arrives disabled) | keep | someone who built or installed a layout addon | ok |  | This toggle is what finishes an addon install; arriving on would put an unvetted grid in the ring. |
| Key layouts | `-` | nav | - | keep | layout editor users | - |  | Nav to the layout editor screen (separate domain). Subtitle changes once a custom layout exists. |
| Get more layouts (addon store) | `-` | nav | - | keep | user importing a Floris/HeliBoard layout | - |  | AddonStoreRow(AddonType.Layout); one row, no stored value. |
| Remember language per app | `perAppLanguage.enabled` | toggle | Off | keep | bilingual who writes English at work, Bengali to family | split |  | Real ~50/50 among multilinguals, invisible to monolinguals. Onboarding already asks personaLanguages ONE/MANY — derive it, don't ask twice. |
| List languages in the Android switcher | `osLanguageSwitcher` | toggle | Off | keep | user who switches IMEs from the system sheet or a hardware keyboard | ok |  | KDoc: the switcher draws the subtype label where a subtype-less keyboard draws its name, so one language name reads as the app's name. |
| Show app name first | `subtypeAppNameFirst` | toggle | Off | REMOVE | nobody | ok |  | Cosmetic label word-order, nested behind an off-by-default toggle, and its own KDoc admits it cannot change which part Android bolds. |
| Select languages in the system settings | `-` | nav | - | demote | Android 13-and-older user whose switcher shows only one language | - |  | On API 34+ the app enables subtypes itself; its own subtitle says "use it only if the switcher looks wrong". Hide below-34 only. |
| Search languages | `-` | text | Empty (registry order) | keep | anyone finding one of 352 entries | - |  | Unavoidable at this catalogue size. Empty list is registry order, not alphabetical — a real discoverability cost. |
| Download language data? (Download / Not now / Cancel) | `-` | action | Asked on every add that has data | keep | user on mobile data adding a language | - |  | Only path that fetches a word list. But it fires even with autoDownloadLanguageData on, so two of its three payloads were already consented to. |
| Layouts (per layout) | `enabledLayoutIds` | toggle | First layout on, rest off | keep | Bengali typist who wants Probhat as well as Avro | ok |  | Turning off the last layout anywhere is a silent no-op, so the ring can never be emptied from here. |
| Fancy style | `layoutBehavior.fancyStyleId` | choice | Bold | keep | fancy-text user picking a style without the on-keyboard strip | ok |  | Bold has the widest font coverage. Chips show only the styled sample — unlike the strip, no plain-name fallback when a style renders as tofu. |
| Conjunct-aware backspace | `conjunctBackspaceLanguages` | toggle | Off, per language | keep | Indic typist tired of backspace leaving জ্ | split |  | Code-point delete matches every other keyboard and keeps precise editing; whole-cluster delete removes more than asked. Genuinely split, too niche to ask. |
| Use known spellings | `suggestionStrip.spellingMapOffLangs` | toggle | On (absent = on) | keep | Banglish typist: tmr → তোমার, table → টেবিল | ok |  | KDoc: the map outranks every other source, so someone wanting the letter-for-letter reading has no other route off. Only shown for BengaliSpellingMap languages. |
| Numeral system | `layoutBehavior.numeralSystemByLang` | choice | The language's own digits (AUTO) | keep | Arabic/Bengali/Hindi typist who wants ٠-٩ on the keys | ok |  | Native digits are right for a native-script layout, and numeralCommitScope=TEXT_ONLY already keeps ASCII in number/phone fields. No row when native digits are 0-9. |
| Also suggest from (per other language) | `secondaryLanguages` | toggle | Off; romanized pairs auto-linked on add | keep | Banglish+English typist whose English keyboard stops autocorrecting Bengali | ok |  | Off for plain multilinguals is right (separate keyboards); RomanizedPairing auto-wires the case that actually needs it. No opt-out for the auto-wiring though. |
| Download all data (n MB) | `-` | action | - | keep | user who tapped "Not now" at add time | - |  | One tap for word list + emoji keywords + n-grams; size on the button is the consent, so no second dialog. |
| Word list (download / size tier / delete) | `-` | list | Large (300k words), not persisted | keep | any non-English typist wanting real suggestions | ok |  | Only English and Bengali bundle a dictionary. Large is a sensible size/quality point; the tier resets to Large on every visit (see missing). |
| Custom dictionaries | `-` | nav | - | keep | user importing their own word list | - |  | Nav into the Typing domain's screen; belongs here too because that is where a language's word data is being looked at. |
| Emoji keywords pack (download / delete) | `-` | action | Not downloaded (auto-fetched when both auto-download toggles on) | keep | non-English user searching emoji by name | ok |  | ~100 KB, determinate progress. Deleting leaves a "declined" mark so the automatic pass will not refetch. |
| Emoji keywords | `-` | nav | - | keep | user managing packs across all languages at once | - |  | Nav into the Emoji domain screen. |
| Conversion dictionary packs | `-` | action | Not downloaded | keep | any Chinese/Japanese/Cantonese typist — no candidates without it | ok |  | All 5 packs have URLs+checksums today; ja_kana is 42 MB, so bundling is out. Composer still commits raw kana/pinyin until fetched. |
| Traditional characters | `cjk.traditionalOutput` | toggle | Off | demote | Taiwan/Hong Kong typist | ok |  | Simplified is the larger population. Renders on the Japanese screen where JapaneseComposer never applies it — gate to zh/yue. |
| Region (Standard / Taiwan / Hong Kong) | `cjk.hanRegion` | choice | Standard (GENERIC) | demote | Taiwan typist who wants 計程車, not 出租車 | ok |  | Character mapping alone does not reach regional vocabulary. Same inert-on-Japanese problem as the toggle above it. |
| Lazy pronunciation (懶音) | `cjk.jyutpingLazy` | toggle | Off | keep | most Hong Kong Cantonese speakers | WRONG → On |  | The code's own comment: these are "the sound mergers most Hong Kong speakers have, and therefore spell" — off means typing lei gets zero candidates for 你. |
| Fuzzy Pinyin | `cjk.pinyinFuzzy` | toggle | Off | keep | southern Mandarin speaker who merges zh/z, n/l, an/ang | split |  | Real north/south split; fuzzy costs precision for speakers who do distinguish. Every major zh IME ships it off. See missing: it should be per-pair. |
| Double Pinyin scheme | `cjk.pinyinDoublePinyin` | choice | Off (full Pinyin) | keep | experienced Double Pinyin typist | ok |  | Full pinyin is what a new user expects. All 5 tables ship, so the "coming soon" disabled branch (LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:1352) is dead code. |
| Remove {language} | `enabledLayoutIds` | action | - | keep | anyone pruning the switch ring | - |  | Only shown with >1 language enabled. Takes every layout of that language out at once; offers to delete its downloaded files. |
| Numerals (commit scope) | `layoutBehavior.numeralCommitScope` | choice | Text fields only | keep | Arabic/Bengali typist who still wants parseable phone numbers | ok |  | KDoc: keeps ASCII in numeric/phone/date/time fields so apps can parse them. Drawn on the Layout screen, not Languages — split from the per-language choice it modifies. |

**Missing here:** Ask before downloading on mobile data (Whisper and LLM downloads confirm on metered networks; language packs and a 42 MB CJK dict do not check at all); Default word-list size tier (Tier is per-composition state, so it snaps back to Large on every visit; wanting Everything means re-picking per language); Auto-link romanized languages (autoPairRomanizedSecondaries runs on every add, so a secondary link removed by hand comes back next time any language is added); Forget remembered apps (per-app language) (layoutByPackage is written invisibly and has no viewer or clear action; one accidental switch pins that app forever); Per-pair Fuzzy Pinyin (11 confusion groups are all-or-nothing; Sogou/Google Pinyin let users pick pairs, e.g. an/ang on but n/l off); Morse commit pause (Fixed 750 ms; slow or motor-impaired users lose sequences mid-letter, and the docs already flag it as unadjustable)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` — wrong: Says "List languages in Android's switcher ... default on" (lines 56 and 129). SettingsRepository.kt:1203 is osLanguageSwitcher = false, and the KDoc explains why off is right.
- `docs/src/content/docs/languages/overview.mdx` — wrong: Line 81 repeats "List languages in Android's switcher, under System language switcher, is on by default". Code default is off.
- `docs/src/content/docs/languages/switching.mdx` — wrong: Lines 98 and 129 repeat the same "on by default" claim for the system switcher. Code default is off.
- `docs/src/content/docs/languages/notation.mdx` — wrong: Line 104 says music/braille/morse/ipa each get "a Numerals choice (defaults to Auto on all four)". All four are numeralSystem = LATIN, and the group only renders when it differs, so no Numerals group appears on any of them.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` — wrong: Line 93 says "Also suggest from ... all start off". RomanizedPairing auto-links romanized pairs both ways on add (SettingsRepository.kt:5319), so Banglish+Bengali start on and even toast about it.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` — wrong: Line 26 gives the region order as SIM, network, then locale. DeviceLocales.kt reads SIM, network, time zone, then locale — the time-zone signal is missing (overview.mdx has it right).
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` — gap: The per-language walkthrough ("up to eight groups", line 66) omits two groups the code renders: Spellings / Use known spellings (on by default, LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:743) and Style / Fancy style (line 702).
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` — stale: Row titles drifted from the shipped strings: docs say "List languages in Android's switcher" and "Choose languages in system settings"; the app draws "List languages in the Android switcher" and "Select languages in the system settings".
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/languages.mdx` — stale: Line 156 documents an internal source comment as a user-facing caveat. The comment (LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:1350) and its unreachable "coming soon" branch should be deleted from the code rather than described in the reference.
- `docs/src/content/docs/languages/notation.mdx` — stale: Line 104 says "only English (QWERTY) and the three Bangla layouts start on by default", which is the raw fallback (BuiltInLayouts.defaultEnabledIds) and contradicts overview.mdx's seeded-from-your-phone story. bengali.mdx:71 makes the same claim.
- `MISSING: metered-connection behaviour for language data` — gap: No page states that language packs, emoji keywords and the 42 MB CJK dictionaries download on mobile data with no confirmation, unlike Whisper/LLM models which do confirm.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:931 — "Remove {language}" button (only with >1 language enabled)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:566 — LanguageDataDeleteDialog: delete a removed language's downloaded files, or keep them`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:1101 — word-list Delete icon`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:989 — emoji-keyword pack Delete icon`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:1246 — CJK pack Delete icon`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:790 — numeral row re-reads a stale stored value as whichever of the two buttons it behaves like, so one press writes a valid value`, `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:5344 — setEnabledLayoutIds falls back to the default layout when the pick is emptied`

_Coverage: Read in full: MainActivity.kt LanguageSettings (now lines 5413-5626 — the file grew 41 lines mid-audit from concurrent edits; I re-read the range at its new offset and the content is unchanged), all 1410 lines of LanguageSettingsScreens.kt, and the KDoc for every listed field in SettingsRepository.kt (enabledLayoutIds:889, secondaryLanguages:900, autoDownloadLanguageData:995, osLanguageSwitcher:1203, subtypeAppNameFirst:1211, perAppLanguage:1213/2285, conjunctBackspaceLanguages:1226, CjkSettings:2152, numeralSystemByLang:2745, numeralCommitScope:2753, fancyStyleId:2818, spellingMapOffLangs:3043) plus conjunctLanguagesFromPrefs:7154 and autoPairRomanizedSecondaries:5319. Read all 7 assigned docs pages in full and cross-checked claims against CjkDictCatalog (all 5 packs have URL+sha, so the docs are right), DoublePinyin (all 5 tables ship, so the disabled branch is dead), FancyStyles (22 styles, docs right), Language.kt (music/braille/morse/ipa are all numeralSystem=LATIN), DeviceLocales and LanguageSuggestions. Re-scanned both source ranges a second time. NOT covered: docs/languages/custom-layouts.mdx and dictionaries.mdx (not assigned, and the keymaps / custom-dictionaries screens they document are other agents' domains), the Emoji keywords and Custom dictionaries sub-screens behind their nav rows, and the onboarding language pages. Two cross-cutting findings that did not fit a single row: (1) no row in this domain passes `default =` to ToggleSetting/ChoiceSetting, so the app's per-row reset control (SettingsUi.kt:673, ResetSetting) never appears anywhere on the Languages screen or any language detail screen even though SettingsDefaults exists and other screens use it; (2) the add-language download prompt fires regardless of autoDownloadLanguageData, so a user with the master switch on is still asked about emoji keywords and n-grams they already consented to — only the word-list half of the question is new._

### Key layouts and layout behaviour

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Import a layout | `customLayouts` | action | n/a | keep | anyone handed a .wmlayout.json by a friend or forum | - |  | Only in-app path for a shared grid; confirms first, never auto-enables (LayoutEditorScreens.kt:436). |
| Import from another keyboard | `customLayouts` | action | n/a | keep | switchers leaving FlorisBoard/HeliBoard with a hand-built grid | - |  | Converts Floris JSON / HeliBoard txt; the only migration path that keeps a custom grid. |
| Language (foreign import dialog) | `customLayouts[].langId` | choice | guessed from the character set | keep | importer of a Cyrillic grid that is Ukrainian, not Russian | ok |  | No foreign format records a language; it picks dictionary, autocorrect, shift and dictation. Guess shown, never silent. |
| Export (per layout row) | `-` | action | n/a | keep | anyone sharing or backing up an hour of grid work | - |  | Reports success and failure both ways on purpose — the file may be the only copy (LayoutEditorScreens.kt:274). |
| Duplicate (per layout row) | `customLayouts` | action | n/a | keep | anyone starting a layout; it is the only way to create one | - |  | There is no "new blank layout" row, so duplicate is the sole create path. Never activates the copy. |
| Delete (your layout) | `customLayouts` | action | n/a | keep | anyone clearing failed experiments | - |  | Confirms first; body warns the keyboard falls back to QWERTY if the layout was on. |
| Reset (edited built-in) | `customLayouts` | action | n/a | keep | anyone who broke a shipped grid and wants it back | - |  | Edits to a built-in are stored as an override under the same id, so removal restores rather than deletes. |
| Layout row (tap to edit) | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | anyone editing a grid | - |  | Whole-row tap; ReturnAnchor scrolls back to the same row on return from the editor. |
| Your layouts / Built in listing | `customLayouts` | list | no custom layouts; built-ins seeded from phone languages | keep | anyone with more than one layout | ok |  | Customs are listed on or off (hiding disabled ones made Duplicate and Import produce invisible results); built-ins filtered to enabled. |
| Layer chips | `customLayouts[].layers` | choice | Letters | keep | anyone editing symbols, numpad or Fn | ok |  | Nine layers; unauthored ones inherit the built-in so a letter move never costs a phone pad. |
| Add an Fn layer | `customLayouts[].layers.fn` | action | absent | keep | terminal / SSH users wanting Esc and F1-F12 | ok |  | Nothing ships an Fn layer, so this template is the only way in; an empty grid is drawn so a stray tap cannot fork QWERTY into Fn. |
| Undo / Redo | `-` | action | 30 session snapshots | keep | anyone who deleted the wrong row | ok |  | Session-scoped by design; persisting would mean a second document per layout for "undo last Tuesday". |
| Add a key to row N | `customLayouts[].layers` | action | n/a | keep | anyone widening a row | - |  | Row action bar, revealed only once a key is selected. |
| Duplicate row | `customLayouts[].layers` | action | n/a | keep | anyone building a second symbol row | - |  | Reindexes per-row heights so they follow the copy. |
| Delete row | `customLayouts[].layers` | action | n/a | keep | anyone trimming a grid | - |  | Disabled at one row left, so a layer can never be emptied by accident. |
| Row height (selected row) | `customLayouts[].layers[].rowHeights` | slider | ×1.00 (0.5-2.0, quarter steps) | keep | authors giving a bottom row or number row extra hit area | ok |  | Collapses back to null at ×1 so untouched layouts never grow the stored field. |
| Add a row | `customLayouts[].layers` | action | n/a | keep | authors of 5-row or split grids | - |  | Appends one empty row to the current layer. |
| Reorder rows | `customLayouts[].layers` | list | as authored | keep | anyone moving the number row to the bottom | ok |  | Drag dialog; carries rowHeights through the permutation. |
| Reorder the keys in row N | `customLayouts[].layers` | list | as authored | keep | anyone doing bulk key moves instead of one nudge at a time | ok |  | Only shown for a selected row with >1 key; labels fall back to action names for icon keys. |
| Show the shift plane | `-` | toggle | Off | keep | Probhat/Jatiya authors, whose shift plane holds half the alphabet | ok |  | View state (rememberSaveable), not a stored preference. Off is right: the primary plane is what you edit. |
| Add tablet keys automatically | `customLayouts[].tabletExpand` | toggle | On | demote | author of a grid already laid out wide by hand | ok |  | Must default On: a file written before the field existed decodes without it, so any other default silently opts out all shipped layouts. |
| Edit as JSON | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | authors needing flick maps, clipboard actions, rename or language change | - |  | The only reachable editor for name, langId, composer, flick and clipboardAction — carries more weight than "escape hatch" implies. |
| Reset this layer | `customLayouts[].layers` | action | n/a | keep | anyone who forked a layer by mistake | - |  | Shown only on an authored layer; drops back to the inherited built-in grid. |
| Problems | `-` | list | n/a | keep | anyone whose layout will not turn on | - |  | Read-only; the same list rememberLayoutEnableGate shows when it refuses to enable, so the two cannot disagree. |
| Label | `customLayouts[].layers[].label` | text | existing label | keep | every layout author | - |  | Core edit. Uses the pending-echo pattern so the DataStore round trip cannot scramble typing. |
| Output | `customLayouts[].layers[].output` | text | blank = types the label | keep | authors of keys whose glyph differs from what they type | ok |  | Blank-means-label keeps the common case a one-field edit. |
| Shift label | `customLayouts[].layers[].shiftLabel` | text | blank = uppercase of label | keep | Bengali/Indic authors, where shift is a different letter, not a case | ok |  | Uppercase fallback is right for Latin; explicit value is required for the scripts that need it. |
| Action | `customLayouts[].layers[].action` | choice | Types text | keep | anyone adding Ctrl, Tab, arrows or a Tasker key | ok |  | 26 options in six groups; catalog-driven so a new action is one entry, not an editor change. |
| Broadcast action | `customLayouts[].layers[].action (Broadcast)` | text | blank | keep | Tasker/MacroDroid users firing a macro from a key | ok |  | Only field the Broadcast action needs; shown conditionally so it costs nobody else a row. |
| Dot number | `customLayouts[].layers[].action (BrailleDot)` | text | 1 | keep | braille layout authors | ok |  | 1-6 only; out-of-range input is silently ignored rather than shown as an error — worth an inline message instead. |
| Width | `customLayouts[].layers[].width` | slider | 1.0 (0.5-5.0, quarter steps) | keep | anyone sizing a spacebar or shift | ok |  | Quarter steps deliberate: every built-in width lands on a step, and a free slider would write 1.0374 into a hand-editable file. |
| Fill the row | `customLayouts[].layers[].width` | action | n/a | keep | anyone left 0.75 short after deleting a key | - |  | One-tap fix for the red width-mismatch warning; only appears when there is >=0.5 of slack. |
| Icon | `customLayouts[].layers[].icon` | text | blank | keep | authors wanting a monochrome glyph instead of a label | ok |  | Live found/missing feedback against KeyIcons. A picker would beat a free-text name field here. |
| Icon hint | `customLayouts[].layers[].iconHint` | text | blank | demote | a handful of authors icon-marking the corner hint | ok |  | Rarest field in the sheet; sits between Icon and Alternates and costs everyone a row. Fold under an "advanced" disclosure. |
| Alternates for press and hold | `customLayouts[].layers[].longPress` | text | as authored | keep | anyone adding accents, .com or a currency to a key | ok |  | Space-separated matches the symbol-set editor; a chip editor would fail on ".com" and "https://". |
| Field adaptation | `customLayouts[].layers[].role` | choice | None | keep | authors of non-Latin grids that want @ and / in email/URL fields | ok |  | Built-ins already carry the roles; None is right for a key the author just added. No reset control, but None is option one. |
| Delete key / move left / move right / duplicate key | `customLayouts[].layers` | action | n/a | keep | every layout author | - |  | Move buttons disable at the row edges; grid is forced LTR so "right" is always index+1. |
| Layout JSON | `customLayouts` | text | the layout, defaults omitted | keep | authors renaming a layout, changing its language, or pasting a grid in | ok |  | encodeDefaults=false keeps a 400-key grid readable. Draft + Apply because half-typed JSON is not a layout. |
| Apply (JSON) | `customLayouts` | action | n/a | keep | anyone hand-editing | - |  | Discards the pasted id so it can never overwrite another layout; runs the import repair and lists what changed. |
| Press and hold ?123 for the number pad | `layoutBehavior.symbolsLongPressNumpad` | toggle | On | keep | anyone entering numbers in a plain text field | ok |  | KDoc: the keypad is otherwise buried in the toolbox, and a held ?123 costs nothing when unwanted. |
| All accents on press and hold | `layoutBehavior.showAllPopupKeys` | toggle | Off | keep | multilingual Latin typists who want every diacritic on one key | ok |  | KDoc: built-in popups are deliberately short; the full set is a wall of glyphs most people never want. |
| Currency keys | `layoutBehavior.currencyKeys` | text | ৳ € £ ¥ ₹ ₿ | keep | anyone whose currency is not first in the built-in six | WRONG → locale currency first, then € £ ¥ ₹ ₿ |  | Taka first is a Bangladesh default shipped worldwide; the device locale already knows the right glyph. Also the one row here with no reset control. |
| Caps lock double press | `layoutBehavior.shiftCapsLockMs` | slider | 350 ms (150-600) | keep | users with tremor or slow double-taps who cannot reach caps lock | ok |  | 350 ms is the platform double-tap ballpark; KDoc states the accident-vs-forgiveness trade directly. |
| Smart detection of key hits | `layoutBehavior.smartHitDetection` | toggle | Off | keep | every thumb-typist with fat-finger boundary misses | WRONG → On |  | Biases only boundary taps, never deliberate presses. Competing keyboards ship this always on; nobody hunts Typing settings for accuracy. |
| 2-D cursor touchpad | `layoutBehavior.spaceCursor2d` | toggle | Off | keep | long-document editors who want up/down as well as left/right | ok |  | Already gated behind a spacebar slide being set to cursor control, and it claims the down direction from swipe-to-hide. |
| Swipe down to hide the keyboard | `layoutBehavior.spaceSwipeDownHide` | toggle | Off | keep | users who dismiss the keyboard constantly on a tall phone | ok |  | KDoc: off so a stray vertical drag never closes the keyboard mid-type. The toolbar flick already covers the common case. |
| The spacebar shows | `layoutBehavior.spacebarDisplay` | choice | Language | keep | bilingual users who need to see which language is armed | ok |  | Language is the value that changes as you cycle; layout name is static for most users. |
| Shift and Enter type a new line | `layoutBehavior.shiftEnterNewline` | toggle | Off | keep | chat users who want a line break without sending | ok |  | KDoc: Enter following the app's declared action is platform behaviour; a keyboard that quietly stops sending is worse than one that cannot insert a newline. |
| Key hint font size | `layoutBehavior.hintFontScale` | slider | 1.0× (0.5-2.0) | keep | low-vision users who cannot read a 10sp corner hint | ok |  | Only meaningful with long-press hints on; 1.0 keeps the 10sp base. Themes can override it. |
| Symbols on shift | `layoutBehavior.numberRowShiftSymbols` | toggle | Off | keep | programmers who want brackets without leaving the letters | ok |  | Only shown with the number row on. Off is right: shift+digit changing the row surprises anyone not expecting it. |
| Number row in symbols | `layoutBehavior.numberRowInSymbols` | toggle | On | keep | nobody at the default; it only helps once switched off | WRONG → Off |  | The symbols layer's own top row is already 1-0 (BuiltInLayouts.kt:797), so On draws digits twice and burns a row of height. |
| Go back to the letters | `layoutBehavior.symbolsReturnToLetters` | toggle | Off | keep | anyone who opens ?123 for a single full stop | split | yes | KDoc names the split itself: half the audience wants one character, half types a whole line of punctuation. Affects every symbol trip; askable in one sentence. |
| Characters that go back | `layoutBehavior.symbolsReturnChars` | text | !?.,;: | demote | the few who want a slash or dash to bounce back too | ok |  | Digits deliberately excluded — typing "12" is exactly when you want to stay. Only shown when the toggle is on; no reset control. |
| Type native digits in | `layoutBehavior.numeralCommitScope` | choice | Text fields only | keep | Arabic/Bengali/Persian typists who need forms to stay parseable | ok |  | KDoc: keeps ASCII in numeric/phone/date/time fields so those stay machine-parseable. Global on purpose — about fields, not scripts. |
| Bottom row height | `layoutBehavior.bottomRowHeightDp` | slider | 0 = follow the key height | keep | users who want a fatter spacebar without a taller keyboard | ok |  | 0 leaves the row untouched until asked. Slider range is 0-96 but the documented floor is 32, so 1-31 dp is settable and gives a hairline bottom row. |
| Side padding | `layoutBehavior.sidePadScale` | slider | 0% (0-30%) | keep | large-phone users wanting thumb reach without one-handed mode | ok |  | Symmetric narrowing; 0 is right because it is a preference nobody has until their phone gets wider. Themes can override. |
| Numeral system | `layoutBehavior.numeralSystemByLang` | choice | the language's own digits (AUTO) | keep | Bengali/Arabic typists who prefer 0-9 in practice | ok |  | Per-language on purpose; row only appears for a language whose digits are not Latin. Two options only — the full system list was noise. |
| Fancy style | `layoutBehavior.fancyStyleId` | choice | bold | keep | users who prefer one fancy style and want it discoverable in Settings | ok |  | Duplicates the on-keyboard style strip, which is where it is actually chosen; bold is the least illegible starting style. |
| Style to start with (Fancy tool) | `layoutBehavior.fancyToolStyleId` | choice | Follow the strip | demote | someone who always types the same fancy style | ok |  | "Follow the strip" is the null pick and is one press away, which is why the row deliberately has no reset control. |
| Keep the language after you turn it off | `layoutBehavior.fancyToolKeepsLanguage` | toggle | Off | demote | heavy fancy-text users wanting it one 🌐 swipe away | ok |  | Off keeps the globe cycling the languages you actually read. Very small audience; a third fancy toggle is a lot of surface. |
| Turn off when the keyboard closes | `layoutBehavior.fancyToolAutoOff` | toggle | Off | keep | anyone who once mailed a colleague in Fraktur | WRONG → On |  | KDoc names the failure itself: fancy text is one nickname or one message, and forgetting the tool restyles the next mail. Default should protect against that. |
| (internal) numberRowUntouched | `layoutBehavior.numberRowUntouched` | - | true | keep | tablet users who turn the digit row off and want it to stay off | ok |  | Not user-visible. Derived from DataStore key presence — the only durable record of "never chose"; without it the tablet overlay would fight the toggle. |
| (internal) keyHeightUntouched | `layoutBehavior.keyHeightUntouched` | - | true | keep | tablet users who sized the board by hand | ok |  | Not user-visible. Same pattern, shared with the number-row height slider so a second flag cannot disagree. |

**Missing here:** Rename a layout (Editor only prints the name as a header; renaming needs the JSON screen. Every copy of a copy reads "X copy copy".); Change a layout's language (langId picks dictionary, autocorrect, shift and dictation. Foreign import asks for it; a duplicate of QWERTY can never be re-languaged outside JSON.); Composer override (A phonetic vs direct grid for the same language is a per-layout choice, reachable only by hand-editing JSON.); Create a blank layout (Empty state says "Copy a layout below"; there is no New row, so building from scratch means duplicating then deleting 30 keys.); Editor preview key height (Preview clamps the user's key height to 38-56dp, so someone typing at 70dp never sees their real proportions while editing.)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` — wrong: "Only enabled layouts appear on either list" and "Your layouts: the enabled layouts you've made" are wrong. LayoutEditorScreens.kt:388-404 deliberately does NOT filter customs by enabled — the comment says filtering made Duplicate and Import produce layouts that then vanished. Same claim in languages/custom-layouts.mdx:103.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` — wrong: "Enabling a layout under Languages doesn't consult this list directly; you can switch on something broken" is wrong. rememberLayoutEnableGate (LayoutEditorScreens.kt:772) now blocks enabling and names every BLOCKING finding. Same claim in languages/custom-layouts.mdx:113.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` — wrong: Edge-case bullet claims the JSON serializer "writes every field explicitly, defaults included" and that the on-screen caption is misleading. LayoutSpec.kt:197 uses encodeDefaults=false for encodeForEditing, so the caption is correct and the bullet is now the wrong one.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` — wrong: Action picker is "25 options across six groups" and the Typing list omits Caps lock. KeyActionCatalog has 26 entries, including CapsLock. Same count in languages/custom-layouts.mdx:45.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` — wrong: "A closing caption reminds you that nothing here affects typing until the layout is switched on." There are two captions: an enabled layout shows layout_editor_live_caption ("changes affect typing straight away"). The docs describe only the old, wrong one.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` — stale: Layout counts are stale: "393 layouts (18 compiled + 375 JSON assets)". Actual is 18 built-ins + 354 asset files = 372. Repeated in languages/custom-layouts.mdx:14 and :103; LayoutSpec.kt:118 says 354 too.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` — gap: The screen's group list names only "Import a layout". The second ungrouped row, "Import from another keyboard" (LayoutEditorScreens.kt:445), is not mentioned on this page at all.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` — gap: The below-the-grid row table and the Options table both omit "Add tablet keys automatically" (per-layout toggle, default On). It sits between Show the shift plane and Edit as JSON.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/keymaps.mdx` — gap: No page states that a layout's name, language (langId) and composer are editable only through the JSON screen — the single biggest gap a user hits after duplicating a layout.
- `docs/src/content/docs/languages/custom-layouts.mdx` — stale: "Every enabled layout shows up here with an edit button." There is no edit button; the row itself opens the editor, and the three trailing buttons are export, duplicate and delete/reset.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/importing-other-keyboards.mdx` — stale: SettingsPath reads "Layouts / Import from another keyboard" (lines 27 and 84); the screen is titled "Key layouts" (home_keymaps_title).
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/importing-other-keyboards.mdx` — stale: "asks you two things before it saves anything" — the dialog asks one thing (the language); the rest is a confirm. Also undocumented: foreign files over 4 MB are rejected with the generic "not a layout" error (LayoutEditorScreens.kt:158,320).

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LayoutEditorScreens.kt:703 — Reset (Restore icon) on an edited built-in row; drops the override and restores the shipped grid`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LayoutEditorScreens.kt:1181 — "Reset this layer" row; shown only on an authored layer`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LayoutEditorScreens.kt:2154 — row-height preset chips, ×1 is the default`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/LayoutEditorScreens.kt:2188 — key-width preset chips, 1 is the default`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/SettingsUi.kt:673 — ResetSetting restore icon, shown whenever a row's value differs from its passed default`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2080 — ToggleSetting reset (covers symbolsLongPressNumpad, showAllPopupKeys, smartHitDetection, spaceCursor2d, spaceSwipeDownHide, shiftEnterNewline, numberRowShiftSymbols, numberRowInSymbols, symbolsReturnToLetters, fancyToolKeepsLanguage, fancyToolAutoOff)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2280 — SliderSetting reset (shiftCapsLockMs, hintFontScale, bottomRowHeightDp, sidePadScale)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2385 — ChoiceSetting reset (spacebarDisplay, numeralCommitScope, numeralSystemByLang, fancyStyleId)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/storage/StorageScreen.kt:424 — global two-step "reset all settings" dialog`

Also read every UI call site of the 24 LayoutBehaviorSettings fields: MainActivity.kt:2884-2890, 3206-3292, 4591-4665, 4841-4851, 5089-5240, 9793-9830; LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:705-800. Verified against BuiltInLayouts.kt (18 built-ins, symbolRows top row is 1-0), 354 files in app/src/main/assets/layouts, LayoutSpec.kt (tabletExpand default, encodeDefaults=false), and strings_layout_editor.xml / strings_layout.xml / strings_keypress.xml / strings_typing.xml for the drawn titles. NOT covered: 22 of the 24 LayoutBehaviorSettings fields are DRAWN on screens owned by other agents (typing, keypress, layout, appearance, languages/<lang>, tools/fancy) — I judged the fields but did not audit the rest of those screens, so expect overlap and dedupe against those agents. Also not covered: the Languages/switching screen where a layout is actually enabled, the addon-repository layout install path, ForeignLayouts.convert internals (only its two dialogs), and validateLayout's finding list beyond what the editor displays. numberRowUntouched and keyHeightUntouched are listed for field completeness but have no UI._

### Emoji, kaomoji, GIFs, stickers

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Emoji button in toolbar | `emojiToolbar` | toggle | On | keep | everyone; emoji is the most-reached tool | ok |  | Keeps the emoji shortcut in the strip even while suggestions show (KeyboardScreen.kt:2399). Also needs EMOJI in enabledTools. |
| Full-screen emoji panel | `emojiFullBleed` | toggle | On | keep | users who want the toolbar reachable while picking | ok |  | On reclaims the toolbar row for emoji, matching Gboard. Off is a minority taste, cheap to find. |
| Emoji suggestions | `emojiPrediction` | toggle | On | keep | users who find emoji chips steal suggestion slots | ok |  | Strip chips after a whole word. Off is the real complaint this answers; On matches every mainstream keyboard. |
| Press an emoji suggestion | `emojiInsertMode` | choice | Replace word | keep | users who want "birthday 🎂", not just 🎂 | ok |  | Genuinely split preference but low stakes and self-teaching after one tap. Replace matches Gboard. Hidden when suggestions off. |
| Default skin tone | `emoji.defaultSkinTone` | choice | None (✋ yellow) | keep | anyone whose skin is not the neutral yellow base | split | yes | No tone is a majority; affects every 👍 sent, and asking is one tap on six swatches. Not currently in the wizard. |
| Override with last used | `emoji.toneOverrideByLastUsed` | toggle | On | demote | user whose global tone is overridden by a stray per-emoji pick | ok |  | KDoc: a per-emoji tone is a more specific choice. Off exists only to un-stick per-emoji memory; very few need it. |
| Return to the keyboard after you insert | `emoji.closeAfterInsert` | toggle | Off | keep | one-emoji-then-keep-typing users | ok |  | Off lets you pick several in a row. Shared with the clipboard panel, so one toggle covers two panels. |
| History tab | `emojiTabMode` | choice | Recent | keep | users who want a stable frequency ranking, not churn | ok |  | Recent matches Gboard and every picker. Most used is a real second taste. Favourites pin first either way. |
| Clear recents button | `emojiClearRecentsButton` | toggle | Off | merge | nobody wants the toggle; some want the button | - |  | A preference over whether a button exists. Clear-all is otherwise unreachable; fold it into the history-cell long-press menu that already offers Remove from recents. |
| Kaomoji and Emoticons | `emoji.kaomojiTabs` | toggle | Off | keep | kaomoji users; 312 faces are dead weight for everyone else | ok |  | KDoc: the two tabs narrow the tab strip and most users never reach for them. Off is right. |
| Emoji descriptions | `emojiLongPressName` | toggle | On | demote | users telling near-identical faces apart; low-vision users | ok |  | Adds the Unicode name to the long-press popup. Nobody plausibly turns it off; the row is close to dead weight. |
| Animated emoji | `emoji.animated` | toggle | On | keep | metered-data users who don't want 800 KB per long-press | ok |  | KDoc: nothing downloads until a long press, and the control only shows on image-accepting fields. On is safe. |
| Send emoji as image | `emoji.sendAsSticker` | toggle | On | keep | users whose recipients see tofu instead of the emoji | ok |  | Draws the glyph at 512×512 locally, no network. Only appears on image-accepting fields. Send mode follows stickerSendMode. |
| Emoji keywords | `-` | nav | — | keep | non-English/Bengali typists searching emoji in their language | - |  | Nav to the keyword-pack sub-screen. Mirrored from each language's own screen (LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:912). |
| Emoji row | `emojiBarMode` | choice | Off | keep | heavy emoji users who want them one tap away | ok |  | Own row costs a permanent key row; Off is the right default. Button is the cheap middle option. |
| Emoji row content | `emojiBarContent` | choice | Most used | keep | users who curated favourites and want only those | ok |  | Only shown while the row is on. Favourites sort first whichever is picked, so the three differ only in the tail. |
| Emoji in the row | `emoji.barCount` | slider | 8 (range 3–16) | keep | tablet and small-phone users tuning density | ok |  | Slots divide the row width; 8 is a sane phone default. Gated on the row being on. |
| Scroll the emoji row | `emoji.barScrollable` | toggle | Off | demote | users who want more than barCount emoji reachable | ok |  | KDoc: off so a sideways swipe can't slide the row out from under a tap. Third-level gated row. |
| Emoji font | `emojiFont` | choice | System | keep | users on old/OEM fonts seeing tofu; style preference | ok | yes | Already asked in the wizard with a live tofu-vs-Noto comparison (OnboardingPages.kt:800). System is the only safe zero-download default. |
| Get the newest emoji / New emoji are available | `emojiFontInstalled.installedId` | action | — | keep | anyone whose device font predates recent emoji | - |  | Direct Noto fetch plus an update card; the Google choice can only serve the build the device already has. Self-hiding when current. |
| Installed emoji font list | `emojiFontInstalled.installedId` | list | none selected | keep | Twemoji/OpenMoji users | - |  | Select or delete each library face. Deleting the selected one falls back to System (SettingsRepository.kt:6585). |
| Import / Replace emoji font file | `-` | action | — | demote | users with a font file not in the addon repo | - |  | Legacy single-slot import beside the multi-font library. Duplicates what Installed does, but is the only route for an off-repo file. |
| Emoji fonts (addon store row) | `-` | nav | — | keep | users looking for where fonts come from | - |  | Placed straight after the picker because Installed needs a font to have arrived from somewhere first. |
| Hide emoji that your device cannot show | `emoji.hideUnrenderable` | toggle | Off | keep | old-device users facing a wall of tofu boxes | ok | yes | Off is right globally; the wizard already asks exactly the users who need it, and only when tofu is detected (OnboardingPages.kt:821). |
| Download automatically (keyword packs) | `emoji.autoDownloadKeywords` | toggle | On | keep | metered-data users; everyone else gets search in their language | ok |  | KDoc: packs are 148 B–112 KB. Gated behind autoDownloadLanguageData too, so two switches must both be on. |
| Emoji keywords (addon store row) | `-` | nav | — | keep | users after a pack the data repo doesn't carry | - |  | Third source alongside the repo download and manual import. |
| Per-language keyword pack row | `emoji.keywordPackVersion` | action | not downloaded | keep | speakers of the 125 covered languages | - |  | Download/delete with count and size. Deleting sets a declined marker so auto-download won't undo it (EmojiDictDownloadManager.kt:178). |
| Import pack / From URL | `emoji.keywordPackVersion` | action | — | keep | speakers of languages the repo doesn't cover | - |  | TSV or the repo's JSON, sniffed. 8 MB cap, http/https only. Stacks on the bundled English/Bengali keywords. |
| Emoji button in toolbar (tool page copy) | `emojiToolbar` | toggle | On | REMOVE | nobody; identical row two screens away | ok |  | Exact duplicate of the Emoji-screen row (MainActivity.kt:9063 vs 5814). The All emoji settings nav below it already covers this. |
| All emoji settings | `-` | nav | — | keep | users who arrived from the toolbox | - |  | Only route from the tool page to the full Emoji screen. |
| Full-screen picker | `mediaFullBleed` | toggle | On | keep | users who want the toolbar while browsing GIFs | ok |  | Same trade as emojiFullBleed, one setting shared by both media panels. Search moves up next to a back button. |
| Klipy API key / GIPHY API key | `klipyApiKey, giphyApiKey` | text | blank (built-in key if any) | keep | everyone; without one key the tool cannot search | ok |  | Blank means use the built-in key, which may itself be blank. Not a taste setting — a hard gate on the tool working. |
| Send stickers as | `stickerSendMode` | choice | Sticker | keep | users in apps that mishandle the WhatsApp sticker MIME | ok |  | Sticker tries image/webp.wasticker first. Also governs emoji sent as images. Image is the escape hatch when an app misbehaves. |
| Send GIFs as | `gifSendMode` | choice | Image | demote | users sending WebP-backed results into WhatsApp | ok |  | Sticker mode only ever applies to WebP results; an animated GIF can never take it, so the choice is inert for most picks. |
| Multiple sources | `gifSourceMode` | choice | Tabs | demote | users with both Klipy and GIPHY keys configured | ok |  | Does nothing at all with one key, which is the common case. No reset control: it is a bare ChoiceControl, not ChoiceSetting. |
| Content filter | `gifContentFilter` | choice | Medium | keep | parents and anyone who wants G-rated results | ok |  | Maps to each provider's own rating. Medium is the safe middle. No reset control: bare ChoiceControl (MainActivity.kt:10032). |
| Results for each search | `gifResultLimit` | slider | 24 (range 6–48) | demote | metered-data users; power users wanting a deeper grid | ok |  | The panel does not paginate, so this is the only way past 24. Real but niche; docs currently deny it exists. |
| Sticker packs | `-` | nav | — | keep | anyone building their own packs | - |  | Only route to pack management; an IME has no window, so every edit has to live in the app. |
| New pack / Import a pack / Stickers addon row | `-` | action | — | keep | pack builders and .wmstickers sharers | - |  | Three ways in: create empty, import a ZIP, or install from an addon repo. Cap of 50 packs. |
| Per-pack export / delete | `-` | action | — | keep | users backing up or sharing a pack | - |  | Export writes a .wmstickers ZIP; delete confirms with a sticker count first. |
| Rename pack | `-` | text | pack name | keep | pack owners | - |  | The pack name is also a search term in the My stickers grid, so it is not cosmetic. |
| Add stickers | `-` | action | — | keep | pack builders | - |  | Photo picker, up to 30 per trip; one still image detours through the editor, a batch does not. Cap 200 per pack. |
| Sticker search words | `-` | text | empty | keep | users with packs too big to scroll | - |  | One field, not name plus tags: the first word doubles as the screen-reader name (StickerSearchWords). |
| Move up / Move down / Move to pack / Delete sticker | `-` | action | — | keep | pack owners organising | - |  | One-step reorder buttons inside a dialog. Clumsy for a 200-sticker pack, but it is the only ordering control. |
| Edit the image | `-` | action | — | keep | users refining a cut-out later | - |  | Hidden, not disabled, for animated stickers: there is no still to edit. Reopens the kept original, not the flattened sticker. |
| Editor mode (Crop / Erase / Restore / Border) | `-` | choice | Crop (Erase on a resumed edit) | keep | sticker makers | ok |  | Resumed edits open on Erase because a stray pan in Crop is the one gesture that throws the mask away. |
| Brush size | `-` | slider | 32 px (range 8–96) | keep | sticker makers doing fine edge work | ok |  | Persisted per sticker in StickerEditState so a resumed edit keeps the brush. Ring preview shows the size before you paint. |
| Border width | `-` | slider | 0 px (range 0–24) | keep | users wanting the WhatsApp-style outline | ok |  | 0 is off, which is right: a border on an uncropped photo is noise. Sliding back to 0 is its own undo. |
| Border colour | `-` | choice | White | keep | users on dark chat backgrounds | ok |  | Only two options, which is the whole design: the border must read on both light and dark chat bubbles. |
| Remove the background | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone cutting a subject out of a photo | - |  | Full build only; ML Kit model fetched on demand with a progress bar. Absent, not greyed, in lite. |
| Undo | `-` | action | — | keep | sticker makers | - |  | Twelve alpha snapshots deep, raster work only; the border is a parameter and resets itself. |
