**Missing here:** Emoji panel grid density (Cell min-size fixed at 44dp; tablet and large-phone users get huge gaps, small-screen users get cramped rows.); Clear emoji history (recents and counts) (Clear-all only exists as an in-panel button gated behind emojiClearRecentsButton; no action anywhere in Settings.); Emoji recents length (MAX_RECENTS hard-coded at 32; a tablet history tab shows a full grid and then stops arbitrarily.); GIF/sticker grid columns (GridCells.Fixed(3) everywhere; three columns of thumbnails is small on a phone and wasteful on a tablet.)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/emoji/gifs.mdx` — wrong: Line 75 states "24 results per fetch, not adjustable. Both providers return a fixed page of results". A "Results for each search" slider (6–48, default 24) exists at MainActivity.kt:10048, backed by gifResultLimit.
- `docs/src/content/docs/emoji/gifs.mdx` — gap: The Options table (lines 60–67) lists six rows but omits the "Results for each search" slider that sits on the same tool page.
- `docs/src/content/docs/emoji/stickers.mdx` — gap: The Options list (lines 105–110) omits the "Results for each search" slider, which is shown on the sticker tool page too (shared GIF/STICKER branch).
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/emoji.mdx` — gap: The Emoji style section (lines 127–138) documents only the font choice and Hide-unrenderable toggle. It omits the "Get the newest emoji" / "New emoji are available" download-and-update card (EmojiFontPreview.kt:110), which is the actual fix for missing emoji.
- `docs/src/content/docs/emoji/customization.mdx` — stale: "Fixing emoji that won't display" (line 42) tells users to switch to Google, but the code's own comment says that choice can only serve the build the device already has and cannot be relied on for new emoji. The direct-download row that actually fixes it is undocumented.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/emoji.mdx` — gap: Neither the Emoji style group nor the Emoji keywords section mentions the Addons store rows that sit in both (MainActivity.kt:6117 and 8024).
- `MISSING: reset-to-default affordance on media choice rows` — gap: No docs page states that Multiple sources and Content filter are the only emoji/media rows with no reset control, while every other row gains one once changed. Reference pages imply uniform behaviour.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/SettingsUi.kt:673 — ResetSetting, the per-row restore icon, drawn only while the value differs from SettingsDefaults`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:5819 — emojiToolbar (and every other Emoji-screen row passes `default =`: 5828, 5839, 5855, 5875, 5884, 5895, 5909, 5918, 5927, 5936, 5945, 5954, 5980, 5996, 6007, 6016, 6060, 6131)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:8021 — emoji.autoDownloadKeywords on the Emoji keywords screen`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:9067 — emojiToolbar on the Emoji tool page`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:9951 — mediaFullBleed; 10002 stickerSendMode; 10014 gifSendMode; 10054 gifResultLimit`, `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:6585 — forgetInstalledEmojiFont, an implicit reset to SYSTEM when the selected emoji font is deleted`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/StickerEditorScreen.kt:389 — border width slider back to 0 is its own undo (documented as such); StickerEditorScreen.kt:450 resetMask clears the whole cut-out after a confirm`, `NO reset control: app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:10020 (gifSourceMode) and 10032 (gifContentFilter) use the bare ChoiceControl instead of ChoiceSetting, so neither gets the restore icon`

_Coverage: Read in full: SettingsRepository.kt EmojiSettings (2497–2588), EmojiFontSettings/EmojiFontChoice/EmojiInsertMode/EmojiSkinTone (508–558), and every emoji/gif/sticker flat field plus its KDoc (985–1000, 1124, 1189, 1264, 1310–1334, 1399–1402, 1483–1519); MainActivity.kt 5797–6191 (EmojiSettings + InstalledEmojiFontList — the assigned range 5597–5981 starts inside LanguageSettings and ends mid-screen, so I extended both ends), 7863–8150 (EmojiKeywordSettings), 9061–9077 (Emoji tool page), 9931–10058 (GIF/sticker tool page, not in the assigned range but named in the settings-fields brief); StickerPackScreens.kt and StickerEditorScreen.kt in full; EmojiFontPreview.kt:100–194; LanguageSettingsScreens.kt:890–1010 (EmojiDictRow); OnboardingPages.kt:790–849 (the wizard's emoji-font page); all six assigned docs pages in full. Verified against code: kaomoji 201/15 groups and emoticons 111/8 groups, 125 dict-catalog languages, MAX_PACKS 50, MAX_STICKERS_PER_PACK 200, the declined-pack marker. NOT verified: picker.mdx's catalog counts (1,914 base emoji / 330 toned / 19 dual-tone) — I did not parse assets/emoji/catalog.tsv. NOT covered (other agents' domains, flagged only where docs cross-reference them): globeAsEmoji and commaAsEmoji under Layout, inlineEmojiSearch under Typing, powerSaving.dropEmojiPrediction, barOrder under Rows & bars, and the sticker section of the settings backup._

### Toolbar and tools (A)

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Show the toolbar | `toolbarBehavior.enabled` | toggle | on | keep | small-screen users who want the strip's height back for keys | ok |  | KDoc: off hides suggestions and every pinned tool, so it is guarded by a confirm dialog. On is the only safe default. |
| Swipe down to hide | `toolbarBehavior.swipeDownHide` | toggle | off | keep | one-handed users dismissing the keyboard without reaching Back | ok |  | KDoc: off so the gesture never surprises anyone reordering or scrolling the bar. Correct — the bar is a drag surface. |
| Only the toolbar with a hardware keyboard | `toolbarBehavior.onlyWithHardwareKeyboard` | toggle | off | keep | tablet users with a case keyboard who still want emoji/clipboard | ok |  | KDoc: off keeps the platform's usual behaviour. Niche but the only way to get tools without the key grid. |
| Reverse the order for right-to-left languages | `toolbarBehavior.reverseForRtl` | toggle | on | AUTOMATE | nobody — the layout's script direction is already known at draw time | ok |  | The app knows the script. An override exists for muscle memory nobody has reported; drop the row, keep the behaviour. |
| Spread the tools across the toolbar | `toolbarBehavior.greedy` | toggle | on | keep | people who pin 2-3 tools and want them left-packed, Gboard style | ok |  | Visible layout difference with only three tools pinned by default. Forced off by the scrollable toggle, which is handled. |
| Toolbar height | `toolbarHeightDp` | slider | 44dp (32-80) | keep | small-phone users reclaiming rows; large-finger users needing bigger targets | ok |  | 44dp is the Material minimum touch target. Range covers both directions sensibly. |
| Scroll the toolbar | `toolbarBehavior.scrollable` | toggle | off | keep | people who pin more tools than fit at a tappable size | ok |  | KDoc says exactly that. Off is right: with three pinned tools a scrollable bar would be pointless. |
| Hide the toolbar and the clipboard on the lock screen | `toolbarBehavior.hideWhenLocked` | toggle | off | keep | anyone replying to notifications from the lock screen with codes in history | ok |  | KDoc: locked or not the keyboard looks the same. Real privacy win but a real feature loss, so opt-in is right. |
| Tool labels | `toolbarLabels` | toggle | off | keep | new users who can't read 60 unfamiliar tool glyphs | ok |  | Labels cost vertical space on a 44dp bar. Off is right for the default three well-known tools. |
| Label text size | `toolbarLabelSize` | slider | 10sp (7-14) | demote | low-vision users who turned labels on | ok |  | Only appears with labels on; a 7sp floor is unreadable and 14sp will not fit a 44dp bar. Narrow real range. |
| Reset pinned tools | `toolbarTools` | action | Emoji, Clipboard, Settings | keep | anyone who dragged their bar into a mess and wants out | - |  | Only escape hatch from a drag-only pinning model. Confirmed by dialog; always resets the global row. |
| Tool circle radius | `toolCircleRadiusDp` | slider | 20dp (0-20) | keep | people who want bare icons or a squared Gboard-style strip | ok |  | 20 is a full circle at the default 38dp width. 0 removes the background and correctly hides the shape row. |
| Tool background shape | `toolShape` | nav | Rounded | demote | theme tinkerers matching the tool background to their key shape | ok |  | Cosmetic-only and already gated behind radius > 0. Shares the key-shape picker, so it costs nothing to keep. |
| Tool background width | `toolbarBehavior.toolWidthDp` | slider | 38dp (38-64) | demote | people building a pill-shaped Gboard-style strip | ok |  | KDoc: 38 is the classic circle, wider stretches to a pill. Range floor equals the default, so it only widens. |
| Toolbox layout | `toolbox.layout` | choice | Icons | keep | users who can't read 60 icon captions and want named rows | ok |  | KDoc: pills are readable without squinting but fewer fit. Icons is right for the density the toolbox needs. |
| Toolbox grid size | `toolboxColumns` | slider | 4 per row (3-6) | keep | tablet users fitting 6, large-finger users dropping to 3 | ok |  | 4 per row is the phone-width sweet spot; range is tight and every value is usable. |
| Pills per row | `toolbox.pillColumns` | slider | 2 (1-3) | demote | pills users in landscape (3) or with long tool names (1) | ok |  | Only shown in Pills layout, which is itself opt-in. Doubly nested, so cheap to keep. |
| Fill the pills with the tool color | `toolbox.pillFilled` | toggle | off | demote | pills users who want colour blocks instead of tinted glyphs | ok |  | KDoc: only has effect while coloredToolIcons is on. Twice-nested cosmetic switch; off keeps the panel calm. |
| Swipe the toolbox in pages | `toolbox.paginate` | toggle | off | keep | users who want a tool at the same spot on the same page every time | ok |  | Real trade (muscle memory vs reach) but scrolling is the safer default with a variable-length tool list. |
| Tools per page | `toolbox.pageSize` | slider | 12 (4-40) | demote | paging users matching page size to their row size | ok |  | KDoc: ceiling is past what fits any phone because a tall page just scrolls. Range is honest but the top half is useless. |
| Colorful tool icons | `coloredToolIcons` | toggle | on | keep | users of monochrome/minimal themes who want one flat foreground | ok |  | Colour is how a 60-icon toolbox stays scannable. Gates three other rows, which is handled by nesting. |
| Gradient tool colors | `toolIconGradients` | toggle | off | demote | theme tinkerers who want two-tone glyphs | ok |  | Purely decorative and doubles the per-tool colour rows from one to two. Correctly nested under coloredToolIcons. |
| Reset custom colors | `toolColorOverrides` | action | n/a | keep | anyone who recoloured tools one by one and wants them all back | - |  | Only bulk undo for a per-tool override map. Correctly hidden until at least one override exists. |
| Enabled (per tool, list switch) | `enabledTools` | toggle | all 63 on (pre-onboarding) | keep | everyone — 63 tools in one toolbox is unusable unpruned | split |  | Onboarding overwrites this with MinimalTools/RecommendedTools/PowerTools, so the all-on constructor default is only reached by a settings reset. |
| Enable (tool detail screen) | `enabledTools` | toggle | same as list | keep | users who arrived via search or a deep link, not the list | ok |  | Deliberate duplicate of the list switch; shares the element for the flight animation. Not a second setting. |
| Icon colour / Icon colour (start) | `toolColorOverrides` | nav | tool's built-in accent | demote | colour-coders who want their pinned tools to match | ok |  | Per-tool cosmetic override, gated on coloredToolIcons and resettable from inside its own picker dialog. |
| Icon colour (end) | `toolColorEndOverrides` | nav | derived from the start colour | demote | gradient users pinning the far end of one tool's gradient | ok |  | Only shown with gradients on. Default tracks the near end so the pair moves together until pinned — good design. |
| Trigger words | `toolKeywords` | text | per-tool list e.g. calc, wiki, qr | keep | non-English typists who want their own word to open a tool | ok |  | Only shown for tools shipping a default keyword. Comma list with a visible reset row when edited. |
| Match capitals | `toolKeywordCase` | toggle | off for every tool | keep | AI-tool users tired of the word "ai" offering a chip | WRONG → on for AI (short, ambiguous keyword) |  | SmartSuggest's own KDoc argues literal matching so "AI" works and "Ai" doesn't, yet toolKeywordCase ships empty — nothing is case-sensitive. |
| Sort order | `launcher.sortOrder` | choice | Alphabetical | keep | people who relaunch the same three apps all day | ok |  | Alphabetical is stable and learnable; recent-first moves under your finger. Recents already get their own leading row. |
| App names | `launcher.showLabels` | toggle | on | keep | anyone who can't identify an app from a 40dp icon | ok |  | Off is a real density win for people who know their icons; on is right for everyone else. |
| Recent apps | `launcher.recentsEnabled` | toggle | on | keep | privacy-minded users who don't want launch history recorded | ok |  | Tracks launches locally and leads the grid. Off is the privacy escape hatch; on is the useful default. |
| Open screens inside apps | `launcher.activityDrilldown` | toggle | on | demote | power users jumping straight to a deep screen in an app | ok |  | Long-press only, so it costs nothing when unused. Off exists mainly to stop accidental long-press surprises. |
| Show private screens | `launcher.showNonExported` | toggle | off | demote | Android developers poking at another app's activity list | ok |  | KDoc: they fail with SecurityException when tapped, so they are debugging fare. Correctly off and correctly hidden by default. |
| Manage plugins | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | plugin users — this is the only route to the manager | - |  | Entry point, not a setting. One of only two tool screens that navigate away instead of managing inline. |
| Emoji button in the toolbar | `emojiToolbar` | toggle | on | keep | users who reach emoji via the globe/spacebar swipe and want the slot back | ok |  | Emoji is one of only three tools pinned out of the box; on matches every other keyboard's behaviour. |
| All emoji settings | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | anyone who reached the emoji tool from Tools rather than Emoji | - |  | Cross-link into the emoji domain; the emoji screen is where skin tone, fonts and the row actually live. |
| Snippets list (add/edit/delete/import/export) | `-` | list | empty | keep | anyone who types the same address or reply repeatedly | - |  | Full inline manager backed by snippets/snippets.json, loaded off the main thread. Not a toggle — the tool is the list. |
| Clipboard history | `clipboard.history` | toggle | on | keep | privacy-strict users who want nothing copied retained | ok |  | Master capture switch. On is the whole point of the tool; off is the one-switch privacy answer. |
| Suggest the last copy | `clipboard.suggestRecent` | toggle | on | keep | anyone pasting a code or link right after copying it | ok |  | Gboard-style paste chip on the strip. Costs one chip slot for a few minutes; the highest-value clipboard feature. |
| Suggest copied codes in code boxes | `clipboard.suggestCodesInCodeFields` | toggle | on | keep | anyone pasting an SMS OTP into a verification field | ok |  | KDoc reasons it precisely: in a code box nothing else is being typed, so hiding the code only forces a panel detour. |
| Pop-up when you copy | `feedback.toastOnCopy` | toggle | off | merge | users of fields that give no visual copy confirmation | ok |  | It is a feedback setting about the keyboard's own copy shortcuts, not clipboard history — the docs already apologise for it sitting here. |
| Clipboard expiry | `clipboard.expiryHours` | slider | 24h (0-168, 0=never) | keep | privacy users wanting a short leash; hoarders wanting a week | ok |  | 24h covers the everyday "I copied that this morning" case without keeping a week of secrets. Pinning exempts a clip. |
| Maximum entries | `clipboard.maxItems` | slider | 100 (5-500) | keep | heavy copiers who blow past 100 inside the expiry window | ok |  | KDoc: the other half of the bound expiry sets — a panel 500 long is a haystack, not history. |
| Bottom control row | `clipboard.bottomRow` | toggle | off | keep | people who paste then immediately keep typing | ok |  | Costs a row of history cards. Off is right; the back header already gets you out of the panel. |
| Full-screen clipboard panel | `clipboard.fullBleed` | toggle | on | keep | users who want the toolbar still reachable from inside the panel | ok |  | KDoc: the panel pays for the toolbar's row with its own back header, and more cards on screen makes picking easier. |
| Pinned entries last | `clipboard.pinnedLast` | toggle | off | demote | users with many pins who mostly want the fresh copy | ok |  | Pure ordering preference with no downside either way; matters only once you have several pins. |
| Search bar | `clipboard.search` | toggle | off | keep | anyone with 100 clips looking for one from yesterday | split |  | With maxItems defaulting to 100, search is how you find anything; but the pill costs panel height most people never need. |
| Detect codes, numbers and links | `clipboard.detectEntities` | toggle | on | keep | anyone extracting an OTP from a full SMS | ok |  | Runs on-device, adds chips above the grid, and its detector is documented as conservative (year/currency/# guards). |
| Phone number formats | `clipboard.phoneFormats` | nav | empty (catch everything) | keep | users whose invoice totals keep wearing a phone-number chip | ok |  | KDoc: empty is all the detector can do before it knows the country, and also where its false positives come from. |
| Forget after you paste a password | `clipboard.clearAfterPasswordPaste` | toggle | on | keep | everyone who pastes from a password manager | ok |  | KDoc: it would otherwise sit there readable by every app until it expired. Purges history and the system clipboard. |
| Link previews | `clipboard.linkPreviews` | toggle | off | keep | people who copy many links and can't tell them apart | ok |  | Makes a direct unauthenticated request to the copied site the moment the panel opens. Off is the only defensible default. |
| Your screenshots | `clipboard.userScreenshots` | toggle | off | keep | people who screenshot then immediately share the image | ok |  | Needs READ_MEDIA_IMAGES and turns the panel into a photo feed. Off, with the grant prompted on first enable. |
| The app needs the storage permission | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | users who denied the prompt and need a way back to it | - |  | Conditional recovery row read through rememberGrantState so it vanishes on return. Not a preference. |
| Show the source app | `clipboard.trackSource` | toggle | off | demote | few — people who copy the same text from several apps | ok |  | KDoc: needs Usage Access and is a best-effort guess of the foreground app. Heavy grant for one line in a long-press popup. |
| The app needs the Usage Access permission | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | users who enabled source tracking but skipped the grant | - |  | Conditional recovery row behind the prominent-disclosure wrapper. Not a preference. |
| Sensitive clips | `clipboard.sensitiveHandling` | choice | Short-lived (hide and forget) | keep | strict users choosing never-save; relaxed users choosing keep | ok |  | KDoc: deliberately the middle option — never-save trades away the everyday wrong-field recovery for a risk a 5-minute expiry closes. |
| Let the keyboard find them | `clipboard.detectSensitive` | toggle | on | keep | users of password managers that predate Android 13's sensitive flag | ok |  | KDoc: most managers still predate the flag and a hand-copied code carries none. Correctly hidden when handling is Keep. |
| Forget sensitive clips after | `clipboard.sensitiveExpiryMinutes` | slider | 5 min (1-120) | keep | users who want a 1-minute leash or a full session's grace | ok |  | KDoc: never capped by the ordinary expiry, so a short leash holds even when history keeps everything. Shown only for Short-lived. |
| Split gap | `splitGapPercent` | slider | 12% (5-40) | keep | tablet users whose thumbs don't land where a 12% gap puts the keys | ok |  | Hand span varies enormously and the gap is the whole point of split mode. Range covers phone to large tablet. |
| Keyboard size & position (nav) | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | users who opened Split expecting height/padding sliders | - |  | Cross-link to Layout, where the rest of split sizing lives. Cheap and prevents a dead-end screen. |
| Floating keyboard width | `floatingWidthDp` | slider | 320dp (240-500) | keep | tablet users who want a narrow floating board they can reach | ok |  | 320dp is a typical phone width, which is the right size for a floating board on a tablet. |
| Keyboard size & position (nav) | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | users who opened Floating expecting the rest of the sizing controls | - |  | Same cross-link pattern as Split. Prevents a one-slider dead end. |
| Turn off with the keyboard | `flashlightAutoOff` | toggle | on | keep | people who light a room from the keyboard then put the phone down | ok |  | A torch silently left on drains the battery; on is right. Off is a genuine use case, so the switch earns its place. |
| Degree readout | `compassShowDegrees` | toggle | on | REMOVE | nobody — a compass without a bearing is strictly worse | ok |  | No KDoc justification. The numeric bearing is the reason to open a compass rather than look at the sky. |
| Show the qibla | `compassShowQibla` | toggle | off | keep | Muslim users finding prayer direction from the keyboard | ok |  | High value to a large, specific group and meaningless to everyone else. Correctly off, and it warns when no location is saved. |
| Angle readout | `levelShowAngles` | toggle | on | REMOVE | nobody — a bubble level without degrees is a decoration | ok |  | Same shape as the compass degree toggle: no KDoc reason, and hiding the number removes the tool's only output. |
| Redo sends Ctrl+Y | `redoUsesCtrlY` | toggle | off | keep | users of apps that only accept the Windows-style Ctrl+Y redo | ok |  | Ctrl+Shift+Z is the Android/Compose convention; Ctrl+Y is the fallback some editors need. Cannot be detected from EditorInfo. |
| Southern hemisphere | `moonSouthernHemisphere` | toggle | off | AUTOMATE | southern-hemisphere users — but they should never have to say so | WRONG → derived from saved latitude / device region |  | weatherLatitude is already stored for Weather and Qibla; its sign answers this. Derive it and keep the toggle as an override. |
| Weather location | `weatherLatitude/Longitude/PlaceName` | text | unset | keep | everyone using Weather, and Compass's qibla depends on it | ok |  | Geocode search plus manual coordinates, no location permission needed. Unset default is the privacy-correct start. |
| Fahrenheit | `weatherFahrenheit` | toggle | off (Celsius) | AUTOMATE | US/Liberia/Myanmar users — who currently get Celsius silently | WRONG → region-derived (°F in US, LR, MM) |  | Calendar's alt calendars and weekend are already region-seeded in the same repository; temperature unit is the same class of guess. |
| First calendar | `calendarAltOne` | nav | region-derived (Bengali in BD, Hijri in AE…, else None) | keep | anyone whose civil or religious calendar is not Gregorian | ok |  | defaultAltCalendars seeds from region; KDoc notes two extra numbers per cell are noise to someone who reads neither. |
| Second calendar | `calendarAltTwo` | nav | region-derived (Hijri in BD/JP-Chinese, else None) | keep | users tracking both a civil and a religious calendar | ok |  | Same region seeding. Only the first slot's day number rides inside the cell, so the second is genuinely secondary. |
| Weekend | `calendarWeekend` | nav | region-derived, SAT_SUN fallback | keep | anyone in a country whose working week isn't Mon-Fri | ok |  | KDoc: a best guess only — several countries changed recently (UAE, 2022), which is exactly why it is a setting. |
| Hijri day adjustment | `hijriAdjustDays` | slider | 0 (-2..+2) | keep | Muslim users matching their local moon-sighting authority | ok |  | Tabular Hijri drifts a day or two from sighting-based calendars; 0 is the arithmetic truth and ±2 covers real drift. Shown only when Hijri is picked. |
| Start with the selfie camera | `camera.preferFront` | toggle | off | keep | people who mostly send selfies from the keyboard | ok |  | Rear camera is the general-purpose default; the in-panel flip button covers the occasional selfie either way. |
| Mirror selfies | `camera.mirrorFront` | toggle | on | keep | people who want text in a selfie to read the right way round | ok |  | KDoc: mirror so the photo matches the preview. On matches what the user just saw; off matches what others see. |
| Send the whole photo | `camera.fullFrame` | toggle | off | demote | users who keep losing the top of a document to the viewfinder crop | ok |  | KDoc: off keeps what-you-see-is-what-you-send, which is the safer promise. On recovers the 4:3 slivers. |
| Save to the gallery | `camera.saveToGallery` | toggle | off | keep | people who want a copy of what they shot from the keyboard | ok |  | Off avoids silently filling Pictures/WM Keyboard with throwaway shots. Easy to discover on the tool's own screen. |
| Shutter sound | `camera.shutterSound` | toggle | on | keep | people shooting in a meeting who want silence | ok |  | On matches every stock camera app and confirms the capture fired. Off is a legitimate, obvious want. |
| Haptics | `camera.haptics` | toggle | on | merge | few — people who want camera buzz separate from keyboard buzz | ok |  | Duplicates the global Sound & haptics preference for one panel. A user who turned keyboard haptics off almost certainly wants these off too. |
| Look up the word at the cursor | `dictionaryAutoLookup` | toggle | on | keep | users who open the dictionary to look up something else | ok |  | Saves a step in the common case (you tapped Dictionary because of the word you're on) and off costs one tap. |
| Key repeat interval | `textEditing.repeatMs` | slider | 60ms (30-200) | demote | users who overshoot the cursor on a held arrow key | ok |  | 60ms is a standard repeat rate. Real but rarely-touched accessibility knob; nothing else in the app exposes it. |
| Calculator layout | `numpadCalculatorLayout` | toggle | off (phone-style, 123 on top) | keep | anyone whose muscle memory comes from a desktop numpad | split |  | Genuinely split: Android's own numeric keypads put 123 on top, desktop numpads put 789. Not worth an onboarding question — the Numpad tool is off for most personas. |
| Pause learning | `incognitoPausesLearning` | toggle | on | demote | few — people who want incognito for clipboard only | ok |  | Not learning is most of what incognito means; turning it off leaves a mode that barely does anything. Keep, but it is near-dead weight. |
| Pause clipboard capture | `incognitoPausesClipboard` | toggle | on | demote | few — people who want incognito for learning only | ok |  | Same shape as Pause learning. Together the two split one coherent mode into a matrix most users will never reason about. |
| Follow private browsing | `autoIncognito` | toggle | on | keep | everyone typing in a Chrome incognito tab or a password-manager note | ok |  | Honours IME_FLAG_NO_PERSONALIZED_LEARNING, which apps set precisely to ask for this. On is the respectful default. |

**Missing here:** Paste-chip lifetime (Clipboard paste chip auto-hides after a fixed 5 minutes; users want it shorter (privacy) or until dismissed.); Recent apps kept (LauncherToolSettings.MAX_RECENTS is hard-coded at 10; grid width is 3-6, so 10 never fills clean rows.); Remember camera self-timer (timerSeconds is panel-local state reset to 0 on every open, so a habitual 3s user re-picks it each time.); Toolbox order reset ("Reset pinned tools" restores the bar but nothing restores DefaultToolOrder after a bad drag session in the grid.)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/tools.mdx` — wrong: Says "62 tools" throughout (title description, intro, headings) and "Lite ships 57 of the 62". ToolbarTool now has 63 entries — RESIZE was added by commit 238966fd and never counted. Should read 63 and 58 of 63.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/tools.mdx` — wrong: Line 50: "Exactly five are toggle-only ... Settings, Themes, Sound & haptics, One-handed mode, Media controls." ToolsWithoutOptions (MainActivity.kt:8555) has six — Resize is also toggle-only.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/tools.mdx` — wrong: Groups table (lines 36-46) is out of date against ToolGroups (MainActivity.kt:8849): Panels is missing App launcher, Keyboard modes is missing Resize.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/tools.mdx` — wrong: Line 134: "All 62 tools start enabled. A fresh install has every tool switched on." Onboarding writes MinimalTools (8), RecommendedTools (21) or PowerTools (35) via setEnabledTools (OnboardingPages.kt:1183); the all-on default is only reached by a settings reset.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/tools.mdx` — gap: The Tools screen's "Gradient tool colors" toggle (toolIconGradients) and the resulting start/end pair of per-tool colour rows are undocumented; line 24 and line 59 describe only the single "Icon colour" row.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/overview.mdx` — wrong: Line 61: toggle-only list reads "Unit converter, Settings, Themes, Sound & haptics, One-handed, Media controls". Unit converter is NOT in ToolsWithoutOptions (it has its own options), and Resize is missing. Contradicts tools.mdx too.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/overview.mdx` — wrong: Line 79: "WM Keyboard exposes 37 of its 62 tools" during direct boot. isDirectBootSafeTool now returns true for 38 of 63 — Resize was added to the safe set and is missing from the enumerated list.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/overview.mdx` — wrong: "62 tools" appears at lines 14, 57 and 116 ("lite ships 57 of the 62"); should be 63 and 58 of 63.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/overview.mdx` — gap: The Options list (lines 95-111) omits two rows the Appearance/Toolbar section actually draws: "Tool background width" (toolbarBehavior.toolWidthDp, 38dp, 38-64) and "Tool background shape" (toolShape, shown when radius > 0).
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/overview.mdx` — gap: Line 116 attributes all gating to ML Kit / lite. Document scanner is additionally hidden on any device without Play services (isSupportedTool, ToolbarTool.kt:121), full build included.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/clipboard.mdx` — gap: The Options list omits "Full-screen clipboard panel" (clipboard.fullBleed) entirely — and it defaults ON, so the panel hiding the toolbar is undocumented default behaviour.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/clipboard.mdx` — gap: The Options list omits "Suggest copied codes in code boxes" (clipboard.suggestCodesInCodeFields, on by default, nested under Suggest recent), "Pop-up when you copy" (only in edge cases), and the "Phone number formats" nav row.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/clipboard.mdx` — stale: Line 12: "one of only three tools pinned to the toolbar out of the box." True on phones; tablets get SmallTabletToolbarTools (5) or LargeTabletToolbarTools (7) via applyDeviceForm.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/media.mdx` — wrong: Line 40: "Enabled — on by default, along with every other tool." MEDIA_CONTROL is only in PowerTools; the Minimal and Recommended onboarding personas leave it off.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/calendar.mdx` — wrong: Line 65: "Calendar ships enabled by default." Calendar is only in PowerTools, so it is off for the Minimal and Recommended personas — the same sentence then correctly says it is left out of recommended, contradicting itself.
- `MISSING: Toolbox order reset / per-tool colour gradient pair` — gap: No page documents that there is no way to restore DefaultToolOrder after rearranging the toolbox grid, while "Reset pinned tools" is documented prominently. Readers reasonably assume the reset covers both.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2080 — ResetSetting inside ToggleSetting: any row given `default =` shows an inline reset control whenever the value differs; applies to every toggle/slider/choice in this domain`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2300 — ResetPinnedToolsSetting: "Reset pinned tools" with a confirm dialog, restores DefaultToolbarTools (drawn at 4959)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:8756 — "Reset custom colors" TextButton on the Tools screen; clears toolColorOverrides + toolColorEndOverrides, shown only when at least one override exists`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11330 — ToolColourRow's picker dialog has an onReset that writes null, putting one tool's icon colour back to its built-in accent`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11397 — ToolKeywordSetting's "Reset to defaults" row, shown only when the saved keywords differ from SmartSuggest.defaultKeywords, with the defaults as its subtitle`

_Coverage: Read in full: MainActivity.kt 8325-9621 (my assigned range, extended slightly to close the INCOGNITO block at 9621 — POWER_SAVING starts at 9622 and belongs to tools-b). Also read the helper composables that range calls out of line: ToolColourRow (11300), ToolKeywordSetting (11344), AltCalendarSetting (11557), WeekendSetting (11609), WeatherLocationSetting (11824), SnippetSettings (12107), ToggleSetting/ResetSetting (1990-2100), ResetPinnedToolsSetting (2300). Additionally covered the toolbar-level settings the brief named, which live OUTSIDE my line range in the Appearance screen at MainActivity.kt 4858-5090 (ToolbarBehavior + ToolboxSettings + toolbarHeight/labels/circle/shape) — these may overlap with an appearance-domain agent. Read the KDoc for ToolbarBehavior/ToolboxSettings (SettingsRepository.kt 585-700), ClipboardSettings + SensitiveClipHandling (2348-2490), CameraSettings/TextEditingSettings/LauncherToolSettings (2055-2290), and every flat field in range (coloredToolIcons, toolIconGradients, emojiToolbar, splitGapPercent, floatingWidthDp, flashlightAutoOff, compass*, levelShowAngles, redoUsesCtrlY, moonSouthernHemisphere, weather*, calendar*, hijriAdjustDays, dictionaryAutoLookup, numpadCalculatorLayout, incognito*, autoIncognito, feedback.toastOnCopy). Also read ToolbarTool.kt in full, ToolAvailability.kt, CalendarDefaults.kt, SmartSuggest.defaultKeywords/caseSensitiveKeyword. Docs: read all six assigned pages in full. Re-scanned 8325-9621 a second time; the only additions on the second pass were the two conditional permission nav rows in CLIPBOARD and the CaptionText-only blocks (weather/calendar/camera/dictionary/incognito info captions, which are not settings). NOT covered, by design: the 8325-8539 slice of my range is FontPickerSection/FontChoiceRow/importFontFile (fonts domain, no tool settings in it); SnippetSettings' internal add/edit/import/export dialogs are reported as one \"list\" entry rather than itemised (snippets domain); handwriting's own three settings live at 9854-9885 (tools-b) though I did verify handwriting.mdx against them and found it accurate — stylus-only off, auto-space on, pause 700ms over 300-2000ms all match, so no docs issue is filed for that page._

### Tools (B)

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Icon colour (top left) | `toolColorOverrides[tool]` | nav | tool's own accent | demote | people who colour-code their toolbox | ok |  | Only drawn when the global colourful-icons switch is on; a per-tool override of a per-tool accent is deep customisation. |
| Icon colour, bottom right | `toolColorEndOverrides[tool]` | nav | derived from the start colour | demote | gradient tinkerers only | ok |  | Second row appears only with tool icon gradients on. Default tracks the near end, so the pair moves together until pinned. |
| Trigger words | `toolKeywords` | text | per-tool list from SmartSuggest.defaultKeywords | keep | anyone who reaches tools by typing rather than tapping | ok |  | Editable keyword is also the only way to stop a tool hijacking a real word; clearing the field disables the chip. |
| Match capitals | `toolKeywordCase` | toggle | off (case-insensitive) | demote | someone whose custom trigger is an acronym | ok |  | Shipped keywords are ordinary lowercase words, so this row is inert for almost everyone; it earns its place only after a custom trigger. |
| Pause clipboard capture | `incognitoPausesClipboard` | toggle | on | keep | anyone using incognito who does not want copies logged | ok |  | Incognito that still records clips would surprise people; on is the only defensible default for a privacy mode. |
| Follow private browsing | `autoIncognito` | toggle | on | keep | Chrome incognito and password-manager users | ok |  | Honours IME_FLAG_NO_PERSONALIZED_LEARNING. Off would mean the keyboard learns from fields that explicitly asked it not to. |
| Power saving now | `powerSaving.manual` | toggle | off | keep | anyone on a dying battery | ok |  | This is the tool's actual switch, persisted like incognito so it survives the IME being rebuilt between fields. |
| Turn on by itself | `powerSaving.trigger` | choice | Android's battery saver | keep | people who already use system battery saver | ok |  | KDoc: it only fires because the user already asked the system to save power, so the change is never a surprise threshold. |
| Low battery is | `powerSaving.batteryPercent` | slider | 20% | keep | people who pick the battery-level trigger | ok |  | Only shown for the two battery triggers. 20% matches Android's own low-battery notion, so it needs no explaining. |
| Off while charging | `powerSaving.offWhileCharging` | toggle | on | keep | anyone who charges while typing | ok |  | Nothing to save with power flowing in. Manual switch is deliberately unaffected. |
| Key vibration | `powerSaving.dropHaptics` | toggle | on | merge | nobody picks this individually; the group as a whole helps | ok |  | Twelve individual drop switches is the definition of settings bloat. Collapse to Light/Balanced/Aggressive with an advanced expander. |
| Key sounds | `powerSaving.dropKeySound` | toggle | on | merge | nobody individually | ok |  | Same as key vibration: belongs in a preset, not its own row. |
| Animations | `powerSaving.dropAnimations` | toggle | on | merge | nobody individually | ok |  | Reuses the reduce-motion flag, so it can surprise someone who never touched accessibility; still preset material. |
| Glide trail | `powerSaving.dropGlideTrail` | toggle | on | merge | nobody individually | ok |  | Cheap half of the glide trade-off; drops the overlay while decoding keeps working. Preset material. |
| Key popup | `powerSaving.dropKeyPopup` | toggle | off | REMOVE | nobody | ok |  | KDoc admits the popup is cheap to draw, so the row ships off and saves nothing measurable when turned on. |
| Glide typing | `powerSaving.dropGestureTyping` | toggle | off | demote | someone squeezing the last hour out of a phone | ok |  | The one genuine trade-off here: swipe decoding is the most expensive thing the keyboard does, but it is why many installed it. |
| Emoji suggestions | `powerSaving.dropEmojiPrediction` | toggle | on | merge | nobody individually | ok |  | Preset material; nobody arrives at this screen wanting emoji prediction back but haptics gone. |
| Smart chips | `powerSaving.dropSmartChips` | toggle | on | merge | nobody individually | ok |  | Per-word scanning for sums and keywords is real cost, but the choice belongs in a preset. |
| Background network | `powerSaving.dropBackgroundNetwork` | toggle | on | demote | metered-data users, not only battery savers | ok |  | The only drop switch with a second reason to exist (data, not power); it silently overrides three other settings elsewhere. |
| Screenshot watching | `powerSaving.dropScreenshotWatch` | toggle | on | merge | nobody individually | ok |  | Only matters to the minority who turned screenshot capture on at all; preset material. |
| On-device models | `powerSaving.dropOnDeviceModels` | toggle | on | merge | Whisper and handwriting users on low battery | ok |  | Heavy CPU inference is worth dropping, but a Whisper user who wants it kept would rather flip one preset. |
| Typing statistics | `powerSaving.dropTypingStats` | toggle | off | REMOVE | nobody | ok |  | Its own KDoc calls the saving "unmeasured and speculative" — a few integer bumps per keystroke. A row that exists to admit it does nothing. |
| Autocorrect | `autocorrect` | toggle | on | keep | everyone; it is the tool's own switch | ok |  | Mirrors the Typing screen's switch, which is right: the toolbar tool toggles the same thing it advertises. |
| Style to start with | `layoutBehavior.fancyToolStyleId` | choice | Follow the strip (null) | keep | someone who always writes in one fancy style | - |  | Deliberately has no reset: the null option is the default and is already one press away in the list. |
| Keep the language after you turn it off | `layoutBehavior.fancyToolKeepsLanguage` | toggle | off | demote | heavy fancy-text users who dislike the layout snapping back | ok |  | Niche behaviour of a novelty tool; off matches the least surprising "put my keyboard back" expectation. |
| Turn off when the keyboard closes | `layoutBehavior.fancyToolAutoOff` | toggle | off | demote | people who forget fancy text is still on | split |  | Arguably should default on — silently typing ⓕⓐⓝⓒⓨ into a work email is a worse failure than an extra tap. |
| Stylus only | `handwritingStylusOnly` | toggle | off | keep | S Pen / stylus owners with palm-rejection trouble | ok |  | Most devices have no stylus; on by default would make the canvas dead to a finger. |
| Automatic space | `handwritingAutoSpace` | toggle | on | demote | handwriters who write CJK or want no gaps | ok |  | Writing words one after another almost always wants spaces; the toggle exists for scripts that do not. |
| Recognition pause | `handwritingCommitDelayMs` | slider | 700 ms (300–2000) | keep | slow writers and Bengali conjunct writers | ok |  | Real personal variance: too short cuts a multi-stroke letter in half, too long feels sluggish. 700 ms is a sane middle. |
| Handwriting model downloads | `-` | list | nothing downloaded | keep | anyone who writes by hand in a non-default language | ok |  | Per-language download/delete rows narrowed to enabled languages. Not a preference — storage management the user must be able to reach. |
| Translate into | `translateTargetLang` | nav | en | keep | everyone who uses the tool; source is auto-detected | ok |  | Target is the only half the tool cannot guess. English is the commonest bridge language; the full list is one dialog away. |
| Cloud Translation API key (optional) | `translateApiKey` | text | blank (built-in key, else free endpoint) | keep | heavy users who exhaust the shared quota | ok |  | Blank means built-in; the field only exists so a user's own key beats it. Nothing to decide unless the free path fails. |
| Sticker packs | `-` | nav | none installed | keep | anyone with .wmstickers packs | - |  | Route to the pack manager; not a preference. |
| Full-screen picker | `mediaFullBleed` | toggle | on | demote | people who want the toolbar kept in reach | ok |  | Panel pays for the toolbar row with its own back header, so on gives strictly more grid. Off is the minority taste. |
| Klipy API key | `klipyApiKey` | text | blank | keep | users on builds with no baked-in key | ok |  | Without a key the tool has no source at all; clearing the key also clears the cached category list, which is correct. |
| GIPHY API key | `giphyApiKey` | text | blank | keep | people who want a second GIF source | ok |  | Optional second provider; one working key is enough for the tool to function. |
| Send stickers as | `stickerSendMode` | choice | Sticker | keep | users of apps that reject sticker MIME types | ok |  | WhatsApp shows real stickers for these; the fallback exists because some apps refuse the sticker MIME. |
| Send GIFs as | `gifSendMode` | choice | Image | keep | WhatsApp users wanting sticker-style GIFs | ok |  | Sticker mode only works for WebP-backed GIFs, so Image is the safe default and the other is opt-in. |
| Multiple sources | `gifSourceMode` | choice | Tabs | demote | people with two provider keys configured | ok |  | Inert unless two keys exist. Drawn as a bare ChoiceControl, so unlike the rows around it it has no reset affordance. |
| Content filter | `gifContentFilter` | choice | Medium | keep | parents, and anyone using the keyboard at work | ok |  | Four levels mapped onto each provider's rating. Medium is the defensible middle; also drawn without a reset control. |
| Results for each search (GIF) | `gifResultLimit` | slider | 24 (6–48) | demote | metered-data users who want fewer thumbnails | ok |  | Page size, not quality. Almost nobody tunes it; the docs even claim it is not adjustable, which is how invisible it is. |
| Brave API key | `braveApiKey` | text | blank | keep | anyone on a build with no baked-in Brave key | ok |  | Both search tools are hidden entirely without a usable key, so this field is the whole gate for the feature. |
| SafeSearch | `searchSafe` | toggle | on | keep | anyone searching images from a phone in public | ok |  | Maps to Brave moderate for web and strict for images. On is the only sane default for a keyboard. |
| Results for each search | `searchResultCount` | slider | 8 (1–10) | demote | almost nobody; one API request either way | ok |  | The screen itself admits each search costs one request regardless, so the slider changes scroll length and nothing else. |
| Start with everything selected | `ocrAutoSelectWords` | toggle | on | keep | people who scan a whole paragraph, versus pickers of single words | ok |  | Genuinely two workflows — trim-down versus pick-up. Whole-capture is the commoner one, so on is right. |
| Insert automatically | `qrScanAutoInsert` | toggle | off | keep | warehouse/checkout users scanning code after code | ok |  | Off keeps a confirm step before text lands in the field; the repeat-scanner is the minority that opts in. |
| Vibrate on detection | `qrScanHaptics` | toggle | on | demote | anyone scanning without watching the screen | ok |  | Cheap confirmation that a code was caught; only silent-phone users turn it off. |
| Load link details | `qrScanLinkPreviews` | toggle | off | keep | anyone wary of scanning an unknown QR link | ok |  | Off by default because it is the one network call in an otherwise offline tool; power saving force-disables it. |
| Save to the gallery (documents) | `docScanSaveToGallery` | toggle | off | keep | people archiving receipts, not just sending them | ok |  | Off keeps the keyboard from quietly filling the camera roll; the archivist opts in. |
| Recognition engine | `whisper.engine` | choice | System | keep | people who want offline dictation or distrust cloud recognition | ok |  | Full build only. System needs no download and usually wins on accuracy, so Whisper stays the deliberate choice. |
| Voice typing view | `voiceBar.mode` | choice | Full panel | keep | people who want to see the field while dictating | ok |  | Panel is discoverable and has the extras (hold-to-talk, language chip); strip and bar trade those away for screen space. |
| Keep listening | `voiceContinuous` | toggle | on | keep | anyone dictating more than one sentence | ok |  | Re-tapping the mic per sentence is the worse default; the engine already gives up after two silent retries. |
| Spoken punctuation | `voiceSpokenPunctuation` | toggle | on | keep | anyone who literally wants to type the word "comma" | ok |  | Only English and Bengali phrase groups, applied to finished phrases only, so the false-positive cost is small. |
| Translate to English | `whisper.translate` | toggle | off | demote | bilingual users dictating a foreign language into English | ok |  | Only shown with Whisper selected; a surprising mode to have on by accident, so off is right. |
| English dialect | `grammarDialect` | choice | American | AUTOMATE | British/Indian/Australian spellers, who are currently corrected wrongly | split |  | Weekend and alternate calendars already derive from region here; dialect should too instead of assuming American. |
| Re-check delay | `grammarDebounceMs` | slider | 350 ms (100–1500) | demote | almost nobody; a latency knob with no visible symptom | ok |  | Users cannot judge 350 versus 500 ms without a stopwatch. Fine to keep, wrong to show above the dialect row's fold. |
| Use Harper everywhere | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | people who want spell-check outside the keyboard | - |  | Opens Android's spell-checker screen with a documented fallback to input-method settings on OEM builds that hid it. |
| Underline only, no fix popup | `spellCheckerNoSuggestions` | toggle | off | demote | people who want squiggles without the system popup stealing taps | ok |  | Android 12+ only and only while Harper is the system checker — two gates deep for a cosmetic difference. |
| Wikipedia language | `wikiLanguage` | text | en | keep | non-English readers | ok |  | en has by far the most articles, so it is the right default; the free-text subdomain field should be a picker like Translate's. |
| Markdown links | `wikiLinksMarkdown` | toggle | off | demote | people writing notes in Markdown apps | ok |  | A bare URL pastes correctly everywhere; Markdown only helps in Markdown-aware fields, so off is right. |
| Clear the recent symbols | `symbolRecents` | action | empty list | keep | anyone whose recents filled with a one-off symbol | - |  | The tool's only settings row, and it is an action with a live count rather than a preference. |
| Calculate as you type | `smartCalc` | toggle | on | keep | anyone typing sums in chat | ok |  | Part of the smart-chip family; the chip only appears on an actual arithmetic expression, so the false-positive cost is low. |
| Degrees | `calcDegrees` | toggle | on | keep | students doing trig; radians users flip it | ok |  | Casual users mean degrees when they type sin(30); radians is the specialist's choice. |
| Result precision | `calcPrecision` | slider | 8 (0–12) | demote | people who want money-style two-decimal answers | ok |  | 8 places is more than a phone calculator ever needs, but it is harmless; the row is tuning, not a decision. |
| Convert as you type (units) | `smartUnits` | toggle | on | keep | anyone reading recipes or heights in the other system | ok |  | Chip fires only on "1 ft"-shaped text; the whole point of the tool is that you do not open it. |
| Feet and inches | `compoundUnits` | toggle | on | keep | anyone converting a height into imperial | ok |  | 3 ft 3.37 in is how people say it; 3.2808399 ft is how a calculator says it. On is right. |
| Convert as you type (currency) | `smartCurrency` | toggle | on | keep | anyone quoted a price in a foreign currency | ok |  | Subtitle names the target currency inline, so the row explains what it will do before it does it. |
| Decimal places | `currencyDecimals` | slider | 2 (0–6) | demote | users of zero-decimal currencies (JPY, KRW, IDR) | ok |  | 2 is right for most currencies; could be derived from the target currency's own minor-unit count instead of asked. |
| Refresh the rates every | `currencyCacheHours` | slider | 6 h (1–48) | AUTOMATE | nobody; upstream rates change about once a day | ok |  | The docs concede anything under 24 h mostly changes how soon a failed fetch retries — that is a retry policy, not a preference. |
| Currency rates from | `rateSources.fiatProviders[0]` | choice | ExchangeRate-API | demote | people whose currency one provider does not quote | ok |  | ER-API covers ~160 currencies against Frankfurter's ~30, so the default is the broad one; the choice is a fallback for outages. |
| Try the other sources (fiat) | `rateSources.fiatProviders (tail)` | toggle | on | REMOVE | nobody — nobody wants a failed conversion on purpose | ok |  | Turning fallbacks off can only make the tool fail more often. Clever data model (head plus tail), but no user need. |
| Convert coins | `rateSources.cryptoEnabled` | toggle | on | keep | crypto holders; everyone else never sees the section | split |  | Adds a Crypto section to the picker for the majority who will never use it, but costs no network until a coin is picked. |
| Coin decimal places | `rateSources.cryptoDecimals` | slider | Auto (0) | demote | nobody much; Auto already keeps five meaningful digits | ok |  | Auto exists precisely because a fixed decimal count is wrong across BTC and SHIB, so the manual values are traps. |
| Refresh coin prices every | `rateSources.cryptoCacheMinutes` | slider | 5 min (1–60) | demote | active traders wanting fresher quotes | ok |  | Unlike the fiat cache this one is defensible — coin prices genuinely move by the minute — but 5 suits nearly everyone. |
| Coin prices from | `rateSources.cryptoProviders[0]` | choice | Coinbase | demote | people whose altcoin only one source quotes | ok |  | Matters only alongside the Add-a-ticker feature, where coverage really does differ between providers. |
| Try the other sources (crypto) | `rateSources.cryptoProviders (tail)` | toggle | on | REMOVE | nobody | ok |  | Same as the fiat fallback switch: no user benefits from fewer fallbacks. |
| Coins | `rateSources.cryptoTickers` | list | 20 of 39 catalogue coins on | keep | someone tracking an altcoin the catalogue omits | ok |  | Chips plus Add-a-ticker. The last coin cannot be switched off, because an empty set silently means "all defaults" again. |
| Image size | `qrSizePx` | slider | 1024 px (256–2048) | demote | someone printing a generated code | ok |  | Affects the sent PNG only; the on-panel preview is a fixed size, so the slider has no visible effect while dragging. |
| Send as (QR) | `qrSendMode` | choice | Image | REMOVE | nobody | - |  | Sticker mode needs a WebP source and a generated QR is always PNG, so both options send an image. Documented as having no effect. |
| Save to the gallery (QR) | `qrSaveToGallery` | toggle | off | keep | someone generating codes to reuse later | ok |  | Off keeps generated throwaways out of the camera roll; consistent with camera and doc-scan. |
| Error correction | `qrEcc` | choice | M | demote | people printing codes onto imperfect surfaces | ok |  | L/M/Q/H are the standard's own letters. Drawn as a bare ChoiceControl, so it has no reset control unlike neighbouring rows. |
| Length | `passwordGenerator.pwLength` | slider | 16 (4–64) | keep | anyone hitting a site's length cap | ok |  | 16 is comfortably strong. The panel edits the same value live, so this screen is the slower route to it. |
| Capital letters | `passwordGenerator.pwUppercase` | toggle | on | keep | people fighting a site's character rules | ok |  | Class toggles exist because sites impose contradictory rules; all three on is the strongest starting pool. |
| Digits | `passwordGenerator.pwDigits` | toggle | on | keep | same as capitals | ok |  | Generator guarantees one character from every enabled class, then shuffles, so switching a class off is meaningful. |
| Symbols | `passwordGenerator.pwSymbols` | toggle | on | keep | people on sites that reject punctuation | ok |  | The most commonly rejected class, so this is the toggle that actually gets used. |
| Exclude look-alikes | `passwordGenerator.pwExcludeAmbiguous` | toggle | off | keep | anyone reading a password aloud or retyping it on a TV | ok |  | Shrinks the pool and so the entropy; off is right when most passwords go straight into a manager. |
| Words | `passwordGenerator.ppWordCount` | slider | 4 (2–10) | keep | passphrase users balancing memorability and strength | ok |  | Four short words is the canonical diceware-ish default and matches the entropy readout shown alongside. |
| Separator | `passwordGenerator.ppSeparator` | text | - | keep | people whose target field rejects hyphens | ok |  | Hyphen is the most widely accepted joiner; blank gives a run-together phrase. Has a reset control via TextFieldSetting. |
| Capitalize the words | `passwordGenerator.ppCapitalize` | toggle | off | demote | people meeting a mixed-case requirement | ok |  | Adds almost no entropy; exists to satisfy site rules, so off is right and the row is bottom-of-screen material. |
| Include a digit | `passwordGenerator.ppIncludeDigit` | toggle | off | demote | same rule-satisfying user as capitalize | ok |  | Appends one digit to one word. Pure compliance knob, not a security choice. |
| Default test | `typingTestMode` | choice | Timed | demote | people who always run the same test shape | ok |  | This is remembered state as much as a setting — the panel's own chip row writes it, so the screen mostly mirrors it. |
| Length (seconds) | `typingTestDuration` | slider | 30 s (15–120) | demote | people who want a 60-second run | ok |  | Bests are keyed per config, so a free slider lets you mint a 103-second record nobody can compare; the code's own preset list is 15/30/60/120. |
| Length (words) | `typingTestWordCount` | slider | 25 (10–100) | demote | same as the seconds slider | ok |  | Same preset-versus-slider mismatch: TypingTestWordCounts lists 10/25/50/100, the settings row offers every value between. |
| Punctuation | `typingTestPunctuation` | toggle | off | keep | people practising realistic prose typing | ok |  | Hidden entirely in Quote mode, which is the right conditional. Off keeps the baseline test comparable to other typing tests. |
| Numbers | `typingTestNumbers` | toggle | off | keep | people who type numbers for a living | ok |  | Same rationale as punctuation; both change the prompt, not the scoring. |
| Tests completed / Recent average / bests | `typingTestsCompleted, typingTestBests, typingTestHistory` | list | empty | keep | anyone tracking improvement across configs | - |  | Read-only records, not settings. The panel only shows the current config's best, so this screen is where the rest live. |
| Clear records | `-` | action | n/a | keep | anyone who wants a clean slate | - |  | Only shown once there is something to clear; wipes bests, history and badges together, which the subtitle states. |
| Achievements | `typingTestAchievements` | list | none unlocked | keep | players who want the goals visible, not just the earned ones | - |  | Read-only badge list with locked ones greyed; the in-keyboard results screen shows only what is already earned. |
| Sort order | `launcher.sortOrder` | choice | A to Z | keep | people who relaunch the same few apps | ok |  | Alphabetical is predictable and stable; recent-first is a genuinely different mental model, so both earn their place. |
| App names | `launcher.showLabels` | toggle | on | demote | people who recognise icons and want more per row | ok |  | Labels are what make search results legible; off is a density preference for a small minority. |
| Recent apps | `launcher.recentsEnabled` | toggle | on | keep | privacy-minded users; turning it off also deletes the stored list | ok |  | It is a tracking switch as much as a layout one, and it erases what it collected, which is the right behaviour. |
| Open screens inside apps | `launcher.activityDrilldown` | toggle | on | demote | people who prefer long-press to mean pin | ok |  | Really a gesture remap: with it off, holding an app pins it instead. Worth keeping, worth naming better. |
| Show private screens | `launcher.showNonExported` | toggle | off | demote | developers poking at other apps | ok |  | Its own KDoc calls them debugging fare — tapping one throws SecurityException. Correctly off, but it is a developer row in a user screen. |
| Start with the selfie camera | `camera.preferFront` | toggle | off | keep | people who mostly send selfies | ok |  | Back camera is the general-purpose one; the selfie-first user is a real but smaller group. |
| Mirror selfies | `camera.mirrorFront` | toggle | on | keep | anyone confused by an un-mirrored selfie; off for readable text | ok |  | Matches what you framed. Off is right only when the shot contains text, which is the rarer case. |
| Send the whole photo | `camera.fullFrame` | toggle | off | demote | people who want the strips the panel cropped away | ok |  | What-you-see-is-what-you-get is the less surprising default; this is the one case where the photo holds more than you framed. |
| Save to the gallery (camera) | `camera.saveToGallery` | toggle | off | keep | people who want a permanent copy of a keyboard snap | ok |  | Without it the capture is scratch storage pruned to the last four files, so this is the only way to keep one. |
| Shutter sound | `camera.shutterSound` | toggle | on | demote | people photographing quietly | ok |  | On matches every camera app. Worth noting the toggle cannot win where the OS enforces a shutter sound by law. |
| Haptics (camera) | `camera.haptics` | toggle | on | demote | people who keep vibration off everywhere | ok |  | Panel-local feedback only; the Send vibration is the ordinary key haptic set under Typing, which the row does not say. |
| Clipboard history | `clipboard.history` | toggle | on | keep | anyone who copies more than one thing an hour | ok |  | The tool's reason to exist; off makes the panel a single-item view. |
| Suggest the last copy | `clipboard.suggestRecent` | toggle | on | keep | people pasting a just-copied code or link | ok |  | Gboard-style paste chip; the strip is the fastest place a fresh copy can be. Sensitive clips are excluded already. |
| Suggest copied codes in code boxes | `clipboard.suggestCodesInCodeFields` | toggle | on | demote | anyone pasting an OTP copied by hand | ok |  | Well-argued exception to the hide-secrets rule — in a code box the code is the only thing being typed. Very few will change it. |
| Pop-up when you copy | `feedback.toastOnCopy` | toggle | off | demote | people in apps that give no copy feedback of their own | ok |  | Android already toasts on copy on many versions, so on would double up. Lives on the clipboard page but writes a feedback field. |
| Clipboard expiry | `clipboard.expiryHours` | slider | 24 h (0 = never, 0–168) | keep | privacy-minded users, and hoarders who set never | ok |  | A day covers the practical span of "I copied that earlier" without keeping a week of secrets around. |
| Maximum entries | `clipboard.maxItems` | slider | 100 (5–500) | demote | heavy copiers who scroll far back | ok |  | The other half of the expiry bound: a busy day can pile up hundreds inside the window, and that is a haystack, not history. |
| Bottom control row | `clipboard.bottomRow` | toggle | off | demote | people who paste then keep typing without leaving the panel | split |  | The emoji panel ships this row on; the clipboard panel ships it off. Same ergonomics, opposite default — one of the two is wrong. |
| Full-screen clipboard panel | `clipboard.fullBleed` | toggle | on | demote | people who want the toolbar in reach | ok |  | The panel pays for the toolbar row with its own back header, so on strictly buys more cards on screen. |
| Pinned entries last | `clipboard.pinnedLast` | toggle | off | demote | people with many pins who mostly want recents | ok |  | Pins-first matches why you pinned; the inversion only helps once the pin list is long. |
| Search bar | `clipboard.search` | toggle | off | demote | people with a long history to sift | split |  | Off by default even though max entries defaults to 100 — the exact size where search starts paying for its row. |
| Detect codes, numbers and links | `clipboard.detectEntities` | toggle | on | keep | anyone extracting an OTP from a copied SMS | ok |  | Turns a copy-edit-paste into one tap; the false positives it does create are what the phone-format row exists to fix. |
| Phone number formats | `clipboard.phoneFormats` | nav | empty (every number-shaped run counts) | keep | anyone whose country's numbers are mis-detected | ok |  | Phone numbers are the one fragment with no shape of their own, so this is where the detector is told what to keep. |
| Forget after you paste a password | `clipboard.clearAfterPasswordPaste` | toggle | on | keep | every password-manager user | ok |  | A pasted password would otherwise sit readable by every app until it expired. Strong default, rightly on. |
| Link previews | `clipboard.linkPreviews` | toggle | off | keep | people who copy many links and want titles | ok |  | Off because it turns a copy into a network fetch of a URL you may not want fetched. Power saving forces it off too. |
| Your screenshots | `clipboard.userScreenshots` | toggle | off | keep | people who screenshot then send immediately | ok |  | Needs the photos permission and starts a folder observer, so it must be opt-in; the row launches the grant itself. |
| The app needs the storage permission | `-` | nav | hidden once granted | keep | anyone who enabled screenshots but denied the permission | - |  | Only drawn while the feature is on and the grant is missing, and it disappears on return, which is the right pattern. |
| Show the source app | `clipboard.trackSource` | toggle | off | demote | people who copy from many apps and lose track | ok |  | Costs the Usage Access special permission for a best-effort guess shown only in a long-press popup. Correctly off. |
| The app needs the Usage Access permission | `-` | nav | hidden once granted | keep | anyone who turned source tracking on without the grant | - |  | Same conditional-grant pattern as the screenshots row, routed through the prominent-disclosure layer. |
| Sensitive clips | `clipboard.sensitiveHandling` | choice | Short-lived | keep | password-manager users; strict users pick Never save | ok |  | Middle option keeps a copied password usable for a few minutes without leaving it in history all day. Good three-way split. |
| Let the keyboard find them | `clipboard.detectSensitive` | toggle | on | keep | users of managers that predate Android 13's sensitive flag | ok |  | Most managers still do not set the flag and a hand-copied code carries none, so flag-only detection would miss most secrets. |
| Forget sensitive clips after | `clipboard.sensitiveExpiryMinutes` | slider | 5 min (1–120) | demote | people who want a tighter or looser leash on secrets | ok |  | Only shown for Short-lived. Deliberately never capped by the ordinary expiry, which is the subtle part users will not notice. |
| Codes from notifications | `otp.enabled` | toggle | off | keep | anyone who types SMS verification codes weekly | ok |  | Needs notification access, so off twice over is right — the app cannot grant it and should not imply it has it. |
| Allow notification access | `-` | nav | hidden once granted | keep | anyone who enabled the chip but not the grant | - |  | Conditional row that states plainly the chip cannot see codes yet. |
| Only in number fields | `otp.numberFieldsOnly` | toggle | on | keep | people whose bank puts the code box behind a text field | ok |  | Digit fields are the shape most code boxes have; off widens it for the apps that do not, at the cost of stray chips. |
| Offer a code for | `otp.expiryMinutes` | slider | 3 min (1–10) | demote | people slow to switch apps for a code | ok |  | KDoc has it right: a chip showing last hour's code is worse than no chip. Three minutes needs almost no tuning. |
| Clear the notification | `otp.dismissNotification` | toggle | off | demote | people who hate a shade full of spent codes | ok |  | A keyboard dismissing another app's notification is a surprising side effect, so opt-in is right. |
| Type codes one character at a time | `otp.perDigitEntry` | toggle | on | AUTOMATE | nobody chooses this; per-box code fields need it | ok |  | Its own KDoc says per-character is indistinguishable from a whole commit in a normal field, so there is nothing to decide. |
| Location | `weatherLatitude/Longitude/PlaceName` | nav | unset | keep | everyone who opens Weather, and qibla users on Compass | ok | yes | The tool is inert without it and the keyboard never reads GPS, so it must be asked. The onboarding tool page already shares this composable. |
| Fahrenheit | `weatherFahrenheit` | toggle | off (Celsius) | AUTOMATE | US users; everyone else already has the right unit | split |  | Region tells you this for free, exactly as the weekend and calendar defaults already do. Wind stays km/h either way, which is inconsistent. |
| First calendar | `calendarAltOne` | nav | region-derived (BD → Bengali, SA → Hijri, DE → none) | keep | Muslim, Bengali, Hebrew, Thai and Japanese calendar users | ok |  | Already automated from region via defaultAltCalendars, so the row only exists for people the region guess misses. |
| Second calendar | `calendarAltTwo` | nav | region-derived (BD → Hijri, most regions none) | demote | people who need two alternate calendars at once | ok |  | Genuine for Bangladesh (Bengali plus Hijri); elsewhere the second slot is usually empty and could be revealed on demand. |
| Weekend | `calendarWeekend` | nav | region-derived (BD → Friday, SA → Fri/Sat, else Sat/Sun) | keep | anyone outside the Sat/Sun world | ok |  | Model example of automating instead of asking. Note SettingsDefaults still reads SAT_SUN, so any reset control would disagree with the live default. |
| Hijri day adjustment | `hijriAdjustDays` | slider | 0 (−2…+2) | keep | anyone whose local moon sighting differs from the tabular date | ok |  | Only shown when a Hijri slot is picked. The drift is real and local, and no algorithm can settle it. |
| Turn off with the keyboard | `flashlightAutoOff` | toggle | on | keep | everyone; stops a torch burning in a pocket | ok |  | Off only suits someone using the keyboard tile as a general torch switch, which the system tile already does better. |
| Degree readout | `compassShowDegrees` | toggle | on | demote | nobody much; it only hides a number | ok |  | Pure cosmetics on a novelty panel. Harmless, but it is the kind of row a 500-entry settings app accumulates. |
| Show the qibla | `compassShowQibla` | toggle | off | keep | Muslim users praying away from home | ok |  | Off for the majority, and the screen shows an inline error when it is on without a saved weather location — good coupling. |
| Angle readout | `levelShowAngles` | toggle | on | demote | nobody much; hides the pitch/roll numbers | ok |  | Same class as the compass degree readout: a cosmetic toggle on a tool most people open twice. |
| Southern hemisphere | `moonSouthernHemisphere` | toggle | off | AUTOMATE | southern-hemisphere viewers, who see a mirrored moon | split |  | A real difference, but the app can often infer it: the weather location's latitude sign answers it without asking. |

**Missing here:** Weather refresh interval (Currency exposes a cache slider; weather hard-codes 15 minutes, so a user watching a storm cannot refresh more often); Wind speed unit (Wind is always km/h even with Fahrenheit on, so US users get a mixed imperial/metric panel); Camera capture resolution (Capped at 1600x1600 for everyone; someone sending a photo of a document wants more, a data-saver wants less); Typing test language (CommonWords and Quotes are English-only, so a Bengali typist cannot measure Bengali typing speed); Password symbol set (Fixed pool; sites that reject specific punctuation force users to regenerate repeatedly instead of narrowing the set); Wikipedia links per article (Capped at 200 although the API allows 500; long articles silently truncate their Links tab); Launcher recents length (MAX_RECENTS is fixed at 10; users who app-hop want a longer row, minimalists a shorter one); QR content length cap (Preview refuses past 2,000 characters with no way to trade error-correction level for capacity)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/typing/power-saving.mdx` — wrong: Says "eleven independent switches" and its table lists eleven; the code ships twelve. The Typing statistics switch (powerSaving.dropTypingStats, MainActivity.kt:9765, default off) is missing from the prose, the table and the screenshot caption.
- `docs/src/content/docs/emoji/gifs.mdx` — wrong: Details section states "24 results per fetch, not adjustable" and the Options table omits the setting entirely — there is a Results for each search slider (gifResultLimit, 6–48, default 24) at MainActivity.kt:10048.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/camera.mdx` — stale: Describes the shared header as Enabled + icon colour + a Keyword shortcut field, but every tool page also draws a Match capitals toggle and, once edited, a Reset to defaults row (MainActivity.kt:11382-11408). The keyword field is also titled Trigger words, not Keyword shortcut.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/app-launcher.mdx` — gap: The Options list gives no defaults for any of the five rows (A–Z, labels on, recents on, drilldown on, private screens off) and omits the shared Enabled, Icon colour and Trigger words rows that open the screen.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/instruments.mdx` — gap: Weather section documents only Location and Fahrenheit; it does not mention that wind speed stays km/h regardless of the Fahrenheit toggle, nor that the 15-minute refresh is fixed and not user-settable.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/utilities.mdx` — gap: Typing speed test Options lists Default test, Length, Difficulty and Records but omits the Achievements group the settings screen draws (all four badges, locked ones greyed, MainActivity.kt:10892).
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/scanner.mdx` — gap: Options sections cover only the tool-specific rows; none of the three scanner screens' shared header rows (Enabled, Icon colour, Trigger words "ocr"/"scan"/"docscan", Match capitals) are documented, though the page mentions the keywords in passing under Details.
- `MISSING: settings that exist but are documented nowhere` — gap: No docs page covers the Fancy text tool's own settings (Style to start with, Keep the language, Turn off when the keyboard closes — MainActivity.kt:9799-9829); languages/fancy-text.mdx describes the strip, not this screen.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11499 — TextFieldSetting draws a ResetSetting trailing icon whenever the text differs from `default` (used by Wikipedia language, passphrase separator, AI model/URL fields)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11397-11408 — ToolKeywordSetting shows an explicit "Reset to defaults" row, subtitled with the shipped keyword list, once the keywords are edited`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11324-11333 — ToolColourRow passes showReset to ColorPickerDialog whenever an icon-colour override exists; picking reset writes null`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:10638-10655 — RateSourceSetting reads both rows' defaults off the shipped provider list rather than restating them, so the reset can never drift`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11984-11989 — WeatherLocationSetting's dialog grows a Clear button once a location is saved`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:10875-10884 — Typing test "Clear records" row (bests, history and badges together), shown only when there is something to clear`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:10299-10311 — Symbols "Clear the recent symbols" action with a live remembered-count subtitle`, `core/settings/.../SettingsRepository.kt:1616 — every ToggleSetting/SliderSetting/ChoiceSetting in this domain passes `default = SettingsDefaults.…`, so each row grows its own reset control from the same data class the value lives on`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:9805-9810 — Fancy "Style to start with" deliberately has no reset: a null default is how ChoiceSetting spells "no one right answer"`

_Coverage: Read in full: MainActivity.kt 8990-10604 (whole second half of ToolDetailSettings plus providerLabel, RateSourceSetting, CryptoCoinPicker, TypingTestToolSettings, typingBestLabel) and 11099-11996 (ToolColourRow, ToolKeywordSetting, TokenPresetSetting, TextFieldSetting, ApiKeyField, AltCalendarSetting, WeekendSetting, TranslateLanguageSetting, openSpellCheckerSettings/openSubtypeEnabler, HandwritingModelManager, WeatherLocationSetting), plus MainActivity.kt 2955-3043 for the OTP group. Because the task's field list named LauncherToolSettings, CameraSettings and ClipboardSettings — all drawn before line 9600 — I also read and covered MainActivity.kt 9000-9051 (launcher), 9079-9329 (clipboard), 9379-9555 (compass/level/moon/weather/calendar/camera); those rows may overlap with tools-a. Settings sources read: SettingsRepository.kt KDoc for KeyboardSettings 1340-1600, OtpSettings 1618-1662, LauncherToolSettings/PasswordGeneratorSettings/RateSourceSettings/CameraSettings/VoiceBarSettings/WhisperSettings 2054-2260, ClipboardSettings 2389-2490, plus PowerSavingSettings.kt in full. Docs read in full: tools/camera.mdx, tools/scanner.mdx, tools/search.mdx, tools/app-launcher.mdx, tools/instruments.mdx; also checked tools/utilities.mdx (60-175), typing/power-saving.mdx (30-75), emoji/gifs.mdx (55-80) and grepped tools/handwriting.mdx, tools/translate.mdx, tools/voice.mdx, smart/grammar.mdx for default mismatches. Deliberately NOT covered (other agents' domains, reached only by nav rows from my range): the Typing screen (\"typing\" route), keypress/feedback (KeySoundGroup at 9841 and its nav row), themes, layout, languages, modes, sticker_packs, phoneformats, AI (AiToolSettings 10953-11293 and TokenPresetSetting), WhisperModelManager and LocalLlmModelManager internals, and the shared \"Enabled\" toggle at 8951. One structural gap worth flagging: the three ChoiceControl rows in my range — Multiple sources (10020), Content filter (10032) and Error correction (10501) — take no `default` argument, so unlike every neighbouring row they carry no reset-to-default affordance._

### AI, voice, Whisper

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Enable | `enabledTools (AI)` | toggle | On | keep | anyone who never wants an AI chip in the toolbox | ok |  | Tool is enabled but not pinned; costs nothing until opened. Off-by-default would hide it from the toolbox entirely. |
| Icon colour (start) | `toolColorOverrides[AI]` | action | Tool accent | demote | theme tinkerer matching the toolbar palette | ok |  | Cosmetic, already gated behind the global colourful-tool-icons switch. Pure decoration. |
| Icon colour (end) | `toolColorEndOverrides[AI]` | action | derived from start | demote | same tinkerer, gradient variant | ok |  | Second row only when gradients are on. Derived default keeps the pair moving together until pinned. |
| Keyword | `toolKeywords[AI]` | text | ai | keep | anyone reaching AI from the suggestion strip | ok |  | "ai" is short and rarely typed as a standalone word; it is also how the docs tell people to open the tool. |
