| Keyword is case sensitive | `toolKeywordCase[AI]` | toggle | Off | demote | nobody in practice for the word "ai" | ok |  | Generic per-tool control; cannot be removed for one tool. Off matches every stored keyword being lower case. |
| Provider | `ai.provider` | choice | Claude (Anthropic) | keep | everyone using the tool; it is the whole setup | split |  | No provider ships a key, so the default only preselects a chip. Gemini is the one with a free tier a user can finish setup on. |
| Anthropic API key | `ai.anthropicKey` | text | empty | keep | Claude subscriber | - |  | Bring-your-own-key only; no built-in or proxied key exists for any cloud provider. |
| Model (Claude) | `ai.anthropicModel` | text | empty → claude-sonnet-5 | keep | user wanting Opus/Haiku instead of the shipped guess | ok |  | Blank falls back to a named default shown in the hint, so the field is opt-in for anyone who cares. |
| OpenAI API key | `ai.openAiKey` | text | empty | keep | OpenAI subscriber | - |  | Same bring-your-own-key contract as every other cloud provider. |
| Model (OpenAI) | `ai.openAiModel` | text | empty → gpt-5.6-luna | keep | user picking a cheaper or newer OpenAI model | ok |  | Blank means the shipped default, printed in the hint. |
| Gemini API key | `ai.geminiKey` | text | empty | keep | free-tier Gemini user — the cheapest way in | - |  | Docs call out Gemini's free tier; this is the only zero-cost cloud path. |
| Model (Gemini) | `ai.geminiModel` | text | empty → gemini-3.5-flash | keep | user wanting Pro over Flash | ok |  | Flash is the right free-tier-friendly guess; the field exists for the exceptions. |
| Server address (Ollama) | `ai.ollamaUrl` | text | empty | keep | self-hoster with Ollama on the LAN | ok |  | No address can be guessed; the hint shows the shape. Blank is the only honest default. |
| Model (Ollama) | `ai.ollamaModel` | text | empty → qwen3 | keep | self-hoster running something other than qwen3 | ok |  | qwen3 is a common Ollama pull; blank falls back to it rather than erroring. |
| Server address (LM Studio) | `ai.lmStudioUrl` | text | empty | keep | LM Studio desktop user | ok |  | Same as Ollama: unguessable address, hint carries the shape. |
| Model (LM Studio) | `ai.lmStudioModel` | text | empty (server's loaded model) | keep | user with several models loaded in LM Studio | ok |  | Blank uses whatever the server currently has loaded — the behaviour LM Studio users expect. |
| xAI API key | `ai.xaiKey` | text | empty | keep | Grok subscriber | - |  | Grok speaks OpenAI's shape, so this chip is convenience over "Other service": preset URL plus default model. |
| Model (Grok) | `ai.xaiModel` | text | empty → grok-4.5 | keep | Grok user on a different tier | ok |  | Blank falls back to the hinted default. |
| DeepSeek API key | `ai.deepSeekKey` | text | empty | keep | cost-sensitive user on DeepSeek pricing | - |  | Also OpenAI-shaped; the named chip buys the preset URL and reasoning-stream handling. |
| Model (DeepSeek) | `ai.deepSeekModel` | text | empty → deepseek-v4-flash | keep | user wanting the reasoning model instead of flash | ok |  | Blank falls back to the hinted default. |
| Service address (Other service) | `ai.compatibleUrl` | text | empty | keep | OpenRouter/Groq/vLLM user — the real escape hatch | ok |  | KDoc: address up to the version segment, client appends /chat/completions, because services disagree on the prefix. |
| Model (Other service) | `ai.compatibleModel` | text | empty (required) | keep | same user; nothing works without it | ok |  | Code comment: "no default model: there is nothing sensible to guess for a service the app knows nothing about". |
| API key (Other service) | `ai.compatibleKey` | text | empty | keep | user of a gateway that wants a key | ok |  | KDoc: optional, because a gateway on the user's own network often wants none. |
| Access token (Hugging Face) | `ai.hfToken` | text | empty | keep | anyone downloading the 2 gated Gemma models | ok |  | Only 2 of 8 catalog models are gated; the row shows a "Get a token" link beside it. |
| Get a token | `-` | action | - | keep | first-time gated-model downloader | - |  | Opens LocalLlmCatalog.TOKEN_URL; without it the token field is a dead end. |
| On-device model catalog (download / delete / Use this model) | `ai.localModelId` | list | "" — sole downloaded model auto-adopted | keep | privacy-first user running everything offline | ok |  | Auto-adopting the only model on disk removes a pointless selection step after the first download. |
| Import your own model | `-` | action | - | keep | user who converted their own .litertlm/.task | - |  | Only two container formats load; anything else is rejected on import. Small audience, but the catalog is fixed at 8. |
| Compute | `ai.localBackend` | choice | CPU | keep | user on a phone whose GPU is actually faster | split |  | Engine already falls back to CPU on GPU failure, so GPU-first is defensible; CPU-first avoids an OOM taking the IME down. |
| Free up (orphaned model files) | `-` | action | - | keep | user whose multi-GB model left the catalog in an update | - |  | Comment: report it rather than delete a file they paid the bandwidth for. |
| Maximum response length | `ai.maxTokens` | choice | 8,192 | keep | user rewriting a long document without truncation | ok |  | ~6,000 words covers any text field; reasoning models silently get 4x. Uncapped risks surprise API spend. |
| Context size (on-device) | `ai.localContextTokens` | choice | Model default (0) | keep | user trading RAM for a longer window | ok |  | KDoc: the whole window, prompt included; a phone pays for every token in memory and changing it reloads the model. |
| Target language for the Translate action | `ai.translateTo` | text | English | keep | anyone using the shipped Translate chip | split |  | English makes Translate a no-op for English typists. Device locale would be a better first guess when it isn't English. |
| Show model reasoning | `ai.showThinking` | toggle | Off | demote | someone debugging a slow local reasoning model | ok |  | KDoc: off hides <think> behind a progress bar and strips it from the result. Raw think text is noise for normal use. |
| Model picker on the panel | `ai.panelModelPicker` | toggle | On | demote | user with 2+ backends who wants panel height back | ok |  | Row already auto-hides below two choices, so the switch only matters to a multi-provider user who never switches. |
| Show a changes view | `ai.diffView` | toggle | On | keep | anyone running Fix grammar and wondering what moved | ok |  | KDoc: costs one chip and no work at all until pressed. Cheapest possible on-by-default. |
| Open a result on the changes | `ai.diffOpensFirst` | toggle | Off | keep | proofreader who always reads the diff first | ok |  | KDoc: off keeps the panel behaving as it always did. Correctly nested under the switch above. |
| Chat with the model | `-` | nav | - | keep | anyone wanting a conversation, not a one-shot rewrite | - |  | Whole undocumented feature behind one row; also the landing screen for a launcher shortcut. |
| Actions on the panel | `ai.customActions / actionOrder / hiddenActions` | nav | 8 shipped actions | keep | anyone with a repeated prompt they run daily | ok |  | Shows the visible count as its value, so the row answers "how many chips do I have" without opening. |
| History | `-` | nav | - | keep | user who needs "delete all" after turning history off | - |  | Comment: always reachable so "Delete all history" does not disappear with the switch that filled it. |
| Order of the actions | `ai.actionOrder` | list | shipped order | keep | user who only ever taps Fix grammar | ok |  | Empty stored order means the shipped order; the panel follows it exactly. Disabled below two items. |
| Action on/off (per action) | `ai.hiddenActions` | list | all 8 visible | keep | user cutting the chip row down to two | ok |  | Shipped actions are hidden, never deleted; the last visible one cannot be turned off or the panel is empty. |
| New action | `ai.customActions` | action | - | keep | user with a house style prompt | - |  | The whole reason the action list is editable rather than fixed. |
| Name (action editor) | `customActions[].name` | text | shipped name or "New action" | keep | anyone writing an action | ok |  | Blank falls back to the built-in name; the stored English name keys a shipped action. |
| Prompt | `customActions[].task` | text | shipped task text | keep | anyone tuning a shipped action's wording | ok |  | You write the task, not the frame; minLines=8 because a prompt is a paragraph. {target} expands to the translate language. |
| Type the prompt at every run | `customActions[].askEachRun` | toggle | Off (On for Custom) | keep | user who wants an ad-hoc instruction box | ok |  | This switch is what makes the shipped Custom chip behave the way it does. |
| Start from the saved prompt | `customActions[].prefillPrompt` | toggle | Off | merge | user editing a template each run | ok |  | Only ever shown under the switch above; the pair is really one three-way choice: saved / ask blank / ask prefilled. |
| Works with an empty field | `customActions[].worksWithoutText` | toggle | Off | keep | user whose action writes text from nothing | ok |  | Off matches the seven shipped actions that transform existing text; only Custom needs it on. |
| Read the text before the cursor | `customActions[].inputMode` | toggle | Off (whole field) | keep | user building their own Continue variant | ok |  | Whole field is right for seven of eight shipped actions; selection always wins over this anyway. |
| Add the result to the end | `customActions[].insertMode` | toggle | Off (replace) | keep | user who wants append-but-read-the-whole-field | ok |  | Split from the switch above on purpose: separating them makes a combination Continue could not express. |
| Answer with the text only | `customActions[].outputOnly` | toggle | On | keep | user writing an Explain-style action | ok |  | On for everything except Explain, whose answer is prose about the text rather than a replacement. |
| Write the whole prompt | `customActions[].rawPrompt` | toggle | Off | demote | prompt engineer writing their own safety wording | ok |  | Off keeps the injection guard that stops field text being read as commands. Also how unrecognised legacy prompts are preserved. |
| Reset / Delete this action | `ai.customActions` | action | - | keep | user who broke a shipped prompt | - |  | For a shipped action this drops the override and the original returns; only user-written actions get a true delete. |
| Keep a history of the results | `ai.historyEnabled` | toggle | Off | keep | user chasing "why does this keep failing" | ok |  | KDoc: the records are the user's own writing, so it stays off until asked; turning it off deletes what was stored. |
| Number of records to keep | `ai.historyMax` | slider | 100 (range 10–500) | keep | user on a small phone capping a 4,000-char-per-side log | ok |  | Each side is capped at 4,000 chars, so 100 records is a bounded few hundred KB. Only meaningful once history is on. |
| Search the history | `-` | text | empty | keep | user finding one answer among a hundred | - |  | Searches input, output and model name; only drawn once there are entries. |
| Filter by action | `-` | choice | All actions | demote | heavy user isolating one action's failures | ok |  | Only drawn when more than one action appears in the data, so the row stays short. |
| Delete all history | `-` | action | - | keep | anyone clearing their own writing off the device | - |  | Confirms with a count first; the same deleteStorage the off switch runs. |
| Model chips (chat) | `-` | choice | last used, else first available | keep | user comparing a local model against a cloud one | ok |  | Hidden below two choices, same rule as the panel picker. Persisted per store, not in settings. |
| New chat / Delete chat | `-` | action | - | keep | anyone using chat at all | - |  | Delete is per-conversation only; there is no bulk clear and no off switch (see missing). |
| Enable | `enabledTools (VOICE)` | toggle | On | keep | user who never dictates and wants the mic gone | ok |  | Dictation is a mainstream expectation; on by default is right. |
| Icon colour (start / end) | `toolColorOverrides[VOICE]` | action | Tool accent | demote | theme tinkerer | ok |  | Cosmetic, gated behind colourful tool icons; the end row only appears with gradients on. |
| Keyword | `toolKeywords[VOICE]` | text | dictate | keep | user who would rather type "voice" or "mic" | ok |  | "dictate" avoids firing on the far commoner word "voice". |
| Keyword is case sensitive | `toolKeywordCase[VOICE]` | toggle | Off | demote | nobody — "dictate" is never a proper noun | ok |  | Generic per-tool control that cannot be removed for one tool alone. |
| Recognition engine | `whisper.engine` | choice | System recognizer | keep | user who wants a hard no-audio-leaves-the-phone guarantee | ok |  | Whisper needs a download and does not stream words live; system works with zero setup. Hidden entirely on lite. |
| Voice typing view | `voiceBar.mode` | choice | Full panel | keep | user who fixes misheard words as they land (strip) | ok |  | Panel gives the biggest target and the level ring. All three surfaces carry collapse/expand buttons, so it is self-discoverable in place. |
| Keep listening | `voiceContinuous` | toggle | On | keep | user dictating one short reply at a time | ok |  | Sentence-at-a-time re-tapping is worse for the common case; two silent retries then idle stops an abandoned open mic. |
| Spoken punctuation | `voiceSpokenPunctuation` | toggle | On | keep | user who actually says the word "period" out loud | ok |  | Matches every mainstream dictation engine; only applies to finished phrases, and only English and Bengali are recognised. |
| Translate to English | `whisper.translate` | toggle | Off | keep | user speaking L1 and typing English | ok |  | Off is transcription, the obvious job. Shown even when the routed model cannot translate (Turbo, all 19 single-language graphs) — it silently does nothing there. |
| Model per language | `whisper.modelByLang` | list | Auto (no pins) | keep | bilingual user pinning a German-only graph to German | ok |  | Routing resolves from the active layout's language; auto ranks downloaded models per language, so a pin is the exception. |
| Browse the catalog + size filter | `-` | choice | collapsed, no size filter | demote | user hunting a specific Whisper size | ok |  | Collapsed by default because "Yours" and "Suggested" answer the question for most people; single-language graphs only appear for enabled languages. |
| Whisper model download / delete | `-` | list | nothing downloaded | keep | anyone using the offline engine | ok |  | Deleting clears any pin pointing at it and the fallback if it was that model; sole remaining model is auto-adopted. |
| Free up (orphaned Whisper files) | `-` | action | - | keep | user reclaiming space after a catalog change | - |  | Same pattern as the on-device LLM screen: report the bytes, let the user delete. |

**Missing here:** Global fallback Whisper model (whisper.modelId is only auto-adopted or cleared on delete; docs list it as a setting but no control writes it.); Keep chat conversations / Delete all chats (Chat transcripts persist with no off switch and no bulk delete, while the less sensitive AI history has both.); Download models over Wi-Fi only (Metered confirm is hard-coded at 500 MB for LLMs and 150 MB for Whisper; a 400 MB model downloads silently on mobile data.); Hold-to-talk threshold (600 ms decides tap-toggle vs walkie-talkie; slow or tremor-affected taps hit the wrong one every time.); Text before the cursor sent by Continue (Hard-coded 4,000 characters; a long-form writer's Continue loses the earlier context silently.)

**Docs:**
- `MISSING: AI chat` — gap: The whole AI chat feature (Tools › AI › Chat with the model, conversation list, per-chat model picker, launcher shortcut landing, 50-conversation / 200-message store) is documented nowhere on the site. Grep for "AI chat" across docs/src/content/docs returns nothing, and ai.mdx's Options list omits the Chat nav row.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/voice.mdx` — wrong: Options table lists "Compact bar \| Off" as a toggle. The setting is a three-way ChoiceSetting titled "Voice typing view" (Full panel / Compact bar over the keys / Collapsed bar, keyboard hidden), default Full panel. "Starting dictation" repeats the two-state framing.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/voice.mdx` — gap: The third surface — the collapsed draggable bar that hides the keyboard, with its snap/vertical/edge/dock persistence and expand-vs-keyboard exit button — is not described anywhere.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/whisper.mdx` — wrong: Options table lists "Global fallback model \| None selected" as a user setting. WhisperModelManager states there is deliberately no global picker; whisper.modelId is only auto-adopted when one model is on disk or cleared when that model is deleted.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/voice.mdx` — stale: SettingsPath components read "Tools / Voice typing / Dictation / …". The group as drawn is "Voice typing" (tooldetail_voice_dictation_group), not "Dictation".
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/whisper.mdx` — stale: "Tap a model in the catalog to download it" — tapping a Whisper row expands its details; the trailing Download button starts the transfer.
- `docs/src/content/docs/tools/ai.mdx` — gap: Options section omits the Hugging Face access token field, the Get a token link, Import your own model, and the Free up orphaned files action, all of which are drawn on the same screen under the On-device provider.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/SettingsUi.kt:673 — ResetSetting restore icon, shown whenever a value differs from its default`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2080 — ToggleSetting reset (Show reasoning, Model picker, Changes view, Open on changes, Keep listening, Spoken punctuation, Translate to English, Keep history, Keyword case)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2280 — SliderSetting reset (Number of records to keep)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2385 — ChoiceSetting reset (Recognition engine, Voice typing view)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11499 — TextFieldSetting reset (every per-provider Model field, server addresses, Translate target language)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11325 — ToolColourRow: colour picker's Reset arm, restores the tool accent`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:11400 — ToolKeywordSetting: "Reset to defaults" row, appears only when the keyword list was edited`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:13311 — AiActionEditor: Reset (shipped action) drops the override so the original returns; Delete for user-written ones`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/WhisperSettings.kt:408 — routing dialog's "Auto" option, writes an empty pin to fall back to automatic routing`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/AiHistoryScreen.kt:113 — turning history off also deletes the stored log`

_Coverage: Read in full: SettingsRepository.kt AiSettings (33 fields, lines 1950–2054), VoiceBarSettings (2193–2231), WhisperSettings (2237–2254), voiceContinuous/voiceSpokenPunctuation KDoc (1383–1390), isWhisperEnabled (318–323); MainActivity.kt AiToolSettings 10953–11290, ToolColourRow 11301–11344, ToolKeywordSetting 11345–11430, TokenPresetSetting 11439–11515, TextFieldSetting/ApiKeyField 11480–11550, generic tool-detail header 8940–8998, VOICE tool block 10147–10218, AiActionsSettings 13016–13113, AiActionEditor 13114–13330; LocalLlmSettings.kt, WhisperSettings.kt, AiChatScreens.kt, AiHistoryScreen.kt in full. Also read AiActions.kt (8 shipped actions), AiClient.kt default models/token ceilings, LocalLlmCatalog (8 models), WhisperCatalog (10 multilingual + 19 single-language = 29), SmartSuggest.defaultKeywords, and docs tools/ai.mdx, tools/voice.mdx, tools/whisper.mdx in full. Re-scanned every source range a second time and cross-checked with a grep for every setAi*/setHfToken/setWhisper*/setVoice* call site in app/ — no further UI entry exists. Deliberately excluded as persisted state rather than settings: voiceBar.active/vertical/snap/rightEdge/yBias/dockBias/returnMode/inline (written only by drag and collapse gestures in WMKeyboardService.kt:8895–9107, no settings control). Not covered: the AI panel's own on-keyboard controls (strip-markdown checkbox, Report button, instruction box), which are panel UI rather than settings entries; power-saving's override of the Whisper engine, which belongs to the power-saving domain; and the shared ToolbarTool enable/pin/toolbox-order machinery beyond the two rows drawn on these two tool screens. Gap in reset coverage worth flagging: TokenPresetSetting (Maximum response length, Context size), the provider chip row, the Compute chip row, and ReorderSetting (Order of the actions) carry no reset affordance at all._

### Rows, symbol sets, modes

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Symbol row | `symbolRowEnabled` | toggle | Off | keep | coders, email/URL typists wanting @gmail.com, https://, brackets one tap away | ok |  | Costs a whole row of screen height for characters most people never need. Off is right. |
| Row order (Emoji / Toolbar / Symbol) | `barOrder` | list | Emoji, Suggestions & toolbar, Symbol | demote | the few running emoji row and symbol row at once | ok |  | emojiBarMode and symbolRowEnabled both default off, so this group reorders one row. Hide it until an optional row is on. |
| Symbol sets (enable checkboxes) | `symbolRowSetIds` | list | All five built-ins enabled | keep | symbol-row users who want one set, no picker chip | ok |  | Row hides the picker chip when only one set is checked; last box can't be unchecked. Sane. |
| Edit symbol set (per set) | `customSymbolSets` | nav | Shipped characters | keep | anyone whose trade needs symbols we didn't ship | - |  | Editing a built-in stores an override under the same id, so modes keep pointing at it. Well designed. |
| New symbol set | `customSymbolSets` | action | No custom sets | keep | power users building a personal snippet strip | - |  | Cheap: one action row, zero cost when unused. |
| Name (symbol set) | `customSymbolSets` | text | Shipped name, or "My set" | keep | anyone naming their own set | ok |  | Blank falls back to the built-in name or "My set", so a set is never unnamed. |
| Characters & snippets | `customSymbolSets` | text | Shipped characters | keep | the whole point of the editor | ok |  | Whitespace-split, so multi-char snippets like https:// are one entry. Save disabled while blank. |
| Reset set / Delete set | `customSymbolSets` | action | Hidden until edited | keep | users who broke a built-in set editing it | - |  | Reset just drops the override; delete also clears the set from modes and the row. Correct. |
| Keyboard modes (list) | `keyboardModes` | list | 6 seeded modes | keep | everyone — password/chat/coding modes fire without setup | ok |  | Seeds do useful work silently; Passwords mode alone (no network tools by a password box) justifies it. |
| New mode | `keyboardModes` | action | — | keep | users with a per-app arrangement the six seeds miss | - |  | Not written to disk until first edit, so backing out costs nothing. |
| Drags edit the active mode | `modeToolOrderEdits` | toggle | On | REMOVE | nobody | ok |  | Off makes drags write globally while the mode's list still wins on screen — the drag looks like it did nothing. Its own info text says so. |
| Name (mode) | `keyboardModes[].name` | text | Mode-specific / "New mode" | keep | anyone picking a mode by hand from the Modes tool | ok |  | Blank falls back to "Unnamed". Needed to tell modes apart in the tool. |
| Icon (mode) | `keyboardModes[].icon` | choice | Mode-specific; generic for new modes | keep | Modes-tool users scanning a list of six icons | ok |  | 36 chips, no text labels, no clear option. Cosmetic but it is the only glyph in the Modes tool. |
| Emoji row (mode) | `keyboardModes[].emojiBarMode` | choice | Inherit (Chat: Row, Passwords: Off) | keep | chat users who want the emoji row only in chats | ok |  | Inherit-by-default is right; the two seeds that override it have obvious reasons (chat wants emoji, password boxes don't). |
| Symbol row (mode) | `keyboardModes[].symbolRowEnabled` | choice | Inherit (Email/Browser/Writing/Coding: On, Passwords: Off) | keep | users who want the symbol row only where it pays | ok |  | This is how the globally-off symbol row still reaches people: it turns itself on in a URL bar or a code editor. |
| Theme (mode) | `keyboardModes[].themeId` | choice | Inherit | keep | users wanting a visual tell that a password mode is live | ok |  | Hard override — beats the hand-picked theme and stands down auto light/dark for the mode's life. KDoc justifies it. |
| Custom pinned tools | `keyboardModes[].toolbarTools` | toggle | Off (on for Passwords/Chat/Writing) | keep | users wanting GIF pinned in chats but not in a bank app | ok |  | Turning on seeds from the current toolbar (replace) or empty (append), so neither start is a cliff. |
| Pinned tools behaviour | `keyboardModes[].toolbarToolsAppend` | choice | Replace mine | keep | users adding chat tools without losing their own pins | ok |  | Switching to append drops tools already in the global pins, so nothing appears twice. Both halves earn their place. |
| Pinned tool chips | `keyboardModes[].toolbarTools` | list | Copy of global pins | keep | anyone who turned Custom pinned tools on | - |  | Only offers globally-enabled, build-supported, usable tools, so a lite build never shows a dead chip. |
| Modify pinned tool order | `keyboardModes[].toolbarTools` | nav | Chip tap order | keep | users editing an existing pin list rather than building one | - |  | Chips already set order by tap sequence, but reordering an existing list otherwise means unchecking everything. |
| Custom toolbox order | `keyboardModes[].toolboxOrder` | toggle | Off (all six seeds define one) | keep | users tired of scrolling the toolbox for the same tool | ok |  | Partial list is enough — unnamed tools keep their global rank behind these. Low-commitment. |
| Toolbox chips | `keyboardModes[].toolboxOrder` | list | Empty when first turned on | keep | anyone who turned Custom toolbox order on | ok |  | Seeds empty rather than copying the global order, which is right — a partial list is the intended use. |
| Modify toolbox order | `keyboardModes[].toolboxOrder` | nav | Chip tap order | keep | same users, editing an existing toolbox list | - |  | Same rationale as the pinned-tool reorder. |
| Custom symbol sets (mode) | `keyboardModes[].symbolSetIds` | toggle | Off (on for Email/Browser/Writing/Coding) | keep | users who want the Web set in a URL bar, Coding in an editor | ok |  | Seeds from the current enabled sets, guards against emptying to zero. This is what makes the symbol row automatic. |
| Symbol set chips (mode) | `keyboardModes[].symbolSetIds` | list | Copy of global enabled sets | keep | anyone who turned Custom symbol sets on | - |  | Offers every set built-in and custom; last chip cannot be removed. |
| Modify symbol set order | `keyboardModes[].symbolSetIds` | nav | Chip tap order | keep | users choosing which set the row opens on in a mode | - |  | Load-bearing, not cosmetic: applyMode sets symbolRowActiveSetId from the first entry. |
| Field types | `keyboardModes[].fieldKinds` | list | Mode-specific | keep | everyone — this is how Passwords/Email/Browser modes fire | ok |  | Seven chips now (Notification reply added in seed 3). Password excluded from Text so a Text mode can't take a login form. |
| Bound apps / Add app | `keyboardModes[].apps` | list | Mode-specific (Chat 15, Writing 14, Coding 4) | keep | users adding a niche chat or editor app we didn't seed | ok |  | Picker lists launcher apps only, no QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES. Apps AND fields must both match when both are set. |
| Reset to default (mode) | `keyboardModes` | action | Shown only on the six seeds | keep | users who edited a seeded mode into a corner | - |  | Matched by id so a renamed built-in keeps it; asks to confirm first. |
| Delete mode | `keyboardModes` | action | — | keep | users who don't want a seeded mode | - |  | But it deletes with no confirmation, from both the list and the editor, while the reversible Reset does confirm. Backwards. |
| Number row | `numberRow` | toggle | On | keep | anyone typing digits — saves a layer switch per number | ok | yes | KDoc: every phone this decade has room for a sixth row. Genuinely ~50/50 and a whole row of height — already asked on the wizard's gestures page. |
| Number row height | `numberRowHeightDp` | slider | 42 dp (tablet 40/38) | keep | small-screen users clawing back height from the digit row | ok |  | Shorter than the 48 dp letter keys by default, which is the point: digits get fast access at less height cost. |
| Symbols on shift | `layoutBehavior.numberRowShiftSymbols` | toggle | Off | keep | coders wanting = \ < > [ ] { } \| ~ without a layer switch | ok |  | Off is right: shift silently changing the digit row would startle everyone else. Niche but a real win for its user. |
| Number row in symbols | `layoutBehavior.numberRowInSymbols` | toggle | On | keep | small-screen users shortening the ?123 layer by a row | ok |  | On is a net gain, not a duplicate: the renderer swaps the symbols layer's own digit row for the bracket fill row. |
| Fix number-row slips | `suggestionStrip.numberRowCorrections` | toggle | On | keep | number-row users who fat-finger a digit into a word | ok |  | Only ever a same-length single-digit swap, so "room3" is never shortened. Safe enough to default on. |

**Missing here:** Symbol row height (Hard-coded 40 dp. The number row next to it has a 32-100 dp slider; the symbol row has nothing.); Per-mode autocorrect / auto-capitalize override (Coding mode still autocorrects identifiers. applyMode overrides tools, theme and rows but no typing behaviour.); Per-mode layout / language (KeyboardMode has no layout field, so a bilingual user cannot pin a Latin layout to their code editor.); How long a manual mode pick lasts (Hard-coded to clear on the next app switch. Users who pick a mode deliberately often want it to stick.); Reset row order (barOrder is the only setting in this domain with no reset affordance; every toggle around it has one.); Confirm before deleting a mode (Delete is unconfirmed in both list and editor; the reversible Reset to default does confirm. Inverted.)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/modes.mdx` — wrong: Line 66: "Field types is a row of chips for the six kinds ... Password, Email, URL, Number, Phone, Text". ModeField has seven entries — Notification reply is missing, and it is the one that matches with app bindings ignored.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/modes.mdx` — wrong: Line 85: Chat mode's apps listed as 6 (Messenger, Instagram, Telegram, WhatsApp, Signal, Discord). ChatApps now has 15 — Facebook, FB Lite, TikTok, Snapchat, Viber, LINE, KakaoTalk, WeChat, Beeper added in seed 3. Its field types also omit Notification reply.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/layout.mdx` — wrong: Line 30: Number row default given as "Off (On on a tablet)". KeyboardSettings.numberRow is true for every device; the tablet override in DeviceFormDefaults is now a leftover.
- `docs/src/content/docs/typing/modes.mdx` — stale: Line 86: same stale 6-app Chat binding list as the reference page.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/modes.mdx` — stale: Line 89 documents the seed-2 upgrade (Chat and Writing appended) but not seed 3, which topped up nine Chat apps and the Notification reply field on already-stored modes.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/rows.mdx` — gap: No mention that the symbol row is suppressed while the Symbols panel is up or any full-bleed panel is open (symbolRowVisible), nor that its 40 dp height is fixed and unlike the number row has no slider.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:12773 — Symbol row toggle passes default, giving the row an inline ResetSetting control`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:12958 — "Reset set" (built-in) / "Delete set" (custom) in SymbolSetEditor`, `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:7695 — deleteSymbolSet drops the override for a built-in, restoring shipped characters`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:13613 — "Drags edit the active mode" toggle passes default`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:13684 — mode Emoji row "Inherit" option (reset-to-global)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:13696 — mode Symbol row "Inherit" option`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:13711 — "Clear" button on a mode's Theme row, plus an Inherit row in ModeThemePickerDialog (app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:324)`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:13736 / 13814 / 13858 — the three "Custom …" toggles set their field back to null, i.e. inherit`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:14004 — "Reset to default" on the six seeded modes, with a confirm dialog at 14024; backed by SettingsRepository.kt:7831`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:5110, 5122, 5131, 5140 — the four number-row rows all pass default`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2655 — "Fix number-row slips" passes default`

_Coverage: Line ranges in the brief were stale; the real ones are MainActivity.kt 12734-13003 (barRowTitle/barRowSubtitle/RowsSettings/SymbolSetEditor) and 13332-14139 (modeBindingsSummary through AppPickerDialog). Read both in full, twice. Also read KeyboardModes.kt in full, SettingsRepository.kt KDoc for symbolRowEnabled/symbolRowSetIds/symbolRowActiveSetId/customSymbolSets/barOrder/modeToolOrderEdits/keyboardModes/numberRow/numberRowHeightDp/numberRowShiftSymbols/numberRowInSymbols/numberRowCorrections, SymbolSets.kt, ModeThemePickerDialog in ThemeScreens.kt, the render side (KeyboardScreen.kt SymbolRowStrip, symbolRowVisible, numberRowShown, bodyRows swap) and the field-kind derivation in WMKeyboardService.kt:2845. Docs read: reference/settings/rows.mdx and modes.mdx in full, plus targeted checks of typing/modes.mdx and reference/settings/layout.mdx. Overlap warning for the parent: the five number-row entries are drawn on the Layout & size screen (MainActivity.kt:5103-5143) and the Typing screen (2648-2657), not on Rows & bars — they were assigned to me as fields, so the layout/typing agents may report them too; dedupe there. Not covered (other domains): the emojiBarMode setting itself on the Emoji screen (Row order only mirrors it), the global toolbar/toolbox tool lists a mode overrides, the theme picker's own contents, the snippets group earlier in strings_rows.xml, and the two nav rows into my screens (Home at MainActivity.kt:1427/1434 and the Modes tool's \"Edit keyboard modes\" at 10595)._

### Themes, editor, photo backgrounds

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Mode | `themeMode` | choice | System | keep | anyone who wants the app dark on a light phone | ok |  | Only affects the settings app and the Default theme. Onboarding's Look page already asks it, so no second question. |
| Material You colors | `dynamicColor` | toggle | On | keep | someone who wants flat colours instead of wallpaper tint | ok |  | OnboardingPages KDoc: "on for everyone, and the person who wants flat colours finds the switch in Appearance". |
| Auto theme | `autoTheme.enabled` | toggle | Off | keep | users whose phone flips light/dark daily | ok |  | When on it seizes theme selection entirely; off is the only safe default. Wizard offers the pair already. |
| Light theme | `autoTheme.lightThemeId` | nav | Default (system) | keep | auto-theme users; half the pair is meaningless without it | ok |  | Only shown while Auto theme is on. Radio list scrolls to the current pick. |
| Dark theme | `autoTheme.darkThemeId` | nav | Default (system) | keep | auto-theme users; the other half of the pair | ok |  | Same as the light slot; both default to Default so turning Auto on changes nothing visible until picked. |
| Trigger | `autoTheme.trigger` | choice | System light/dark | keep | night-shift workers and people wanting real sunset timing | ok |  | System is what nearly everyone means by auto; Sun falls back to System with no saved location. |
| Light theme from | `autoTheme.dayStartMinutes` | nav | 7:00 | keep | schedule users only | ok |  | Only shown under the Schedule trigger. isBetween wraps midnight so a night-shift window works. |
| Dark theme from | `autoTheme.nightStartMinutes` | nav | 19:00 | keep | schedule users only | ok |  | Only shown under the Schedule trigger. |
| Theme gallery style | `appUi.themeGalleryStyle` | choice | Auto | demote | someone who finds family cards with dots confusing | ok |  | Auto already resolves from the persona quiz. A three-way segmented row above the grid to restyle a list is over-served. |
| Create theme | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone building a theme | - |  | Primary action of the screen; seeds a dark theme from the first swatch and opens the editor. |
| Import theme | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone given a .wmtheme.json file | - |  | Permissive MIME list because providers report .json as text/plain. Applies immediately after import. |
| Import FlorisBoard theme | `-` | action | — | demote | FlorisBoard migrants, once, ever | - |  | A once-in-a-lifetime migration action holding permanent space beside Create and Import. Belongs in an overflow menu. |
| Theme card (select) | `keyboardThemeId` | list | Default (system) | keep | everyone; this is the point of the screen | ok |  | Default follows Material You and the mode, so it is right until the user has an opinion. Wizard offers a shortlist. |
| Card edit / export / delete | `customThemes` | action | — | keep | theme makers and sharers | - |  | Built-ins get edit (duplicates) and export but never delete. Delete confirms and removes both image files. |
| Pin to Themes tool | `toolbarBehavior.themesPanelBuiltIns` | toggle | 6 built-ins pinned | keep | people who flip looks mid-typing | ok |  | Six-theme default spread of dark/light/AMOLED/animated. Per look, not per family, matching what the panel lists. |
| Variant swatch dots | `keyboardThemeId` | list | parent look | keep | users of Ocean/Snow/Catppuccin families | ok |  | Only drawn on family cards in grouped mode; tapping both previews and applies, with no second level. |
| Theme add-ons | `-` | nav | — | keep | someone who has exhausted the built-ins | - |  | Placed after the built-ins deliberately. Addon-store behaviour belongs to the addons domain. |
| Looks (variant chips, Add look, delete) | `customThemes[].variants` | list | one look | keep | theme makers shipping a colourway set | ok |  | Max 12 looks. Add copies the open look and shares its image paths; deleting a variant leaves files for the sweep. |
| Family name | `customThemes[].familyName` | text | empty (falls back to parent name) | keep | theme makers naming a set | ok |  | Only shown once a family has variants. Blank falls back to the parent's own name. |
| Theme name | `customThemes[].name` | text | My theme | keep | anyone with more than one theme | ok |  | Local-echo field; blank writes "Untitled" rather than an empty card label. |
| Dark theme | `customThemes[].dark` | toggle | On (new themes are dark) | keep | anyone building a light theme | split |  | Flipping it reseeds every colour. New themes are always born dark regardless of the phone's mode. |
| Seed swatch row | `customThemes[].* (reseed)` | action | 15 swatches | keep | anyone who wants a theme without 30 colour taps | ok |  | reseeded() keeps gradients, images, shape, borders, radii and animation; carries board alpha across when a photo is set. |
| Board background | `customThemes[].boardBackground` | choice | #17181C | keep | every theme maker; also the scrim over a photo | ok |  | Alpha-capable, and its alpha is the only dim control over a background photo. Doubles as the scrim. |
| Board gradient | `customThemes[].boardGradient` | toggle | Off | keep | gradient-theme makers | ok |  | Switch seeds a 135° linear blend, then type + angle + 2-4 alpha stops. Same editor as the key gradient. |
| Background image | `customThemes[].backgroundImage` | nav | None | keep | anyone wanting a photo behind their keys | ok |  | Dialog offers device / online / collection / remove. Applying zeroes board alpha and softens keys to 62%. |
| Crop image (and landscape) | `-` | action | Keyboard 2.4:1 | keep | anyone whose photo is not keyboard-shaped | ok |  | Three fixed presets, pinch/pan, cover-scaled so there is never a gap. Irreversible: writes a new JPEG, deletes the source. |
| Image opacity | `customThemes[].backgroundImageOpacity` | slider | 100% | keep | anyone fading a busy photo | ok |  | Only shown once an image is set. Shared by both orientations. |
| Image blur | `customThemes[].backgroundImageBlur` | slider | 0 (off) | keep | anyone whose photo eats the key labels | ok |  | 0-25. Mutually exclusive with the animated switch: raising blur zeroes backgroundAnimated. |
| Play animated image | `customThemes[].backgroundAnimated` | toggle | Off | AUTOMATE | anyone who picked a GIF and expected it to move | WRONG → On when the picked file is animated |  | Picking a GIF silently shows one frame until a switch further down is found. The decoder already knows the file is animated. |
| Landscape background image | `customThemes[].backgroundImageLandscape` | nav | None | keep | tablet and landscape typists | ok |  | Always shown, even with no portrait image. Falls back to the portrait image when empty. |
| Photo credit row | `customThemes[].backgroundPhoto` | nav | shown when a service photo is set | keep | nobody chooses it; both services require it | - |  | Licence obligation, not a preference. Stored on the theme so it survives export and works offline. |
| Rotating background | `photoBackground.*` | nav | Off | keep | users who already have a photo collection | ok |  | Row hidden until the collection is non-empty or rotation is on — good, but it is also the only way in. |
| Photo services | `photoBackground.*ApiKey` | nav | hidden | keep | people who hit the shared build key's quota | ok |  | Hidden until a key is set or a service photo is kept. The picker's "Add a key" also reaches it. |
| Key shape | `customThemes[].keyShape` | choice | Rounded | keep | every theme maker | ok |  | 12 shapes in a dialog that draws each at real key proportions, because names alone say nothing. |
| Key colours (8 slots) | `customThemes[].keyBackground + 7` | choice | seeded; 4 of 8 are Auto | keep | every theme maker | ok |  | Letter, text, modifier, modifier text, enter, enter icon, pressed, border. Nullable ones show "Auto" and reset to it. |
| Key border width | `customThemes[].keyBorderWidthDp` | slider | 0 dp | keep | low-contrast themes like Solarized | split |  | Only appears once a colour is set, then starts at 0 — so setting a border colour appears to do nothing. |
| Key gradient | `customThemes[].keyGradient` | toggle | Off | keep | theme makers wanting a key sheen | ok |  | Same gradient editor as the board; seeds a subtle 90° white sheen. |
| Key textures (6 slots) | `customThemes[].keyTexture*, popupTexture` | list | unset | keep | pixel-art and sticker-style theme makers | ok |  | Normal, modifier, enter, space, pressed, popup. Stored at 512px PNG; sensible fallback chains mean one image themes the board. |
| Texture fit / opacity | `customThemes[].keyTextureScale, keyTextureOpacity` | choice | Crop, 100% | keep | pixel-art themes need Tile | ok |  | Only shown once a texture exists. Crop never distorts, which is the right default. |
| Single-key styles | `customThemes[].keyOverrides` | list | none | keep | themes that recolour Enter or the spacebar | ok |  | Five nullable colours per key, keyed by lowercase letter or action name so the style follows the letter across layouts. |
| Stickers (decals) | `customThemes[].decals` | list | none, max 6 | keep | decorative-theme makers | ok |  | Position, size, rotation and opacity sliders over a live mini preview. Purely visual; the layer takes no touches. |
| Accent | `customThemes[].accent` | choice | #8AB4F8 | keep | every theme maker | ok |  | Drives shift tint, active tools, gesture trail and panel buttons — one of the four required colours. |
| Gesture trail colour | `customThemes[].gestureTrailColor` | choice | Auto (accent) | keep | glide typists on themes where accent is invisible | ok |  | Alpha-capable. Auto following accent is right for nearly every theme. |
| Popup colours (background, text, border) | `customThemes[].popup*` | choice | Auto | keep | theme makers whose derived bubble reads wrong | ok |  | All three Auto; the derived values are contrast-computed so most themes never touch them. |
| Popup border width | `customThemes[].popupBorderWidthDp` | slider | 0 dp | keep | outlined-bubble themes | split |  | Same trap as the key border: appears only after a colour, then starts at zero width. |
| Popup shape | `customThemes[].popupShape` | choice | follows Key press setting | keep | themes wanting bubbles unlike the global shape | ok |  | Moved out of the Corners group so a theme need not enable custom radii to reach it. |
| Popup placement | `customThemes[].popupPlacement` | choice | Default (follow settings) | keep | a shared theme that needs the bubble style to travel | ok |  | Default correctly leaves the user's own global choice alone; only a published theme has reason to override it. |
| Menu shape | `customThemes[].menuShape` | choice | Automatic (safe derivative) | demote | a theme author overruling the safety rule; very few | ok |  | safeContainerKind already picks right: decorative shapes fall back to rounded because a slanted menu clips its rows. |
| Toolbar shape + colours (5 slots) | `customThemes[].toolShape, toolbarIcon, toolCircle*, toolBorder*` | choice | shape follows global; colours Auto | keep | theme makers whose toolbar disappears into the board | ok |  | Icons, resting circle, active circle, outline plus its width row. Same colour-then-width pattern as the key border. |
| Panels: cards & suggestion text | `customThemes[].chipBackground, suggestionText` | choice | Auto | keep | themes whose keys invert against their board | ok |  | Suggestion text is contrast-guarded against the board, so cream-key-on-black themes still get a legible strip. |
| Chips: colours (4 slots) + width | `customThemes[].chipText, chipActive*, chipBorder*` | choice | Auto, 0 dp | keep | theme makers who care about tool panels | ok |  | Resting text, selected fill, selected text, outline. All derive from the theme; a plain theme never opens this group. |
| Chip shape / radius | `customThemes[].chipShape, chipCornerRadiusDp` | choice | Rounded, 12 dp | keep | themes matching chips to their key shape | ok |  | Radius row only appears for rounded and cut, the two shapes that read it. |
| Card shape | `customThemes[].cardShape` | choice | Automatic (safe derivative) | demote | a theme author overruling the safety rule; very few | ok |  | Same rule as the menu shape: a scalloped card eats its own first line, so the derived answer is nearly always right. |
| Theme-specific corner radii | `customThemes[].keyCornerRadiusDp (sentinel)` | toggle | Off | keep | themes whose look depends on a radius | ok |  | Off means follow the global sliders, which is right for most themes. Switch seeds and clears all three together. |
| Key / popup / tool radius sliders | `customThemes[].keyCornerRadiusDp, popupCornerRadiusDp, toolCircleRadiusDp` | slider | seeded from globals (8 / 12 / 20 dp) | keep | theme makers who need a fixed radius | ok |  | Seeded from the live globals, so turning the switch on changes nothing visually until dragged. |
| Layout for this theme | `customThemes[].toolbarHeightDp (sentinel)` | toggle | Off | keep | pixel/display themes needing bigger keys to work | ok |  | One switch seeds or clears eleven overrides. Off is right: a theme resizing someone's keyboard uninvited is hostile. |
| Theme layout sliders (11) | `customThemes[].toolWidthDp … gestureTrailOpacity` | slider | seeded from globals | keep | theme authors shipping a complete look | ok |  | Tool width, toolbar/popup/key height, gap, side pad, font, bold, hint, trail width and opacity. Per-screen sizing still wins. |
| Animation | `customThemes[].animation` | choice | None | keep | showcase themes like Aurora and Sunset drift | ok |  | Only runs while the keyboard is on screen. None is right: motion behind keys is a taste, not a baseline. |
| Animation speed | `customThemes[].animationSpeed` | slider | 1× | keep | animated-theme makers | ok |  | Only shown once animation is on. 0.25×-3× around a ~16 s cycle. |
| Key press effect | `customThemes[].keyEffect (+param, images)` | choice | None | keep | people who want confetti on every keypress | ok |  | Six kinds plus an emoji field and up to six custom images. Never plays under reduce motion or power saving. |
| Effect intensity | `customThemes[].keyEffectIntensity` | slider | 1× | keep | anyone whose confetti is too much confetti | ok |  | Only shown once an effect is chosen. |
| Key font (theme) | `customThemes[].fontId` | nav | Follow settings | keep | display-theme makers | ok |  | Loses to every non-Latin script's Noto face, deliberately, so a Latin display font cannot blank a Bengali board. |
| Add a font for a script | `customThemes[].scriptFontIds` | list | none | keep | pixel-theme makers on non-Latin scripts | ok |  | The only way a display theme keeps its look off Latin. Still loses to the user's own per-script pick. |
| Key sound (theme) | `customThemes[].soundStyle, soundCustomId` | nav | Follow settings | keep | authors pairing a theme with a sound pack | ok |  | Tapping a row previews it. A missing installed sound falls back to the global choice rather than going silent. |
| Start rotation (intro) | `photoBackground.rotateEnabled` | action | — | keep | first-time visitors | - |  | Off, the screen is one explanation and one button instead of fifteen rows for something not switched on. |
| Change the photo now (Shuffle) | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone on the Manual interval | - |  | Disabled below two pooled photos, since a shuffle that lands on the same picture reads as broken. |
| Change the background | `photoBackground.rotateEnabled` | toggle | Off | keep | people who want a new background daily | ok |  | Off is right: it downloads photos and repaints the board. Has a reset control. |
| How frequently | `photoBackground.interval` | nav | Daily | keep | anyone who finds daily too fast or too slow | ok |  | Six choices in a dialog because the names do not fit a chip row. Fetching is floored at one request an hour regardless. |
| Change the background of | `photoBackground.scope` | nav | The theme in use | keep | users of an auto light/dark pair | ok |  | Current-theme is the least surprising. Rotation is applied at draw time, so built-ins can rotate too. |
| Pick themes | `photoBackground.scopeThemeIds` | nav | none selected | keep | anyone choosing "Themes I select" | ok |  | Only shown under SELECTED_THEMES; without it that choice would select nothing. |
| Source: Photo collection | `photoBackground.sources (SAVED)` | toggle | Off | keep | anyone who added their own photos | WRONG → On |  | A user with photos in the collection but no service key turns rotation on and gets nothing. Their own picks should count first. |
| Source: Online photos | `photoBackground.sources (ONLINE)` | toggle | On | keep | people who want fresh photos without curating | ok |  | Turning it off hides its photos immediately but keeps the files, so re-enabling costs no download. |
| Subjects | `photoBackground.topics` | nav | none | keep | anyone who wants only nature or only architecture | ok |  | Only shown when the online source is on. Empty means each service's own selected photos. |
| Search words | `photoBackground.queries` | text | empty | keep | anyone with a specific subject in mind | ok |  | Comma-separated, trimmed and de-blanked. Has a reset control. |
| Wide photos only | `photoBackground.landscapeOnly` | toggle | On | keep | nobody turns it off deliberately; it is a quality gate | ok |  | KDoc: the board is a wide short strip whichever way the device is held. Arguably should be automatic rather than asked. |
| Safe photos only | `photoBackground.safeSearch` | toggle | On | keep | anyone who does not want a surprise on their keyboard | ok |  | On is the only defensible default for an unattended background feed. |
| Use mobile data | `photoBackground.fetchOnMetered` | toggle | Off | keep | users on unlimited data who want faster top-ups | ok |  | KDoc: a top-up nobody asked for should not cost mobile data. Rotation itself is never gated on it. |
| Photos to keep ready | `photoBackground.poolTarget` | slider | 8 | keep | users tight on storage, or wanting more variety | ok |  | 3-20, clamped on read. Below 4 a top-up becomes urgent. Has a reset control. |
| Photo collection | `-` | nav | — | keep | anyone reusing a photo across themes | - |  | Also reachable from the background-source dialog; storage total shown at the top of the grid. |
| Delete the photos the app got | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone reclaiming space | - |  | Wipes the pool directory without confirmation — cheap to undo (it refills), so no dialog is defensible. |
| Unsplash API key | `photoBackground.unsplashApiKey` | text | empty (build key used) | keep | anyone hitting the shared build key's 50/hour cap | ok |  | Saving clears the rate-limit memory and cached pages, which belonged to the old key. No reset control on the field. |
| Pexels API key | `photoBackground.pexelsApiKey` | text | empty (build key used) | keep | heavy photo browsers | ok |  | Same clear-on-save behaviour. 200 requests an hour on a free key. |
| Library tile menu (use / match colours / delete) | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone curating their collection | - |  | "Match colours" only appears when a seed colour was measured at download time, so it costs nothing to offer. |
| Service filter chips | `-` | choice | All | keep | someone who prefers one service's photography | ok |  | Only drawn when both services have a key. Session-local, kept across a trip into a photo page. |
| Subject chips | `-` | choice | none | keep | browsers with no search word in mind | ok |  | Picking a subject clears the query and vice versa; the slug is sent, never the translated label. |
| Landscape chip | `-` | toggle | On | keep | anyone who wants a portrait crop after all | ok |  | Session-local mirror of the rotation setting; the keyboard is a wide strip so on is right. |
| Colour filter (14 swatches) | `-` | choice | Any | keep | someone matching a photo to a theme's palette | ok |  | Circles because 14 labelled buttons fit no phone. A colour alone silently searches the colour's own name. |
| Refresh | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone who wants a different selection | - |  | Feeds are otherwise cached and coalesced, so this is the only way to spend a request deliberately. |
| Preview this photo for | `-` | choice | Upright | keep | anyone applying a photo to the landscape slot | ok |  | Changes nothing on the theme; it just re-renders the mock-up. Most typing is portrait. |
| Use it for (slot) | `-` | choice | Portrait | keep | tablet users and anyone wanting both slots | ok |  | Portrait is what a phone user means. BOTH applies twice, downloading a crop per orientation. |
| Apply / Save / Undo / Match colours | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone applying a service photo | - |  | The page stays open after Apply so the next likely actions are already in front of the user. Undo restores the whole family spec. |
| Readability fix (scrim / blur) | `-` | action | advisory | keep | anyone who picked a bright or busy photo | ok |  | Advisory rather than blocking, with a one-tap fix. Scrim is capped at Readability.MAX_SCRIM_ALPHA. |

**Missing here:** Match theme colours to each rotated photo (seedPalette is read by withRotation and has a repository setter, but no screen ever draws it — unreachable); Auto-scrim rotated photos for readability (readabilityGuard silently dims the board on every rotation with no row explaining or disabling it); New theme light/dark (Create theme is hard-coded dark=true and the first blue swatch, even on a phone set to Light); Photo pool storage cap (24 MB budget is fixed; the library screen shows the size but offers no way to raise or lower the ceiling); Key translucency when a photo is applied (62% is fixed; users who dislike it retune two or three colour rows by hand after every photo); Free crop aspect (Only 2.4:1, 1.7:1 and 1:1; a split or floating keyboard is neither, so the crop misses)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/themes.mdx` — wrong: Key shape table lists 11 shapes and the prose says "eleven names don't fit side by side"; KeyShapeKind has 12 — CIRCLE is missing
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/themes.mdx` — wrong: Popup corner radius documented as 0-24dp with "no matching global slider, always seeds to 12dp". Editor range is 0-40 and it seeds from the real global slider (Key press → Popup corner radius, MainActivity.kt:4552)
- `docs/src/content/docs/themes/editor.mdx` — wrong: Same popup-radius claim repeated ("seeded to 12dp, there's no matching global popup-radius slider"); also says "all eleven shapes" while its own table lists twelve
- `docs/src/content/docs/themes/overview.mdx` — wrong: "There are 26 built-in themes… the other eight". BuiltInThemes flattens to 28: Catppuccin Frappé and Macchiato were added and are unlisted
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/themes.mdx` — wrong: Same 26/8 built-in count, same two missing Catppuccin variants
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/themes.mdx` — gap: Billed as the field reference for the whole editor but omits nine whole groups: Looks/variants, key textures, single-key styles, stickers, key press effect, font and sound, chips and buttons, layout overrides, and the animated-background toggle
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/themes.mdx` — gap: Gallery section omits the Theme gallery style choice (Auto/Grouped/Flat), the Import FlorisBoard theme button, and the per-card pin-to-Themes-tool toggle
- `MISSING: theme families and looks` — gap: Nothing anywhere documents ThemeSpec.variants — family cards with swatch dots, the Looks chip row, Add look, Family name, or that a variant is a globally addressable theme id. Grep for "variants"/"Add look" across docs returns nothing in the themes tree
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/importing-other-keyboards.mdx` — stale: "A FlorisBoard theme that holds both a day and a night version becomes two themes" — groupAsFamily now makes one family entry with the second as a variant
- `docs/src/content/docs/themes/sharing.mdx` — gap: Never mentions the gallery's third import button (.flex FlorisBoard themes), nor that a grouped card exports the whole family while a flat card exports one look
- `docs/src/content/docs/themes/backgrounds.mdx` — stale: "two sliders sit right below the image row" then lists three bullets — Play animated image was added and the sentence was not updated
- `docs/src/content/docs/themes/online-photos.mdx` — gap: Accurate on every row that exists, but silent on the two rotation behaviours with no UI: readabilityGuard scrims every rotated photo and seedPalette can rewrite the palette. A user cannot find or stop either

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:3948 — NullableColorRow shows an "Auto" button in the colour picker whenever the field is overridden; onReset writes null (3950). Covers ~25 colour slots.`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:478 — KeyShapePickerDialog draws an "Automatic" radio row when onAuto is passed; used by the menu-shape and card-shape pickers (1921, 1937).`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:2606 — the Corners switch clears keyCornerRadiusDp, popupCornerRadiusDp and toolCircleRadiusDp together.`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:2678 — the Layout switch clears all eleven layout/type overrides together.`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:3647 — GradientEditor's switch off writes null, discarding the gradient.`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:3154 — ThemeFontPickerDialog's first row is "Follow settings" (writes null); ThemeSoundPickerDialog the same at 3274.`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:3056 — the per-script font picker's default row removes the scriptFontIds entry, restoring the script's Noto face.`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/ThemeScreens.kt:1667 — deleting a background image restores the board's alpha if applying the photo had zeroed it.`, `core/settings/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:5587 — clearThemePhoto does the same restore for the source-dialog Remove path (ThemeScreens.kt:1431).`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/PhotoSettingsScreens.kt:222 — ToggleSetting(default=) reset icon on Change the background; same at 311 (wide), 319 (safe), 327 (metered) and 599 (both source toggles).`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/PhotoSettingsScreens.kt:350 — SliderSetting(default=) reset on Photos to keep ready.`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/PhotoSettingsScreens.kt:299 — TextFieldSetting(default=) reset on Search words.`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/PhotoDetailScreens.kt:243 — Undo restores the whole pre-apply ThemeSpec family after applying a photo or reseeding from it.`, `GAP: the gallery's Mode, Material You and Auto-theme rows are raw ListItem+Switch/ChoiceControl with no default= reset; the two API-key fields have none either; and there is no "reset this theme" anywhere in the editor.`

_Coverage: Read in full: ThemeScreens.kt (all 4180 lines, in six passes, then re-scanned the group boundaries), PhotoSettingsScreens.kt, PhotoBackgroundUi.kt, PhotoBrowseScreens.kt, PhotoDetailScreens.kt, ThemeSpec.kt, PhotoBackgroundSettings.kt, ThemeOverrides.kt, RotationPool.kt, RotatingBackground.kt, plus the theme/photo KDoc blocks in SettingsRepository.kt (AutoThemeSettings 108-186, KeyboardSettings 910-930, AppUiSettings/ThemeGalleryStyle 2320-2346, setters 5443-5675) and OnboardingPages.kt LookPage (423-520) to confirm what the wizard already asks. Docs read in full: reference/settings/themes.mdx, themes/overview.mdx, editor.mdx, backgrounds.mdx, online-photos.mdx, sharing.mdx, plus the theme sections of reference/importing-other-keyboards.mdx. NOT covered: the keyboard-side Themes tool panel (toolbar/tools domain, though its pin toggle is reported here), the Icons and Keyboard font screens (separate domains), the addon-store install flow for themes, ThemePreview/KbTheme rendering internals, PaletteThemes colour values beyond counting entries, and docs/themes/fonts.mdx and icon-packs.mdx. Theme-editor colour rows are grouped by family as instructed — slot counts are in each reason; the raw total is roughly 45 individual colour slots across the editor plus 5 more per single-key override._

### Privacy, backup, storage, statistics

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Permissions | `-` | nav | n/a | keep | anyone auditing what the keyboard can reach | - |  | Inventory screen, not a request funnel. Only place to pre-grant ahead of a feature. |
| Learn from typing | `learnFromTyping` | toggle | On | keep | privacy-conscious user on a shared or work phone | ok |  | Master switch for the on-device lexicon; suggestions are worthless without it, so on is right. |
| Add words to the system dictionary | `addWordsToSystemDictionary` | toggle | Off | keep | user who also runs another keyboard or the platform spell checker | ok |  | KDoc: only setting here that writes outside the app; on-device lexicon already covers this keyboard. |
| Expand dictionary shortcuts | `suggestionStrip.expandUserDictShortcuts` | toggle | Off | keep | Gboard migrant with omw-style shortcuts already defined | WRONG → On |  | Zero cost with no shortcuts defined; AOSP and Gboard expand them by default, so off silently breaks a migrated setup. |
| Incognito mode | `incognito` | toggle | Off | keep | user typing something they don't want learned | ok |  | A permanently-on incognito is just learnFromTyping off; a sticky manual switch must start off. |
| Follow private browsing | `autoIncognito` | toggle | On | keep | anyone using a Chrome incognito tab or password-manager notes | ok |  | KDoc: reads IME_FLAG_NO_PERSONALIZED_LEARNING, per-field only, never touches the manual switch. |
| Back up with Android | `cloudBackup` | toggle | Off | keep | user replacing a phone who never exports a file | split |  | Off protects learned words and API keys from Google; but a user who never finds it loses everything on a new phone. |
| Clear learned words | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user wiping typing history before handing the phone over | - |  | Irreversible, deletes 4 files with no confirmation dialog. Should confirm; every other destructive action here does. |
| Pause learning | `incognitoPausesLearning` | toggle | On | demote | nobody obvious — off makes incognito nearly a no-op | ok |  | Also gates typing-stat counting. Off leaves incognito doing only chip-blocking and a badge. |
| Pause clipboard capture | `incognitoPausesClipboard` | toggle | On | demote | user who wants clip history kept even in incognito | ok |  | Same shape as Pause learning: a switch that subtracts from the feature it sits under. |
| Follow private browsing (duplicate) | `autoIncognito` | toggle | On | merge | nobody — same field drawn twice | ok |  | Identical field to the Privacy row; two rows for one boolean invites the did-I-set-both question. |
| Where backups go | `autoBackup.destination` | choice | Folder | keep | self-hoster with Nextcloud, MinIO or a NAS | ok |  | KDoc: SAF folder needs no account and works with no Play services; Drive/Dropbox/OneDrive hidden when no client id. |
| Backup folder | `autoBackup.folderUri` | nav | none | keep | anyone using the default destination | - |  | Picking the folder is what takes the persistable grant; the enable switch is inert without it. |
| WebDAV address | `autoBackup.webDavUrl` | text | empty | keep | Nextcloud or ownCloud user | - |  | Screen warns when the URL is not https, since Basic auth would go in the clear. |
| WebDAV user | `autoBackup.webDavUser` | text | empty | keep | Nextcloud or ownCloud user | - |  | Half of destinationConfigured for WEBDAV. |
| WebDAV password | `autoBackup.webDavPassword` | text | empty | keep | Nextcloud or ownCloud user | - |  | In SECRET_KEYS: stays out of exports and out of device-protected storage. |
| S3 endpoint | `autoBackup.s3.endpoint` | text | empty | keep | R2, B2, MinIO or Wasabi user | ok |  | Empty means AWS derived from region, the right no-typing default. |
| S3 bucket | `autoBackup.s3.bucket` | text | empty | keep | S3 user | - |  | Required by destinationConfigured. |
| S3 region | `autoBackup.s3.region` | text | us-east-1 | keep | S3 user | ok |  | KDoc: us-east-1 is what R2 and most MinIO setups expect for signing. |
| S3 prefix | `autoBackup.s3.prefix` | text | empty | keep | user sharing one bucket across apps | ok |  | Optional folder inside the bucket; empty root is the right default. |
| S3 access key id | `autoBackup.s3.accessKeyId` | text | empty | keep | S3 user | - |  | Required by destinationConfigured. |
| S3 secret key | `autoBackup.s3.secretAccessKey` | text | empty | keep | S3 user | - |  | Masked password field, in SECRET_KEYS. |
| Path-style addressing | `autoBackup.s3.pathStyle` | toggle | Off | keep | MinIO user, or anyone pointing at a bare IP | ok |  | KDoc: virtual-hosted is what AWS prefers and R2 requires; path style is a MinIO or IP necessity. |
| FTP host | `autoBackup.ftp.host` | text | empty | keep | home NAS owner with FTP-only sharing | - |  | Required by destinationConfigured. |
| FTP port | `autoBackup.ftp.port` | text | 21 | keep | NAS owner on a non-standard port | ok |  | 21 is the protocol default; a non-numeric entry is silently ignored rather than saved. |
| FTP user | `autoBackup.ftp.user` | text | empty | keep | NAS owner | - |  | Required by destinationConfigured. |
| FTP password | `autoBackup.ftp.password` | text | empty | keep | NAS owner | - |  | Masked field, in SECRET_KEYS. |
| FTP folder | `autoBackup.ftp.path` | text | empty | keep | NAS owner keeping backups in one directory | ok |  | Relative to wherever the login lands; empty is the sane default. |
| Use TLS | `autoBackup.ftp.secure` | toggle | On | keep | owner of an old NAS that offers plain FTP only | ok |  | KDoc: plain FTP sends the password as text, which the app refuses for WebDAV; allowed only because some boxes offer nothing else. |
| Google Drive authorization | `-` | action | Not authorized | keep | user with no Drive app installed who still wants Drive | - |  | App-folder scope only; the whole row is hidden on builds with no Play services. |
| Dropbox sign in / out | `autoBackup.dropboxRefreshToken` | action | Signed out | keep | Dropbox user | - |  | Row exists only when an app key was compiled in; refresh token not access token, so a daily job still works. |
| OneDrive sign in / out | `autoBackup.oneDriveRefreshToken` | action | Signed out | keep | OneDrive user | - |  | Same shape as Dropbox; hidden when no client id is compiled in. |
| Automatic backup | `autoBackup.enabled` | toggle | Off | keep | anyone who would never remember to export by hand | ok |  | KDoc: no default destination exists, because every candidate is somewhere the user did not ask to put their keyboard. |
| How often | `autoBackup.intervalHours` | choice | 24 h | keep | heavy customiser wanting six-hourly copies | ok |  | Daily matches the charging-only job constraint; more often would mostly no-op. |
| How many to keep | `autoBackup.keep` | choice | 5 | keep | user with a small cloud quota | ok |  | Five daily generations is a week of undo; the newest is never rotated out. |
| Encrypt the backup | `autoBackup.encrypt` | toggle | Off | keep | user backing dictionary or clipboard up to a shared cloud folder | ok |  | An encrypted file whose passphrase is lost is unrestorable; default sections exclude the personal ones, so off is safe. |
| Passphrase | `autoBackup.passphrase` | text | empty | keep | user encrypting to a synced folder | - |  | KDoc: stored in the clear because an unattended run has nobody to type it; protects the file where it lands, not the device. |
| Back up now | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user verifying a destination actually works | - |  | Only way to find out a WebDAV password is wrong without waiting a day. |
| Include: Settings | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | On | keep | anyone restoring a configured keyboard | ok |  | The point of a backup. No reset arrow: this toggle passes no default even though DEFAULT_SECTIONS exists. |
| Include API keys | `autoBackup.includeSecrets` | toggle | Off | keep | user moving phones who set up five provider keys | ok |  | KDoc: a bundle with this on is a file that has to be treated like a password. |
| Include: Themes | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | On | keep | user who built custom themes | ok |  | Configuration, not personal data; background images travel embedded. |
| Include: Dictionary | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | Off | keep | user who spent months teaching the keyboard names | ok |  | Personal typing history; enabling it triggers the unencrypted-personal-data warning on this screen. |
| Include: Clipboard | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | Off | keep | user with pinned clips they rely on | ok |  | Copied text is the most sensitive thing here; off, and warned about when turned on. |
| Include: Snippets | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | On | keep | anyone who wrote snippets | ok |  | Hand-authored configuration with nowhere to re-download it from. |
| Include: Sticker packs | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | Off | keep | user with hand-made sticker packs | ok |  | Off for size, not privacy; the runner drops it anyway past the 16 MB embed ceiling. |
| Include: Icon packs | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | Off | keep | user with custom icon packs | ok |  | Same size reasoning as stickers. |
| Include: Custom word lists | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | On | keep | user who imported a jargon list | ok |  | Imported by hand, so nowhere to fetch it back from. |
| Include: Addon repositories | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | On | keep | user with third-party addon sources added | ok |  | Addresses only, a few bytes; merges rather than replaces on import. |
| Include: Emoji history | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | On | keep | anyone whose emoji Recents tab is tuned | ok |  | Personal but tiny and undownloadable — the documented reason it defaults on unlike dictionary. |
| Include: Typing statistics | `autoBackup.sections` | toggle | On | keep | user who cares about their all-time WPM | ok |  | Counts only, tiny, unrecoverable if lost. |
| Export backup | `-` | action | n/a | keep | anyone moving to a new phone | - |  | Datestamped filename in Locale.US so repeated exports sort correctly on every locale. |
| Import backup | `-` | action | n/a | keep | anyone restoring | - |  | Reads and decodes before writing; the dialog lists section counts and warns on embedded API keys. |
| Microphone | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | user pre-granting before a meeting | ok |  | Goes through rememberDisclosedPermissionRequest, the same disclosure as the voice panel's own path. |
| Camera | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | camera and OCR tool user | ok |  | Granted state routes to app details, the only place a grant can be taken back. |
| Contacts | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | user whose contact names keep getting autocorrected | ok |  | Same disclosure route as the contact-suggestions setting. |
| Calendar | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | calendar tool user | ok |  | Read-only, only while the tool is open. |
| Photos | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | user wanting screenshots to appear in the clipboard | ok |  | Android has no screenshots-only permission, so this opens the whole library; worth surfacing here. |
| Storage | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | user on Android 9 or older saving to the gallery | ok |  | Correctly hidden from API 29 up, where the row would be a switch wired to nothing. |
| Notification access | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | media-controls and OTP-chip user | ok |  | Special access with an alarming system warning; the subtitle is where the narrow use is explained. |
| Usage access | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | user who wants a copied-from label on clips | ok |  | Foreground-app name only; must be granted on a system screen. |
| Accessibility service | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | TalkBack user who wants keyboard gestures | ok |  | Subscribes to no events; only marks the key grid as bypassing TalkBack touch handling. |
| Internet | `-` | list | Granted at install | demote | privacy auditor checking the manifest without reading it | - |  | No control at all — pure inventory. Three such rows is a third of the screen for zero action. |
| Network state | `-` | list | Granted at install | demote | privacy auditor | - |  | Only used for the metered-connection check before large downloads; not actionable. |
| Vibrate | `-` | list | Granted at install | demote | nobody, realistically | - |  | Haptics have their own settings; listing the permission adds nothing a user can act on. |
| Free up N | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user low on space | ok |  | Hidden below 512 KB — the press costs more than the space it wins. Only touches caches. |
| Refresh | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user who just deleted something elsewhere | - |  | Screen paints from a cached snapshot, so a manual re-measure has to exist. |
| Show all / Show less | `-` | action | Show less | keep | user hunting a category that currently reads 0 B | ok |  | Hiding empty rows by default keeps a 25-category screen scannable. |
| Delete <category> | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user reclaiming a 500 MB AI model | - |  | Confirmation wording keyed to Danger: free, re-downloadable, or gone for good. |
| Reset settings data | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user with a broken config who would otherwise reinstall | - |  | The only in-app factory reset. Two dialogs, deliberately: every theme, layout and mode goes with it. |
| Count typing statistics | `typingStatsEnabled` | toggle | On | keep | user who dislikes being counted at all | ok |  | Aggregate counts, on-device, paused by incognito and power saving. Off by default would leave the screen permanently empty. |
| Day / Week / Month | `-` | choice | Day | keep | user looking at a long-run trend | ok |  | View state only (rememberSaveable), not persisted. Day is the natural first view. |
| Words / Chars / Speed | `-` | choice | Words | keep | user tracking WPM over time | ok |  | View state only. Switches the chart between bars and a speed line. |
| Delete statistics | `-` | action | n/a | keep | user resetting after lending the phone out | - |  | Confirms, then bumps statsVersion so the running IME drops its in-memory copy instead of resaving. |

**Missing here:** Back up on Wi-Fi only (Job sets only setRequiresCharging; a daily upload to S3, WebDAV or Drive burns mobile data with no way to stop it.); Back up only while charging (Hard-coded true. A user who rarely charges overnight silently never gets a backup.); Confirm before clearing learned words (One mistap deletes four learning files irreversibly; every other destructive action in this domain confirms.); Incognito pauses emoji history (emojiUsage.record runs unconditionally, so incognito still fills the Recent tab — contradicts what incognito promises.); Custom backup interval and retention (Fixed lists (6/12/24/168 h, keep 3/5/10); a weekly-quota cloud user cannot pick 2 or 30.); Reset the include-in-export set (All 12 stored toggles pass no default to ToggleSetting, so none gets the reset arrow every other row in the app has.)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/privacy/permissions.mdx` — wrong: Line 62: "There's no permissions screen in the app, by design." A Permissions screen exists (PermissionsScreen.kt), reached from Privacy > Permissions (MainActivity.kt:12021).
