- `docs/src/content/docs/privacy/data.mdx` — wrong: Lines 69 and 71: "Settings have no in-app reset everything to defaults option" and "There is also no in-app factory reset." Both false — About > Storage > Settings data runs a double-confirmed clearAllPreferences() (StorageCategories.kt:748).
- `docs/src/content/docs/privacy/backup.mdx` — wrong: Lines 205-208: "none of them are persisted preferences; every switch resets to its default each time you reopen the screen." The include switches live in autoBackup.sections, and line 52 of the same page says the opposite.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/backup.mdx` — gap: The entire Automatic backup group is absent: destination choice, all seven sinks and their fields, enable, interval, keep, encrypt, passphrase, Back up now, and the last-run / last-error line.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/privacy.mdx` — stale: Line 11 says the screen is three groups, nothing else; there are four — the Permissions nav row sits above On-device learning.
- `docs/src/content/docs/privacy/overview.mdx` — stale: Lines 27-29 and 84: claims the in-app caption says the keyboard works offline apart from one optional weather tool, and that Privacy has three groups. privacy_on_device_info now names the full tool list, and there are four groups.
- `docs/src/content/docs/privacy/network.mdx` — stale: Lines 214-218 repeat the stale "the in-app Privacy screen says ... apart from the weather tool" claim.
- `docs/src/content/docs/privacy/data.mdx` — stale: Line 93 repeats the same stale caption claim (offline apart from the optional weather tool).
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/privacy.mdx` — stale: Line 57: "deletes three files." It deletes four — learning/correction_stats.json (autocorrect revert memory) was added. Same undercount at data.mdx line 57.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/backup.mdx` — stale: Line 25 calls the include group eleven independent toggles; the code draws twelve (11 sections plus Include API keys), and the table below already lists twelve.
- `MISSING: Statistics screen (About > Statistics)` — gap: No docs page anywhere. typingStatsEnabled (default on), what is counted, the incognito and power-saving pause, and the delete action are all undocumented.
- `MISSING: Permissions screen reference` — gap: No reference page for Privacy > Permissions — its three groups, the granted / not granted / install labels, and the fact that granted runtime rows route to system app details.
- `docs/src/content/docs/privacy/backup.mdx` — gap: The Automatic backup section says it runs while your device charges but never says it will upload over mobile data — the job declares no network constraint.

_Coverage: Read in full: MainActivity.kt 6320-7620 (backup helpers, StoredTextField, DestinationRow, WebDavRows, S3Rows, FtpRows, OAuthRow, DriveRow, AutoBackupGroup, BackupSettings, both import dialogs) and 11998-12103 (PrivacySettings — the assigned range 11622-11715 is stale; PrivacySettings actually begins at line 12009). Read in full: PermissionsScreen.kt (238 lines), storage/StorageScreen.kt (454), statistics/StatisticsScreen.kt (454). Read KDoc for learnFromTyping, addWordsToSystemDictionary, expandUserDictShortcuts, incognito, incognitoPausesClipboard, incognitoPausesLearning, autoIncognito, cloudBackup, typingStatsEnabled, statsVersion, BackupDestination, S3Config, FtpConfig, AutoBackupSettings (including DEFAULT_SECTIONS and destinationConfigured). Read LockedSettings.kt, DirectBootSettings.kt, CloudBackup.kt and AutoBackupScheduler.kt in full; AutoBackupRunner.kt only for its constants and Outcome type. Read all six docs/privacy/*.mdx plus reference/settings/privacy.mdx and backup.mdx in full, and the Storage section of reference/settings/about.mdx. Also covered Tools > Incognito (MainActivity.kt:9591-9619) because incognito fields were named in the domain, although that screen belongs to the tools agent. NOT itemised: the roughly 25 individual StorageCategory rows in StorageCategories.kt are collapsed into two entries (Delete <category> and Reset settings data) rather than listed one by one; storage/StorageCategoryScreen.kt was scanned for controls (per-item delete, Clear all, Manage) rather than read line by line; privacy/network.mdx was read only around its Options and edge-case sections, the rest being the tools agent's network table. DirectBootSettings.kt contains no user-visible settings — it is a derived view applied on read — so it produced no entries._

### Accessibility, hardware keyboard, power, addons, about

| Setting | Field | Type | Default | Needed? | Who benefits | Default ok? | Ask at setup | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Colour vision | `colorVisionFilter` | choice | Off | keep | colour-blind users on coloured themes | ok |  | Daltonisation over the resolved palette; correction not simulation. Off is right for the large majority. |
| High contrast keys | `highContrastKeys` | toggle | Off | keep | low-vision users, bright sunlight | ok |  | Flattens the theme to black/white; destroys every theme choice, so it must be opt-in. |
| Key outlines | `keyOutlines` | toggle | Off | keep | users on translucent or photo-background themes | ok |  | Only helps when key fill and board sit close in brightness; a theme with its own border keeps it. |
| Bold key labels | `boldKeyLabels` | toggle | Off | keep | low-vision users | ok |  | One-line render change, cheap, no side effects. Default off keeps the theme's own weight. |
| Readable font | `keyFontId (derived)` | toggle | Off (mirrors keyFontId) | keep | dyslexic and low-vision readers | - |  | Derived toggle over keyFontId = Atkinson Hyperlegible; no stored default of its own. One tap beats the font browser. |
| Text size | `fontScale` | slider | 100% (70–150%) | keep | low-vision users | ok |  | Scales key glyphs only. Ceiling of 150% is hard-coded and may be too low for severe low vision. |
| Keyboard font | `keyFontId` | nav | System default | demote | typography tinkerers | - |  | Same font browser as Appearance → Fonts. Second door is fine but it sits directly under the Readable-font shortcut. |
| Reduce motion | `reduceMotion` | toggle | Off | keep | vestibular sensitivity | ok |  | KDoc: only motion goes, meaningful feedback stays. Should seed from the OS animation scale; nothing reads it today. |
| TalkBack support | `screenReaderMode` | choice | Labels | keep | screen-reader and switch-access users | WRONG → Explore |  | Its own KDoc says Explore "is what TalkBack users expect"; Explore is inert unless touch exploration runs (KeyboardScreen.kt:10058). |
| Keyboard gestures service | `-` | nav | Not granted | keep | TalkBack users who want glide and swipe | - |  | Only shown when mode is Gestures. Carries the prominent disclosure Play requires before the a11y-service screen. |
| Ignore repeated presses | `keyDebounceMs` | slider | 0 ms (off) | keep | hand tremor, spasticity | ok |  | KDoc: drops the bouncing second contact. Off is right — set too high it eats deliberate double letters. |
| Long-press delay | `longPressDelayMs` | slider | 300 ms (150–800) | keep | motor-impaired users, accidental popup sufferers | ok |  | 300 ms is under Android's own 400–500 ms, matching a keyboard's faster cadence. Range covers both complaints. |
| Key size & spacing | `-` | nav | — | keep | users hunting key size on the wrong screen | - |  | Pure cross-link to Layout & size. Docs call this the commonest mix-up, so the row earns itself. |
| Haptics & sound | `-` | nav | — | keep | users looking for feedback under Accessibility | - |  | Cross-link to Key press. Same argument as the key-size row. |
| Process hardware keys | `hardwareKeyboardInput` | toggle | On | keep | hardware typists wanting autocorrect and suggestions | ok |  | Governs typed characters only; the leader, Esc and panel nav work either way. On gives the keyboard's value on a physical board. |
| Tool shortcuts | `hardwareKeyboard.shortcutsEnabled` | toggle | On | keep | hardware-keyboard users | ok |  | KDoc: the default leader is a double-tapped modifier that produces no character and passes through anyway, so on costs nothing. |
| Tool shortcuts list | `hardwareKeyboard.leader + toolByLetter` | nav | Double-tap Ctrl | keep | hardware power users | - |  | Opens the letter table. Shown only while Tool shortcuts is on, which is the right gate. |
| Ctrl + number opens a toolbar tool | `hardwareKeyboard.toolbarDigitChord` | toggle | On | keep | hardware users reaching pinned tools fast | split |  | Its own KDoc admits browsers use Ctrl+1..9 for tabs and the keyboard wins while a field has focus. Genuine coin flip. |
| Write the modifier names | `hardwareKeyboard.hintModifierWords` | toggle | On | demote | non-Mac users reading badges | ok |  | Cosmetic badge wording. KDoc justifies on (⌃/⇧ glyphs are a Mac convention) but this belongs on the shortcuts sub-screen. |
| Time to press a key | `hardwareKeyboard.pickerTimeoutMs` | slider | 8.0 s (1–30) | demote | slow readers of the badge row | ok |  | KDoc: three seconds was enough to act, nowhere near enough to read a bar of new badges. Belongs beside the leader. |
| Arrow keys move a highlight | `hardwareKeyboard.panelNavigation` | toggle | On | demote | hardware users driving open panels | ok |  | KDoc: without it a shortcut can open a tool but not use one. Off is nearly self-defeating; demote out of the main list. |
| Esc closes the tool | `hardwareKeyboard.escClosesPanel` | toggle | On | demote | nobody concrete — only fires while a panel is open | ok |  | Only consumed when the keyboard actually has something open, so the app conflict it guards against barely exists. |
| Number keys pick suggestions | `hardwareKeyboard.suggestionHotkeys` | choice | Alt + number | keep | hardware typists committing suggestions | ok |  | Alt+digit avoids both bare digits and the Ctrl+digit tab conflict. Three modes is the right amount of choice. |
| Show the suggestion numbers | `hardwareKeyboard.suggestionHintsAlways` | toggle | On | demote | hardware users who find the digits noisy | ok |  | KDoc: the strip is the one thing a hardware user looks at constantly, so its keys are worth the ink. |
| macOS shortcuts | `hardwareKeyboard.macShortcuts` | toggle | Off | keep | Mac-layout Bluetooth keyboard owners | ok |  | KDoc: on a PC keyboard Meta is Search/Windows and belongs to the system. Off is correct; could be auto-detected per device. |
| Ctrl+Space changes the language | `hardwareKeyboard.languageSwitchChord` | toggle | On | keep | multilingual hardware typists; off for code-editor users | ok |  | KDoc: established convention on ChromeOS and Windows; the cost is narrow (editor completions) and named in ReservedChords. |
| Show the keyboard for shortcuts | `hardwareKeyboard.autoShowUi` | toggle | On | demote | hardware users opening tools that need somewhere to draw | ok |  | A tool with no surface is a no-op, and the IME is restored on close, so off is close to a broken state. |
| Shortcut key | `hardwareKeyboard.leader` | choice | Double-tap Ctrl | keep | users whose Ctrl double-tap collides with an app | ok |  | Dialog leads with the three double-tap modifiers because most people editing it hold a phone with no keyboard attached. |
| Tool letter (one row per supported tool) | `hardwareKeyboard.toolByLetter` | list | tool initials where free | keep | hardware power users | ok |  | Complete map, not a delta, so unbinding is expressible. Rows for disabled tools are labelled rather than hidden. |
| Reset to defaults (letters + leader) | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone who scrambled their bindings | - |  | Confirm dialog then setHwToolLetters(DefaultToolLetters) + setHwLeader. The only bulk reset in this domain. |
| Power saving now | `powerSaving.manual` | toggle | Off | keep | anyone at 5% battery on a train | ok |  | Live state rather than a preference, but persisted deliberately like incognito so it survives field changes. |
| Switch on by itself | `powerSaving.trigger` | choice | Android's battery saver is on | keep | users who never open keyboard settings | ok |  | KDoc: it only fires because the user already asked the system to save, so it answers something they did. |
| Low battery is | `powerSaving.batteryPercent` | slider | 20% (5–50) | keep | users on the low-battery trigger | ok |  | Shown only for the two battery triggers. 20% matches Android's own low-battery warning point. |
| Off while charging | `powerSaving.offWhileCharging` | toggle | On | keep | users who charge at a desk | ok |  | Nothing to save with power flowing in; the manual switch is deliberately unaffected. Hidden when the trigger is Off. |
| Key vibration | `powerSaving.dropHaptics` | toggle | On | keep | everyone saving battery | ok |  | KDoc: the single biggest per-keystroke power draw. Highest saving-to-annoyance ratio of the twelve. |
| Key sounds | `powerSaving.dropKeySound` | toggle | On | keep | everyone saving battery | ok |  | Cheap to give up and mostly off already, so the drop is rarely even noticed. |
| Animations | `powerSaving.dropAnimations` | toggle | On | keep | everyone saving battery | ok |  | Reuses reduceMotion rather than adding a second motion flag, so nothing is drawn between frames. |
| Glide trail | `powerSaving.dropGlideTrail` | toggle | On | keep | glide typists who want the decoder but not the overlay | ok |  | Trail opacity goes to zero so the gesture keeps working — the cheap half of dropping gesture typing. |
| Key popup | `powerSaving.dropKeyPopup` | toggle | Off | demote | barely anyone — the bubble is cheap to draw | ok |  | KDoc itself calls it cheap. It is a switch whose whole justification is that it saves little. |
| Gesture typing | `powerSaving.dropGestureTyping` | toggle | Off | keep | users squeezing the last percent who can type by tapping | ok |  | KDoc: the most expensive thing the keyboard does, but also why many installed it — hence off by default. |
| Emoji suggestions | `powerSaving.dropEmojiPrediction` | toggle | On | keep | everyone saving battery | ok |  | Per-word scan for emoji, given up with almost no felt loss. |
| Smart chips | `powerSaving.dropSmartChips` | toggle | On | keep | everyone saving battery | ok |  | Sums, unit and currency matching run against every word typed; dropping them is invisible unless you use them. |
| Background network | `powerSaving.dropBackgroundNetwork` | toggle | On | keep | everyone saving battery and data | ok |  | Unrequested fetches only — link previews, QR previews, dictionary look-up-on-selection. Hand-opened tools still work. |
| Screenshot watching | `powerSaving.dropScreenshotWatch` | toggle | On | keep | everyone saving battery | ok |  | A folder observer running while the keyboard is closed; giving it up costs one clipboard convenience. |
| On-device models | `powerSaving.dropOnDeviceModels` | toggle | On | keep | Whisper and handwriting users on a dying battery | ok |  | Falls back to the system recognizer and to gesture typing rather than breaking; heavy CPU inference either way. |
| Typing statistics | `powerSaving.dropTypingStats` | toggle | Off | REMOVE | nobody measurable | ok |  | Its own KDoc: "the saving is unmeasured and speculative". A few integer bumps per keystroke does not deserve a switch. |
| Share Play Store link | `-` | action | — | keep | users recommending the app | - |  | Opens the share sheet, not the store. Hidden on F-Droid builds, which is the right gate. |
| Share F-Droid link | `-` | action | — | keep | users recommending the app to FOSS friends | - |  | Hidden on Play builds. Same mechanism as the row above. |
| Share GitHub link | `-` | action | — | merge | almost nobody — a third near-identical share row | - |  | On a non-Play build this is the third row that opens the same share sheet. One row with a source picker would do. |
| Version | `-` | nav | — | keep | anyone filing a bug; egg hunters | - |  | Read-only fact plus the seven-tap keycap-catcher egg. Draws a chevron it does not honour for the first six taps. |
| Licence | `-` | nav | MIT | keep | legal/compliance readers | - |  | Opens the app's own MIT text. Required for redistribution. |
| Source code | `-` | nav | — | keep | contributors and sceptics | - |  | Opens the repository directly rather than sharing it, unlike the rows above. |
| Storage | `-` | nav | — | keep | users freeing space after model downloads | - |  | Sub-screen owned by another domain; the entry point belongs here since About is where people look for disk use. |
| Statistics | `-` | nav | — | keep | users curious about their typing speed | - |  | Sub-screen owned elsewhere. Sits oddly under "App" next to Version and Licence. |
| Diagnostics | `-` | nav | — | keep | bug reporters and the maintainer | - |  | Log viewer plus shareable report; never contains typed text. Directly supports the Feedback group below. |
| Run setup again | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone who skipped or mis-answered the wizard | - |  | The wizard's only way back in. A replay presets and seeds nothing, so the row's promise holds. |
| Check for updates / Install the update | `-` | nav | — | keep | sideloaders and impatient users | - |  | Row's action follows its state so it never lies about what pressing it does. Whole group hidden when unsupported. |
| Update prompts | `UpdatePrefs.autoPrompt (SharedPreferences)` | toggle | On | keep | users who resent Play dialogs appearing unasked | ok |  | KDoc: an update nobody hears about is the failure this feature fixes. Off keeps the card and row. No reset control — no default passed. |
| Report a bug | `-` | nav | — | keep | bug reporters | - |  | Public, searchable, trackable — correctly listed before the email row. |
| Email the developer | `-` | nav | — | keep | users with something private to report | - |  | mailto: with a pre-filled body; falls back to a toast naming the address when no mail app exists. |
| User guide | `-` | nav | — | keep | anyone lost in 500 settings | - |  | The docs site is the only place most of this app is explained. |
| Privacy policy | `-` | nav | — | keep | privacy-minded users; Play policy | - |  | Store listings require a reachable policy; this is the in-app path to it. |
| Open-source licences | `-` | nav | — | keep | legal compliance | - |  | Apache-2.0 §4(a) and the MIT/BSD/OFL notices require the text to travel with the binary. Not optional. |
| Dictionaries (Word lists group) | `-` | nav | — | REMOVE | nobody | - |  | AboutScreens.kt:642 — the onClick is an empty lambda, yet NavRow draws a chevron promising navigation. Make it a caption or delete it. |
| Allow plugins | `PluginStore.subsystemEnabled (own store)` | toggle | Off | keep | users who want third-party panels | ok |  | Whole subsystem off until asked for; nothing installs or runs before it. Correct posture for code execution. |
| Install from a file | `-` | action | — | keep | plugin authors and sideloaders | - |  | Shows the same capability disclosure as the store path; no route skips it. |
| Get more plugins (addon store row) | `-` | nav | — | keep | users who don't know the store exists | - |  | Opens the store pre-filtered to Plugin, which is the only useful shape of that link from here. |
| Enabled (per plugin) | `plugin.enabled` | toggle | On | keep | users silencing one misbehaving plugin | ok |  | Independent of the subsystem switch; auto-flips off after two abandons, and the subtitle says so. |
| Clear stored data | `-` | action | — | keep | users whose plugin filled its 64 KB | - |  | The quota is fixed at 64 KB, so a manual purge is the only recovery short of uninstalling. |
| Clear log | `-` | action | — | demote | plugin authors debugging | - |  | Whole Log group only appears once something was printed, which already keeps it out of everyone else's way. |
| Uninstall plugin | `-` | action | — | keep | anyone removing a plugin | - |  | Confirms, and names what is deleted (script plus stored data). |
| Add repository | `-` | action | — | keep | users following an addon link | - |  | Live-resolves the manifest URL before Add enables; a deep link pre-fills but still requires the tap. |
| Refresh (all repositories) | `-` | action | — | demote | users waiting on a just-published addon | - |  | Every visit already refreshes every repository in the background (AddonScreens.kt:443), so the button is mostly redundant. |
| Repository menu: Refresh / Homepage / Remove | `-` | action | — | keep | users curating their repository list | - |  | Homepage only shown for an https homepage in the manifest. Remove is the only way out of the list. |
| Catalogue search | `-` | text | empty | keep | users in a large catalogue | - |  | Matches name, description, author and tags; rememberSaveable so returning from an addon keeps the filter. |
| Catalogue type filter chips | `-` | choice | All (or the type you arrived with) | keep | users who arrived asking for one type | ok |  | Only types the manifest actually contains get a chip, so the row never offers an empty filter. |
| Install / Update / Uninstall / Preview / Use | `-` | action | — | keep | everyone installing an addon | - |  | Install and apply are deliberately two decisions; the apply prompt is asked once after the install lands. |
| How many languages do you type in? | `onboarding.personaLanguages` | choice | Unset (treated as one) | keep | everyone — drives spacebar swipes and the globe key | ok | yes | Genuinely ~50/50, one tap, and it decides whether the globe key is dead. Already asked; do not ask again elsewhere. |
| How much keyboard do you want? | `onboarding.personaDepth` | choice | Unset (treated as balanced) | keep | everyone — picks the starter tool set and the wizard length | ok | yes | Highest-leverage question in the app: replaces the enable-all-70-tools default and gates four wizard pages. |
| How private should it be? | `onboarding.personaPrivacy` | choice | Unset (treated as standard) | keep | privacy-minded users | ok | yes | Strict turns off learn-from-typing, clipboard history and stats, and adds incognito — three settings people rarely find. |

**Missing here:** Follow the system's "Remove animations" setting (Nothing reads ANIMATOR_DURATION_SCALE; a user who disabled animations OS-wide still gets keyboard motion.); Key label text size above 150% (Ceiling is hard-coded; severe low vision needs more than 150%, and key height is on another screen.); Refresh addon repositories automatically / on Wi-Fi only (Every visit fetches every repository manifest with no gate; metered-data users cannot turn it off.); Re-answer the persona quiz without replaying the wizard (personaDepth/Languages/Privacy still shape the tool set and theme gallery, but are invisible and unchangeable in settings.); Stop auto-disabling a plugin after failures (Two timeouts on a slow phone permanently disable a working plugin; the author cannot opt out.)

**Docs:**
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/about.mdx` — wrong: "The App group holds four rows" — the code draws seven: Version, Licence, Source code, Storage, Statistics, Diagnostics, Run setup again. Statistics and Run setup again are documented nowhere on the page.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/about.mdx` — gap: The Updates group is undocumented: the state-dependent Check-for-updates / Install row and the "Update prompts" toggle (default On, SharedPreferences-backed) appear nowhere.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/about.mdx` — wrong: "A full build lists 19 bundled components; a lite build lists 12." AboutScreens.kt builds 21 on a full Play build and 13 on lite F-Droid — "Inter & Manrope" and "Play In-App Updates" are missing from the doc.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/about.mdx` — stale: "This row doesn't navigate anywhere; it's read-only" about Version — seven taps opens the keycap-catcher easter egg (AboutScreens.kt:510-528).
- `docs/src/content/docs/typing/power-saving.mdx` — wrong: "eleven independent switches" — PowerSavingSettings has twelve drop flags; "Typing statistics" (dropTypingStats, default Off) is absent from both the table and the Options list.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/settings/addons.mdx` — wrong: "both editions install and run the same eleven addon types" and the type table lists eleven; AddonType has twelve installable entries — SoundPack is missing.
- `docs/src/content/docs/reference/shortcuts.mdx` — gap: The Options table omits "Ctrl+Space changes the language" (languageSwitchChord, default On), which typing/hardware-keyboard.mdx does document — the two tables disagree.
- `MISSING: onboarding / the setup wizard` — gap: No page documents the persona quiz, which pages it adds or removes, or the settings each answer writes (spacebar swipes, globe key, tool set, learn-from-typing, clipboard history, stats). Only the About "Run setup again" row hints it exists.

**Reset controls already present:** `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/SettingsUi.kt:673 — ResetSetting: per-row restore icon, drawn only while the value differs from the shipped default`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:2049 — ToggleSetting's `default` parameter is what grows that per-row reset control`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/AccessibilityScreens.kt:57,66,75,84,93,108,128,149,200,211 — every Accessibility row passes SettingsDefaults, so all ten have a reset`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:3325,3335,3361,3370,3384,3394,3403,3413,3423,3433,3442,3451 — every hardware-keyboard row passes SettingsDefaults`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:3557 — "Reset to defaults" button on the hardware shortcuts screen; confirm dialog at 3585 restores DefaultToolLetters and DefaultLeader`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/MainActivity.kt:9632-9770 — all sixteen power-saving rows pass SettingsDefaults.powerSaving`, `app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/storage/StorageScreen.kt:424 — ResetSettingsDialog: global reset of every setting, reached from About → Storage → Your data`, `NOT reset-capable: app/src/main/java/com/wasimaster/wmkeyboard/app/updates/UpdateUi.kt:195 — the "Update prompts" toggle passes no `default`, so it is the one switch in this domain with no restore control`, `NOT reset-capable: PluginScreens.kt:81 and :251 — "Allow plugins" and the per-plugin Enabled switch are raw Material Switches in WmRow, outside the ToggleSetting/reset mechanism`

_Coverage: Read in full: AccessibilityScreens.kt (all 231 lines); MainActivity.kt 3255-3455 (the whole Typing → Physical keyboard group, extending above the requested 3351 start so the group's first two rows are covered) and 3456-3690 (HardwareShortcutsSettings, LeaderCaptureDialog, start of LetterCaptureDialog); MainActivity.kt 9623-9774 (the ToolbarTool.POWER_SAVING detail screen, located by grep since no separate PowerSavingScreen file exists); core/settings/PowerSavingSettings.kt; SettingsRepository.kt KDoc for HardwareKeyboardSettings (739-821), ScreenReaderMode (560-576), the accessibility block (1017-1045), longPressDelayMs (1243), OnboardingSettings/Persona enums (2292-2346); AboutScreens.kt (all 725 lines); updates/UpdatePolicy.kt, UpdateUi.kt, UpdatePrefs.kt; PluginScreens.kt (all 458 lines); Onboarding.kt, OnboardingGraph.kt, OnboardingPersona.kt, OnboardingPages.kt (all 1227 lines). AddonScreens.kt: read lines 1-1110, 1403-1523 and 1804-1953 in full and grepped the whole 2493-line file for every settings widget — it contains zero ToggleSetting/SliderSetting/ChoiceSetting/TextFieldSetting, so its entries are actions, not stored preferences. Docs read in full: reference/settings/accessibility.mdx, reference/settings/about.mdx, reference/settings/addons.mdx, accessibility/overview.mdx, reference/shortcuts.mdx, typing/power-saving.mdx, plus the Options table and language section of typing/hardware-keyboard.mdx. NOT read: addons/installing.mdx, addons/managing.mdx, addons/overview.mdx and all five plugins/*.mdx (about 1050 lines) — I only checked their referenced constants (MAX_PLUGINS=50, MAX_ABANDONS=2, MAX_TOTAL_BYTES=64 KB, MAX_REPOS=30) against the code, all of which match; the narrative in those eight pages is unverified. Also unverified: OnboardingDiscover.kt was grepped rather than read (seven discover-card toggles: smart chips, hotwords, clipboard, OTP, fancy text, AI, quick toggles — all mirrors of settings owned by other domains) and OnboardingTry.kt was not read. Storage, Statistics and Diagnostics are listed as About nav rows only; their sub-screens are almost certainly another agent's domain and I did not audit their contents. Every onboarding control other than the three persona questions writes a setting owned by another domain (theme mode, keyboard theme, emoji font, haptics, key sound, key popup, spacebar swipes, globe key, number row, per-tool enables, calendar/weather/compass options), so I listed only the persona trio and flagged the rest here to avoid double-counting._
